In this work, we propose Sync-R1, an end-to-end reinforcement learning framework that jointly optimizes personalized understanding and generation within a single, explicit reasoning loop. Through this unified feedback process, Sync-R1 enables personalized comprehension to guide content creation, while the resulting generation quality reciprocally refines understanding within an integrated reward landscape. To efficiently orchestrate this dual-task synergy, we introduce Sync-GRPO, a reinforcement learning method utilizing an ensemble reward system. Furthermore, we propose Dynamic Group Scaling (DGS), which adaptively filters low-potential trajectories to reduce gradient variance and accelerate convergence. To better reflect real-world complexity, we introduce UnifyBench++, featuring denser textual descriptions and richer user contexts. Experimental results demonstrate that Sync-R1 achieves state-of-the-art performance, showcasing superior cross-task reasoning and robust personalization without requiring complex cold-start procedures. The code and the UnifyBench++ dataset will be released at: https://github.com/arctanxarc/UniCTokens.

</details>


### [216] [Automated high-frequency quantification of fish communities and biomass using computer vision](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10449)
*Kota Ishikawa,Takuma Masui,Keita Koeda,Rickdane Gomez,Lucas Yutaka Kimura,Michio Kondoh*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文开发了一种基于计算机视觉的自动化框架，利用立体相机系统和深度学习技术（鱼类识别、多目标跟踪、3D重建）从水下视频中量化鱼类群落结构（物种丰度与生物量），并通过与目视普查和环境DNA调查的对比，证明了该方法在连续、非侵入式、定量监测方面的优势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有鱼类群落调查方法（捕捞法、水下目视普查、环境DNA宏条形码）存在劳动强度大、高频定量观测能力有限、缺乏可靠的丰度和生物量估计等不足，亟需一种能够实现高频、连续、非侵入式定量监测的方法。

Method: 使用定制立体相机系统获取水下视频，集成深度学习鱼类识别、多目标跟踪和3D重建技术，构建自动化框架以估计物种水平的丰度和生物量。在20天内以每小时一次的频率对珊瑚礁鱼类群落进行观测，并与目视普查和环境DNA调查结果进行比较。

Result: 该方法成功揭示了鱼类群落物种丰富度、丰度和生物量的动态波动及其与物种组成变化的关系；与目视普查和环境DNA方法相比，该方法在持续观测物种的连续、非侵入式定量监测方面具有互补优势。

Conclusion: 该自动化框架为长期监测提供了可扩展的基础，提升了对鱼类群落精细时间尺度动态变化的分辨能力。

Abstract: Quantifying fish community structure is essential for understanding biodiversity and ecosystem responses in a changing environment, yet existing survey methods provide limited high-frequency, quantitative observations. Conventional approaches, including catch-based methods, underwater visual censuses, and environmental DNA metabarcoding, either require intensive labor or lack reliable estimates of abundance and biomass. Here, we develop an automated framework for quantifying fish communities from underwater video using computer vision. Using videos acquired with a custom-made stereo camera system, the framework integrates deep learning-based fish identification, multi-object tracking, and 3D reconstruction to estimate species-level abundance and biomass. We applied the approach to a reef fish community over a 20-day period with hourly daytime observations, revealing dynamic fluctuations in species richness, abundance, and biomass associated with changes in species composition. By comparing fish communities estimated from visual census and environmental DNA surveys, we demonstrate that our method provides complementary strengths for continuous, non-invasive, and quantitative monitoring of consistently observed species. This approach provides a scalable foundation for long-term monitoring and advances the capacity to resolve fine-scale temporal dynamics in fish communities.

</details>


### [217] [Automated Detection of Abnormalities in Zebrafish Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10464)
*Sarath Sivaprasad,Hui-Po Wang,Anna-Lisa Jäckel,Jonas Baumann,Carole Baumann,Jennifer Herrmann,Mario Fritz*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个大规模斑马鱼胚胎发育显微图像数据集，并首次引入基于Transformer的时空特征模型，在受精率分类（准确率98%）和毒性评估（准确率92%）两项基准任务上取得了优异结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 斑马鱼胚胎是药物发现的重要模型，但当前评估依赖人工检查，成本高且劳动密集；机器学习虽有自动化潜力，但缺乏大规模标注数据集限制了进展。

Method: 构建了大规模高分辨率显微图像序列数据集，包含对照和3,4-二氯苯胺化合物暴露条件下的胚胎发育过程，由专家在细粒度时间层级进行标注。基于此数据集，提出了首个集成时空特征的Transformer基线模型，用于早期发育异常预测。

Result: 在受精率分类任务（130,368张图像）上达到98%准确率，在毒性评估任务（55,296张图像）上达到92%准确率。

Conclusion: 自动化方法在增强基于斑马鱼的毒性分析方面具有巨大潜力。

Abstract: Zebrafish embryos are a valuable model for drug discovery due to their optical transparency and genetic similarity to humans. However, current evaluations rely on manual inspection, which is costly and labor-intensive. While machine learning offers automation potential, progress is limited by the lack of comprehensive datasets. To address this, we introduce a large-scale dataset of high-resolution microscopic image sequences capturing zebrafish embryonic development under both control conditions and exposure to compounds (3,4-dichloroaniline). This dataset, with expert annotations at fine-grained temporal levels, supports two benchmarking tasks: (1) fertility classification, assessing zebrafish egg viability (130,368 images), and (2) toxicity assessment, detecting malformations induced by toxic exposure over time (55,296 images). Alongside the dataset, we present the first transformer-based baseline model that integrates spatiotemporal features to predict developmental abnormalities at early stages. Experimental results present the model's effectiveness, achieving 98% accuracy in fertility classification and 92% in toxicity assessment. These findings underscore the potential of automated approaches to enhance zebrafish-based toxicity analysis.

</details>


### [218] [Adaptive Context Matters: Towards Provable Multi-Modality Guidance for Super-Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10470)
*Jinyi Luo,Minghao Liu,Yifan Li,Zejia Fan,Jiaying Liu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文首次对多模态超分辨率进行理论建模，揭示了现有方法在模态利用上的次优瓶颈，并提出M³ESR框架通过动态模态融合和自适应温度调度机制优化泛化风险，显著提升性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超分辨率是严重病态问题，现有语义引导和多模态方法虽利用大模型或外部先验增强语义对齐，但异质模态融合在实践和理论上仍未被充分理解。

Method: 提出M³ESR框架，包含：1）空间动态模态加权模块；2）时间自适应模态温度调度机制，实现灵活的自适应时空模态加权以控制风险。

Result: 实验表明M³ESR显著提升了泛化性能和语义一致性表现。

Conclusion: 通过理论分析揭示模态权重与有效贡献的对齐以及降低表示复杂度可改进泛化风险界，所提方法通过动态模态融合实现了准确的风险控制和模态贡献优化。

Abstract: Super-resolution (SR) is a severely ill-posed problem with inherent ambiguity, as widely recognized in both empirical and theoretical studies. Although recent semantic-guided and multi-modal SR methods exploit large models or external priors to enhance semantic alignment, the fusion of heterogeneous modalities remains insufficiently understood in practice and theory. In this work, we provide the first theoretical modeling of multi-modal SR, revealing that prior methods are bottlenecked by sub-optimal modality utilization. Our analysis shows that the generalization risk bound can be improved by strengthening the alignment between modality weights and their effective contributions, while reducing representation complexity. This theoretical insight inspires us to propose the novel Multi-Modal Mixture-of-Experts Super-Resolution framework (M$^3$ESR) that employs generalization-oriented dynamic modality fusion for accurate risk control and modality contribution optimization. In detail, we propose a novel spatially dynamic modality weighting module and a temporally adaptive modality temperature scheduling mechanism, enabling flexible and adaptive spatial-temporal modality weighting for effective risk control. Extensive experiments demonstrate that our M$^3$ESR significantly boosts generalization and semantic consistency performances, which confirms our superiority.

</details>


### [219] [OpenSGA: Efficient 3D Scene Graph Alignment in the Open World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10484)
*Gang Chen,Sebastián Barbas Laina,Stefan Leutenegger,Javier Alonso-Mora*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一个统一的场景图对齐框架OpenSGA，通过融合视觉-语言、文本和几何特征，并引入距离门控空间注意力编码器、最小成本流分配器和全局场景嵌入生成器，实现了帧到扫描和子扫描到子扫描两种对齐任务。同时发布了大规模数据集ScanNet-SG，包含超过70万样本和500+物体类别，实验表明该方法在两项任务上均显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有场景图对齐方法主要聚焦于子扫描到子扫描对齐，严重依赖几何点云特征，而帧到扫描对齐和开放集视觉-语言特征尚未被充分探索；同时现有数据集规模小、物体类别有限，制约了系统的训练和评估。

Method: 提出统一的场景图对齐框架，融合视觉-语言、文本和几何特征并加入空间上下文；设计了距离门控空间注意力编码器、基于最小成本流的分配器和全局场景嵌入生成器；通过自动化标注流程生成了包含70万+样本、509个ScanNet类别和3000+GPT-4o标签类别的大规模数据集ScanNet-SG。

Result: 在帧到扫描和子扫描到子扫描两项任务上均取得了最佳综合性能，显著超越了现有场景图对齐方法。

Conclusion: 所提出的统一框架和大规模数据集有效推动了场景图对齐领域的发展，尤其填补了帧到扫描对齐和开放集视觉-语言特征应用的空白。

Abstract: Scene graph alignment establishes object correspondences between two 3D scene graphs constructed from partially overlapping observations. This enables efficient scene understanding and object-level relocalization when a robot revisits a place, as well as global map fusion across multiple agents. Such capabilities are essential for robots that require long-term memory for long-horizon tasks involving interactions with the environment. Existing approaches mainly focus on subscan-to-subscan (S2S) alignment and depend heavily on geometric point-cloud features, leaving frame-to-scan (F2S) alignment and open-set vision-language features underexplored. In addition, existing datasets for scene graph alignment remain small-scale with limited object diversity, constraining systematic training and evaluation. We present a unified and efficient scene graph alignment framework that predicts object correspondences by fusing vision-language, textual, and geometric features with spatial context. The framework comprises modules such as a distance-gated spatial attention encoder, a minimum-cost-flow-based allocator, and a global scene embedding generator to achieve accurate alignment even under large coordinate discrepancies. We further introduce ScanNet-SG, a large-scale dataset generated via an automated annotation pipeline with over 700k samples, covering 509 object categories from ScanNet labels and over 3k categories from GPT-4o-based tagging. Experiments show that our method achieves the best overall performance on both F2S and S2S tasks, substantially outperforming existing scene graph alignment methods. Our code and dataset are released at: https://autonomousrobots.nl/paper_websites/opensga.

</details>


### [220] [M$^2$E-UAV: A Benchmark and Analysis for Onboard Motion-on-Motion Event-Based Tiny UAV Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10496)
*Weiqi Yan,Lixin Chen,Xiangrui Hou,Zhipeng Cai,Youbiao Wang,Yangyang Shi,Yu Zang,Cheng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出M²E-UAV基准数据集和M²E-Point基线方法，用于解决事件相机在运动对运动条件下检测微型无人机的问题。M²E-Point将事件编码为点集，使用EdgeConv提取局部结构，通过DBSCAN生成边界框。实验表明点基事件建模是强基线，而IMU条件化带来的增益有限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 事件相机在运动对运动（observer和target同时运动）条件下检测微型无人机极具挑战性，因为自身运动会激活大量背景边缘事件（建筑、植被、地平线等），而无人机仅表现为稀疏的事件簇。现有研究缺乏针对这一实际问题的基准和方法。

Method: 提出M²E-UAV基准数据集（87,223训练样本、21,395验证样本，涵盖四种场景）；提出M²E-Point基线方法，将事件编码为[x,y,t,p]点集，用EdgeConv提取局部事件结构，预测事件级无人机前景分数，再通过DBSCAN聚类生成边界框；同时提出M²E-Point+IMU变体，引入IMU条件化以分析惯性线索的作用。

Result: M²E-Point在验证集上达到0.9673 F1和0.5501 mAP50-95；IMU条件化变体达到0.5561 mAP50-95，仅带来边际提升。

Conclusion: 点基事件建模是运动对运动条件下事件相机无人机检测的强基线，简单IMU条件化带来的聚合增益有限，该基准和基线为未来研究提供了初始参考。代码将开源。

Abstract: Tiny UAV detection from an onboard event camera is difficult when the observer and target move at the same time. In this motion-on-motion regime, ego-motion activates background edges across buildings, vegetation, and horizon structures, while the UAV may appear as a sparse event cluster. To explore this practical problem, we present M$^2$E-UAV, a benchmark and analysis setup for onboard motion-on-motion event-based tiny UAV detection. The processed M$^2$E-UAV benchmark contains 87,223 training samples and 21,395 validation samples across four scene families: sunny building-forest, sunny farm-village, sunset building-forest, and sunset farm-village. We provide M$^2$E-Point, a point-based event baseline, and M$^2$E-Point + IMU, an IMU-conditioned variant, to analyze the role of inertial cues under onboard motion-on-motion detection. M$^2$E-Point encodes events as $[x,y,t,p]$ point sets, extracts local event structure with EdgeConv, and predicts event-level UAV foreground scores, from which bounding boxes are derived via DBSCAN. Our validation-stage analysis shows that point-based event modeling is a strong baseline, while simple IMU conditioning provides only marginal aggregate gains. Under the train/validation split, M$^2$E-Point achieves 0.9673 F1 and 0.5501 mAP50-95, while the IMU-conditioned variant reaches 0.5561 mAP50-95 with only marginal aggregate changes, serving as an initial baseline for future exploration in this domain. Code will be ready in https://github.com/Wickyan/M2E-UAV.

</details>


### [221] [DuetFair: Coupling Inter- and Intra-Subgroup Robustness for Fair Medical Image Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10521)
*Yiqi Tian,Sangjoon Park,Bo Zeng,Pengfei Jin,Yujin Oh,Quanzheng Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 针对医学图像分割模型在子组内存在隐藏失败（高损失样本被均值掩盖）的问题，提出DuetFair双轴公平框架，兼顾子组间适应和子组内鲁棒性，并基于此提出FairDRO方法（结合分布感知混合专家与子组条件分布鲁棒优化），在三个医学图像分割基准上取得最优公平缩放性能和最差子组Dice提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有公平性方法专注于提升子组平均性能，隐含假设每个子组内部是同质的，但这会掩盖子组内的高损失困难样本（即组内隐藏失败问题），需要同时考虑子组间的适应性以及子组内的鲁棒性。

Method: 提出DuetFair双轴公平框架，联合考虑子组间适应（inter-subgroup adaptation）和子组内鲁棒性（intra-subgroup robustness）；并基于此设计FairDRO方法，包含两个关键组件：分布感知混合专家（distribution-aware mixture-of-experts, dMoE）和子组条件分布鲁棒优化（subgroup-conditioned DRO）损失聚合。

Result: 在Harvard-FairSeg上取得最优公平缩放性能；在HAM10000上，在年龄和种族两种分组方案下均改进了最差子组性能；在3D放疗靶区分割数据集上，肿瘤分期分组下最差子组Dice提升3.5个点（↑6.0%），机构分组下提升4.1个点（↑7.4%），均超过最强基线。

Conclusion: FairDRO通过同时处理子组间公平性和子组内困难样本，有效解决了组内隐藏失败问题，在多种医学图像分割任务和分组方案下均显著提升了公平性和最差子组性能。

Abstract: Medical image segmentation models can perform unevenly across subgroups. Most existing fairness methods focus on improving average subgroup performance, implicitly treating each subgroup as internally homogeneous. However, this can hide difficult cases within a subgroup, where high-loss samples are obscured by the subgroup mean. We call this problem \textbf{intra-group hidden failure}. To solve this, we propose \textbf{DuetFair} mechanism, a dual-axis fairness framework that jointly considers inter-subgroup adaptation and intra-subgroup robustness. Based on DuetFair, we introduce \textbf{FairDRO}, which combines distribution-aware mixture-of-experts (dMoE) with subgroup-conditioned distributionally robust optimization (DRO) loss aggregation. This design allows the model to adapt across subgroups while also reducing hidden failures within each subgroup. We evaluate FairDRO on three medical image segmentation benchmarks with varying degrees of within-group heterogeneity. FairDRO achieves the best equity-scaled performance on Harvard-FairSeg and improves worst-case subgroup performance on HAM10000 under both age- and race-based grouping schemes. On the 3D radiotherapy target cohort, FairDRO further improves worst-group Dice by 3.5 points ($\uparrow 6.0\%$) under the tumor-stage grouping and by 4.1 points ($\uparrow 7.4\%$) under the institution grouping over the strongest baseline.

</details>


### [222] [Improving Human Image Animation via Semantic Representation Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10523)
*Chang Liu,Mengting Chen,Yixuan Huang,Haoning Wu,Chen Ju,Shuai Xiao,Jinsong Lan,Yanfeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出SemanticREPA方法，通过表示对齐（结构对齐+ID对齐）利用语义表示作为监督信号，无需额外条件输入即可生成更稳定、一致的人体图像动画视频。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有图像到视频生成方法存在肢体扭曲、面部失真问题；现有基于人体语义表示（密集姿态、ID嵌入）的方法降低生成灵活性，且RGB像素监督缺乏对3D几何关系和时间一致性的强调。

Method: 1) 训练结构对齐模块，将视频潜变量中的结构表示与深度估计特征对齐；2) 冻结该模块，利用其对扩散模型的结构表示提供额外监督（结构矫正）；3) 开发ID对齐模块，将生成视频的ID表示与人脸识别特征对齐；4) 用预测的结构表示优化相关区域的身份修复。

Result: 在长序列运动和高强度运动场景中，生成质量更优，角色一致性和结构稳定性显著提升。

Conclusion: 通过结构对齐和ID对齐的表示对齐策略，在不牺牲灵活性的前提下实现了更连贯、稳定的人体图像动画生成。

Abstract: The field of image-to-video generation has made remarkable progress. However, challenges such as human limb twisting and facial distortion persist, especially when generating long videos or modeling intensive motions. Existing human image animation works address these issues by incorporating human-specific semantic representations, e.g., dense poses or ID embeddings, as additional conditions. However, conditioning on these representations could decrease the generation flexibility. Moreover, their reliance on RGB pixel supervision also lacks emphasis on learning necessary 3D geometric relationships and temporal coherence. In contrast, we introduce a novel approach named SemanticREPA that leverages these semantic representations as supervision signals through representation alignment. Specifically, we begin by training a structure alignment module that aligns the structure representations obtained from video latents with video depth estimation features. We then fix the pretrained module, and utilize it to provide additional supervision on the structure representations of the diffusion models, achieving structure rectification to generate coherent and stable human structures. Simultaneously, we develop an ID alignment module to align the ID representations of the generated videos to face recognition features. We further propose to use the predicted structure representations to refine identity restoration in relevant regions. With structure and ID alignment, our method demonstrates superior quality on extended character motions and enhanced character consistency.

</details>


### [223] [GemDepth: Geometry-Embedded Features for 3D-Consistent Video Depth](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10525)
*Yuecheng LiulJunda Cheng,Longliang Liu,Wenjing Liao,Hanrui Cheng,Yuzhou Wang,Xin Yang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: GemDepth通过显式注入相机运动先验和几何嵌入模块，结合交替时空Transformer，在视频深度估计中同时提升了空间细节精度和时间一致性，达到了SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频深度估计方法主要依赖Transformer进行时序平滑，但在旋转或剧烈视角变化下难以维持严格的3D几何一致性，且在精细区域存在空间模糊和时间不一致问题。

Method: 提出GemDepth框架，核心包括：(1) 几何嵌入模块(GEM)预测帧间相机姿态生成隐式几何嵌入，注入运动先验；(2) 交替时空Transformer(ASTT)捕捉潜在点级对应关系，同时增强空间精度和时间一致性；(3) 数据高效训练策略。

Result: 在多个数据集上取得了SOTA性能，尤其在复杂动态场景中表现优异。

Conclusion: 显式感知相机运动和全局3D结构是实现3D一致性的前提，GemDepth通过几何引导有效弥合了高效率与鲁棒几何一致性之间的差距。

Abstract: Video depth estimation extends monocular prediction into the temporal domain to ensure coherence. However, existing methods often suffer from spatial blurring in fine-detail regions and temporal inconsistencies. We argue that current approaches, which primarily rely on temporal smoothing via Transformers, struggle to maintain strict 3D geometric consistency-particularly under rotations or drastic view changes. To address this, we propose GemDepth, a framework built on the insight that an explicit awareness of camera motion and global 3D structure is a prerequisite for 3D consistency. Distinctively, GemDepth introduces a Geometry-Embedding Module (GEM) that predicts inter-frame camera poses to generate implicit geometric embeddings. This injection of motion priors equips the network with intrinsic 3D perception and alignment capabilities. Guided by these geometric cues, our Alternating Spatio-Temporal Transformer (ASTT) captures latent point-level correspondences to simultaneously enhance spatial precision for sharp details and enforce rigorous temporal consistency. Furthermore, GemDepth employs a data-efficient training strategy, effectively bridging the gap between high efficiency and robust geometric consistency. As shown in Fig.2, comprehensive evaluations demonstrate that GemDepth achieves state-of-the-art performance across multiple datasets, particularly in complex dynamic scenarios. The code is publicly available at: https://github.com/Yuecheng919/GemDepth

</details>


### [224] [TIE: Time Interval Encoding for Video Generation over Events](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10543)
*Zhilei Shu,Shangwen Zhu,Zihang Liang,Xiaofan Li,Qianyu Peng,Xinyu Cui,Bo Ye,Yiming Li,Fan Cheng,Jian Zhao,Yang Cao,Zheng-Jun Zha,Ruili Feng*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出时间间隔编码（TIE），一种基于RoCE的即插即用间隔感知位置编码方法，将时间间隔作为DiT交叉注意力中的一等公民，解决现有视频生成器无法表示重叠事件和时间间隔的问题。在OmniEvents数据集上，TIE将时序约束满足率从77.34%提升至96.03%，边界误差从0.261s降至0.073s。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成模型（如DiT）使用逐点位置编码表示时间，无法处理重叠事件（68%的通用视频片段和99%以上的机器人/游戏视频存在此类情况），存在维度不匹配问题：时间间隔和重叠事件在数学上无法被注意力机制表示。

Method: 提出时间间隔编码（TIE），一种基于RoPE兼容的双线性注意力的即插即用间隔感知方法。核心包含两个原则：时间可积性（事件聚合其整个持续时间的位里证据）和持续时间不变性（消除对较长间隔的琐碎偏置）。在均匀核下，推导出高效的闭式sinc解，保留标准注意力接口并通过间隔积分自然衰减边界噪声。

Result: 在OmniEvents数据集上：时序约束满足率从77.34%提升至96.03%，边界误差从0.261s降至0.073s，轨迹级时序对齐指标也得到改善，同时保持基础DiT模型的视觉质量。

Conclusion: TIE作为即插即用的间隔感知编码方法，有效解决了现有视频生成器无法处理重叠事件的关键局限，显著提升了时序可控性，同时不牺牲生成质量。

Abstract: Director-style prompting, robotic action prediction, and interactive video agents demand temporal grounding over concurrent events -- a regime in which 68% of general clips and over 99% of robotics/gameplay clips contain overlapping events, yet existing multi-event generators rest on a single-active-prompt assumption. However, modern video generators, such as Diffusion Transformers (DiT), represent time as discrete points through point-wise positional encodings. This formulation creates a fundamental dimension mismatch: temporally extended intervals and overlapping events are mathematically unrepresentable to the attention mechanism. In this paper, we propose Time Interval Encoding (TIE), a principled, plug-and-play interval-aware generalization of rotary embeddings that elevates time intervals to first-class primitives inside DiT cross-attention. Rather than introducing another heuristic interval embedding, we show that, within RoPE-compatible bilinear attention, TIE is characterized by two basic principles: Temporal Integrability, which requires an event to aggregate positional evidence over its full duration, and Duration Invariance, which removes the trivial bias toward longer intervals. Under a uniform kernel, this characterization yields an efficient closed-form sinc-based solution that preserves the standard attention interface and naturally attenuates boundary noise through interval integration. Empirically, TIE preserves the visual quality of the base DiT model while substantially improving temporal controllability. In our experiments on the OmniEvents dataset, it improves human-verified Temporal Constraint Satisfaction Rate from 77.34% to 96.03% and reduces temporal boundary error from 0.261s to 0.073s, while also improving trajectory-level temporal alignment metrics. The code and dataset are available at https://github.com/MatrixTeam-AI/TIE.

</details>


### [225] [DeepSight: Long-Horizon World Modeling via Latent States Prediction for End-to-End Autonomous Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10564)
*Lingjun Zhang,Changjie Wu,Linzhe Shi,Jiangyang Li,Jiaxin Liu,Lei Yang,Hang Zhang,Mu Xu,Hong Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出DeepSight，一种将世界模型与VLM结合的端到端自动驾驶方法，通过在BEV空间并行预测未来帧的潜在语义特征实现长时域建模，并引入自适应文本推理机制利用社会知识改善长尾场景表现，在Bench2drive基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有端到端自动驾驶系统虽引入VLM架构，但其推理机制直接沿用通用领域方法，缺乏针对自动驾驶场景（特别是视觉推理模块）的深入定制化探索。

Method: 1) 构建驾驶世界模型，在BEV空间中对连续未来帧的潜在语义特征进行并行预测，实现长时域未来状态建模；2) 引入高效自适应文本推理机制，利用额外社会知识与推理能力改善长尾场景下的驾驶性能。

Result: 在闭环Bench2drive基准上取得了最先进（SOTA）的结果。

Conclusion: 该方法是一种新颖、高效且有效的端到端自动驾驶方案，其代码已开源。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving systems are increasingly integrating Vision-Language Model (VLM) architectures, incorporating text reasoning or visual reasoning to enhance the robustness and accuracy of driving decisions. However, the reasoning mechanisms employed in most methods are direct adaptations from general domains, lacking in-depth exploration tailored to autonomous driving scenarios, particularly within visual reasoning modules. In this paper, we propose a driving world model that performs parallel prediction of latent semantic features for consecutive future frames in the bird's-eye-view (BEV) space, thereby enabling long-horizon modeling of future world states. We also introduce an efficient and adaptive text reasoning mechanism that utilizes additional social knowledge and reasoning capabilities to further improve driving performance in challenging long-tail scenarios. We present a novel, efficient, and effective approach that achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) results on the closed-loop Bench2drive benchmark. Codes are available at: https://github.com/hotdogcheesewhite/DeepSight.

</details>


### [226] [SenseBench: A Benchmark for Remote Sensing Low-Level Visual Perception and Description in Large Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10576)
*Chen Zhong,Xiao An,Jiaxing Sun,Zihan Gui,Guangyi Yang,Wei He*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: SenseBench是首个面向遥感图像低层视觉感知与描述的诊断基准，包含超过1万实例、6大类22种细粒度退化类型，通过物理驱动的分层分类法统一无参考和全参考范式，评估了29个VLM，揭示了领域偏差、多失真坍塌、流畅性幻觉和感知-描述反转效应等问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有IQA方法输出不可解释的标量分数，无法满足遥感专家对物理驱动退化诊断的需求；VLM虽能提供语言化的质量评估，但其视觉先验偏向自然图像，能否跨越领域鸿沟感知和描述遥感图像退化尚缺乏系统研究。

Method: 构建物理驱动的分层退化分类体系，统一无参考和全参考范式；设计两个互补评估协议：客观低层视觉感知和主观诊断描述；收集超过1万实例，涵盖6大类22种细粒度退化类型；对29个SOTA VLM进行全面评估。

Result: 29个VLM在SenseBench上表现出偏斜的领域先验、多失真坍塌、流畅性幻觉以及感知-描述反转效应。

Conclusion: SenseBench为遥感低层视觉感知领域提供了可靠的评估平台和高质量诊断数据，有助于推动VLM在该领域的发展。

Abstract: Low-level visual perception underpins reliable remote sensing (RS) image analysis, yet current image quality assessment (IQA) methods output uninterpretable scalar scores rather than characterizing physics-driven RS degradations, deviating markedly from the diagnostic needs of RS experts. While Vision-Language Models (VLMs) present a compelling alternative by delivering language-grounded IQA, their visual priors are heavily biased toward ground-level natural images. Consequently, whether VLMs can overcome this domain gap to perceive and articulate RS artifacts remains insufficiently studied. To bridge this gap, we propose \textbf{SenseBench}, the first dedicated diagnostic benchmark for RS low-level visual perception and description. Driven by a physics-based hierarchical taxonomy that unifies both non-reference and reference-based paradigms, SenseBench features over 10K meticulously curated instances across 6 major and 22 fine-grained RS degradation categories. Specifically, two complementary protocols are designed for evaluation: objective low-level visual \textit{perception} and subjective diagnostic \textit{description}. Comprehensive evaluation of 29 state-of-the-art VLMs reveals not only skewed domain priors and multi-distortion collapse, but also \textit{fluency illusion} and a \textit{perception-description inversion} effect. We hope SenseBench provides a robust evaluation testbed and high-quality diagnostic data to advance the development of VLMs in RS low-level perception. Code and datasets are available \href{https://github.com/Zhong-Chenchen/SenseBench}{\textcolor{blue}{here}}.

</details>


<div id='cs.CL'></div>

# cs.CL [[Back]](#toc)

### [227] [Sanity Checks for Long-Form Hallucination Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08346)
*Geigh Zollicoffer,Minh Vu,Hongli Zhan,Raymond Li,Manish Bhattarai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出受控不变性方法（Force和Remove测试）揭示现有推理轨迹幻觉检测方法主要依赖最终答案的表面线索而非推理质量，并证明基于词法轨迹特征的轻量级检测器TRACT在控制这些伪影后仍具竞争力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前幻觉检测方法虽使用思维链推理轨迹，但不确定它们真正评估的是推理过程本身，还是仅仅利用最终答案的表面相关特征（artifacts）。

Method: 提出受控不变性方法论：1) Force测试：保持推理轨迹不变，将最终答案替换为真实答案；2) Remove测试：移除宣告答案的步骤但保留轨迹。同时提出TRACT轻量级检测器，基于词法轨迹特征（如犹豫趋势、步长动态、跨回答词汇收敛性）。

Result: 控制最终答案伪影后，TRACT在未扰动轨迹上达到或超越现有基线，证明检测信号存在于轨迹本身而非答案层面。

Conclusion: 当前推理感知幻觉检测的核心挑战不是轨迹中缺乏信号，而是未能将其与端点线索（最终答案伪影）分离开来。

Abstract: Hallucination detection methods for large language models increasingly operate on chain-of-thought reasoning traces, yet it remains unclear whether they evaluate the reasoning itself or merely exploit surface correlates of the final answer. We introduce a controlled-invariance methodology that exposes this distinction through two oracle tests: \textsc{Force}, which replaces each response's final answer with the ground truth while preserving the reasoning trace, and \textsc{Remove}, which strips answer-announcement steps while leaving the trajectory intact. This reveals if their predictive power derives from answer-level artifacts rather than from the structure or validity of intermediate reasoning. We further show that once these artifacts are controlled for, effective detection does not necessarily require complex learned representations: TRACT, a lightweight scorer built on lexical trajectory features (hedging trends, step-length dynamics, and cross-response vocabulary convergence), achieves strong robustness while remaining competitive with or outperforming existing baselines on unperturbed traces. These findings suggest that the current central challenge in reasoning-aware hallucination detection is not the absence of signal in the trace, but the failure to isolate it from endpoint cues.

</details>


### [228] [How Much Do Circuits Tell Us? Measuring the Consistency and Specificity of Language Model Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08348)
*Michael Li,Nishant Subramani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过边缘属性修补方法，在6个任务和7个模型中测量电路复用、一致性和特异性。发现任务内电路复用率高且共享组件对性能至关重要（消融导致近100%准确率下降），但电路缺乏任务特异性——消融一个任务的电路对另一任务性能的损害程度与其自身电路相当。原因是跨任务电路存在大量因果重要的重叠部分，仅有少量任务特有组件且贡献有限。这表明虽然电路发现能识别重要组件，但其缺乏任务特异性限制了针对性理解和干预的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有可解释性电路框架通常评估组件的必要性和充分性，但对电路是否具有任务特异性（即组件是否唯一服务于特定任务）的研究不足。作者希望系统量化电路复用程度，探究其在任务内的一致性（重复出现的组件）和跨任务的特异性（独有组件）。

Method: 使用边缘属性修补（edge attribution patching）方法，在6个不同任务和7个模型上，测量每个样本的电路（per-example circuits），分析组件在任务内的复用情况（一致性）、跨任务的复用情况（特异性），并通过消融实验验证共享组件的因果重要性。

Result: 1）任务内电路复用率高，共享组件对性能至关重要，消融导致近100%的相对准确率下降。2）电路并非任务特异的——消融一个任务的电路对另一任务性能的损害程度与该任务自身电路相当。3）跨任务电路存在大量因果重要的重叠部分。4）确实存在少量任务特定组件，但这些组件仅对电路性能贡献微小。

Conclusion: 虽然当前在注意力头和MLP层级别发现的电路能识别重要组件，但它们的任务特异性不足，这引发了对电路能否真正支持针对性理解和干预模型行为的质疑。电路发现方法需要进一步发展以区分共享通用功能和任务特定功能的组件。

Abstract: The circuits framework in mechanistic interpretability aims to identify causally important sparse subgraphs of model components, typically evaluated by measuring necessity and sufficiency. We measure circuit reuse, the proportion of components shared across per-example circuits within a task, and investigate two less-studied properties of this: consistency, the recurrence of components within a task, and specificity, their uniqueness to a task. Using edge attribution patching across six tasks and seven models, we find that within-task reuse is high and that shared components are necessary for task performance, with ablations causing up to $\sim$100% relative accuracy drops. However, circuits turn out not to be task-specific: ablating one task's circuit damages another task's performance about as much as that task's own circuit does. We discover that this is due to substantial overlap between circuits across tasks, which are causally important for performance. Some circuits do contain a smaller set of task-specific components, but these account for only a modest portion of circuit performance. Overall, our findings suggest that while circuit discovery at the level of attention heads and MLP layers identifies important components, their lack of task-specificity raises questions about the degree to which circuits can support targeted understanding and intervention on model behavior.

</details>


### [229] [Change My View? The Dynamics of Persuasion and Polarization in Online Discourse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08383)
*David Freeborn,Malihe Alikani,Anthony Sicilia*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究利用大语言模型分析Reddit的r/ChangeMyView论坛语料，发现辩论中表达让步和共情对齐的策略显著促进信念改变，而正面反驳、可信度攻击和话题偏离则降低信念改变的可能性。这表明有效的公共推理不仅依赖于证据内容，同样依赖于关系框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 日常话语中的信念分歧现象与哲学层面"共享证据和理性论证应导致观点趋同"的假设存在矛盾，作者希望探究实际辩论中促成信念改变的关键因素。

Method: 使用大语言模型对Reddit的r/ChangeMyView论坛讨论进行半程预测（概率估计作为会话基线），并通过混合机器辅助程序对每条回复编码十种修辞策略（让步、共情、逻辑挑战、可信度诉求等），分析各策略对信念改变预测的贡献。

Result: 加入修辞策略特征显著提升了预测能力；让步和共情对齐显著增加信念改变概率，而正面反驳、可信度攻击和话题偏离减少信念改变概率。

Conclusion: 有效公共推理既依赖证据内容也依赖关系框架，应修正规范性理性对话理论以纳入关系性因素。

Abstract: Philosophical accounts of persuasion often assume that shared evidence and rational argumentation should lead to a convergence of views between peers, yet everyday discourse often suggests otherwise. In this study, we use large language models to analyze a corpus of debates on Reddit's r/ChangeMyView, where belief revision is publicly signaled. Large language models were asked, halfway through each discussion, to forecast whether such an acknowledgement would arise; their probabilistic estimates serve as a conversational baseline. Each reply was then coded, through a hybrid machine-assisted procedure, for ten familiar rhetorical strategies -- concession, empathy, logical challenge, credibility appeals, and so forth. Adding these strategic features markedly improves predictive power and yields a consistent pattern: moves that express concession or empathetic alignment substantially increase the prospect of belief change, whereas frontal refutation, credibility attacks, and topic deflection diminish it. The findings indicate that effective public reasoning depends as much on relational framing as on evidential content, and they invite a refinement of normative accounts of rational dialogue.

</details>


### [230] [jina-embeddings-v5-omni: Text-Geometry-Preserving Multimodal Embeddings via Frozen-Tower Composition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08384)
*Florian Hönicke,Michael Günther,Andreas Koukounas,Kalim Akram,Scott Martens,Saba Sturua,Han Xiao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出冻结编码器模型组合方法，基于VLM架构，通过冻结文本和非文本编码器、仅训练连接组件（占总权重0.35%），构建了支持文本、图像、音频和视频输入的统一语义嵌入模型套件jina-embeddings-v5-omni，在极大降低训练成本的同时实现了与更大多模态嵌入模型相竞争的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大规模多模态嵌入模型通常需要全参数重训练或微调，训练成本高昂且容易破坏原有文本嵌入模型的性能。作者希望探索一种高效的方法，在保留已有高性能文本嵌入模型能力的同时，低成本地扩展其支持多种模态输入。

Method: 基于VLM架构（视觉-语言模型），将Jina Embeddings v5 Text模型作为骨干文本嵌入模型，为其添加图像和音频编码器。所有骨干模型和非文本编码器保持冻结，仅训练连接文本模型与新增编码器的连接组件，这部分参数仅占联合模型总权重的0.35%。

Result: 训练效率极高，仅训练极少参数；文本输入的嵌入与原始Jina Embeddings v5 Text模型完全相同；在多模态嵌入评估中，性能与更大规模的多模态嵌入模型几乎持平，达到与当前最优方法竞争的水平。

Conclusion: 冻结编码器模型组合是一种高效且有效的多模态嵌入模型构建方法，能够在保持文本嵌入能力不变、训练成本极低的前提下，实现与大规模全参数训练模型相当的多模态嵌入性能。

Abstract: In this work, we introduce frozen-encoder model composition, a novel approach to multimodal embedding models. We build on the VLM-style architecture, in which non-text encoders are adapted to produce input for a language model, which in turn generates embeddings for all varieties of input. We present the result: the jina-embeddings-v5-omni suite, a pair of models that encode text, image, audio, and video input into a single semantic embedding space. Our method is to extend the two Jina Embeddings v5 Text models to support additional media by adding encoders for images and audio. The backbone text embedding models and the added non-text media encoders remain frozen. We only trained the connecting components, representing 0.35% of the total weights of the joint model. Training is therefore much more efficient than full-parameter retraining. Additionally, the language model remains effectively unaltered, producing exactly the same embeddings for text inputs as the Jina Embeddings v5 Text models. Our evaluations show that this approach produces results that are competitive with the state-of-the-art, yielding nearly equal performance to larger multimodal embedding models.

</details>


### [231] [AIPO: : Learning to Reason from Active Interaction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08401)
*Junnan Liu,Linhao Luo,Thuy-Trang Vu,Gholamreza Haffari*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出AIPO框架，通过主动多智能体交互（验证、知识、推理三个协作智能体）增强强化学习中的探索，扩展策略模型推理能力边界，并在多个推理基准上取得一致提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RLVR算法的探索受限于策略模型自身能力边界，而引入外部专家演示的方法依赖完整轨迹级指导，样本效率低、信息稀疏，且将探索限制在静态指导空间内。

Method: 提出AIPO框架，在训练中让策略模型主动咨询三个协作智能体（验证、知识、推理智能体），获得细粒度、有针对性的指导；并引入重要性采样系数和裁剪策略来缓解离策略偏差和梯度消失问题。推理时无需依赖协作智能体。

Result: 在AIME、MATH500、GPQA-Diamond、LiveCodeBench等多个推理基准上，AIPO一致提升推理性能，在不同策略模型和RLVR算法上均表现鲁棒，有效扩展了策略模型的推理能力边界。

Conclusion: 主动多智能体交互能有效突破RLVR中策略模型的探索局限，AIPO是一种通用且高效的增强推理框架。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have demonstrated remarkable reasoning capabilities, largely stimulated by Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR). However, existing RL algorithms face a fundamental limitation: their exploration remains largely constrained by the inherent capability boundary of the policy model. Although recent methods introduce external expert demonstrations to extend this boundary, they typically rely on complete trajectory-level guidance, which is sample-inefficient, information-sparse, and may confine exploration to a static guidance space. Inspired by the potential of multi-agent systems, we propose $\textbf{AIPO}$, an enhanced reinforcement learning framework that improves LLM reasoning through active multi-agent interaction during exploration. Specifically, AIPO enables the policy model to proactively consult three functional collaborative agents, $\textit{Verify Agent}$, $\textit{Knowledge Agent}$, and $\textit{Reasoning Agent}$, when encountering reasoning bottlenecks, thereby receiving fine-grained and targeted guidance to actively expand its capability boundary during training. We further introduce a tailored importance sampling coefficient together with a clipping strategy to mitigate the off-policy bias and gradient vanishing issues that arise when learning from agent-provided feedback. After training, the policy model performs reasoning independently without relying on collaborative agents. Extensive experiments on diverse reasoning benchmarks, including AIME, MATH500, GPQA-Diamond, and LiveCodeBench, show that AIPO consistently improves reasoning performance, generalizes robustly across different policy models and RLVR algorithms, and effectively expands the reasoning capability boundary of the policy model.

</details>


### [232] [Built Environment Reasoning from Remote Sensing Imagery Using Large Vision--Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08404)
*Dongdong Wang,Deepak Balakrishnan,Ravi Srinivasan,Shenhao Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了利用大型语言模型（LLMs）结合遥感影像为智慧城市提供建筑环境分析，包括设计建议、施工评估、土地利用和风险识别，并通过多模态建模比较了InternVL和Qwen等先进LLMs的性能，证明了该方法在智慧城市决策中的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索利用大型语言模型结合遥感影像来辅助智慧城市的建筑环境分析与决策制定。

Method: 采用多空间尺度的遥感影像作为多模态语言模型的输入，评估其对建筑环境推理的影响，并比较InternVL和Qwen等先进LLMs在生成建筑环境建议时的准确性和可靠性。

Result: 实验结果表明，将遥感影像与大型语言模型相结合在辅助智慧城市决策方面具有潜力。

Conclusion: 集成遥感影像与大型语言模型能够有效支持智慧城市的建筑环境分析及决策制定。

Abstract: This work investigates the use of large language models (LLMs) for tasks in smart cities. The core idea is to leverage remote sensing imagery to characterize the built environment, including design suggestions, constructability assessment, landuse patterns, and risk identification. We examine remote sensing imagery at multiple spatial scales as inputs for multimodal language modeling and evaluate their effects on built-environment-related reasoning. In addition, we compare state-of-the-art LLMs, including InternVL and Qwen, in terms of accuracy and reliability when generating built environment recommendations. The results demonstrate the potential of integrating remote sensing imagery with large language models to assist smart cities and decision-making.

</details>


### [233] [Effective Explanations Support Planning Under Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08406)
*Hanqi Zhou,Britt Besch,Charley M. Wu,Tobias Gerstenberg*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个计算模型，将语言解释转化为行动规划（通过大语言模型生成策略先验和价值地图，再在部分可观测条件下执行规划），以解释效用为导向评估解释质量，并通过四项预注册实验验证了高质量解释能提升导航表现和被判断为更有帮助。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 从A到B的路径解释具有挑战性，因为需要模拟听者基于所听到的内容会如何行动。现有方法缺乏对语言如何转化为实际行动规划的建模，因此需要一种计算框架来捕捉这一过程。

Method: 1) 使用大语言模型将解释翻译成类似程序的引导信息（策略先验和价值地图）；2) 在部分可观测条件下由规划智能体执行该引导；3) 根据路径的效率和可靠性对解释评分，并对重新规划行为进行惩罚；4) 在24张地图上收集1,200条解释，进行四项预注册实验，包括有用性判断、基线导航测试以及不同质量解释的行为测试。

Result: 高分解释被判断为更有帮助；有解释的参与者比没有解释的表现更好；高质量解释比低质量解释对导航提升更大。

Conclusion: 程序性解释是一种效用导向的沟通方式，其核心在于语言如何在不确定性下被具体化为行动。

Abstract: Explaining how to get from A to B can be challenging. It requires mentally simulating what the listener will do based on what they are told. To capture this process, we propose a computational model that converts utterances into action plans: a large language model translates an explanation into program-like guidance (a policy prior and value map), and a planning agent executes it under partial observability. We score explanations by the efficiency and reliability of the resulting paths, penalizing replanning. Across four preregistered experiments, we collect a corpus of 1,200 explanations over 24 maps, elicit helpfulness judgments, measure baseline navigation, and test behavior with explanations of differing quality. Higher-scored explanations are judged more helpful and improve navigation: participants with explanations outperform those without, and high-scoring explanations help more than low-scoring ones. Together, these results show procedural explanation as utility-guided communication shaped by how language can be grounded into action under uncertainty.

</details>


### [234] [A Semantic-Sampling Framework for Evaluating Calibration in Open-Ended Question Answering](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08432)
*Zhanliang Wang,Jiancong Xiao,Ruochen Jin,Shu Yang,Bojian Hou,Li Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Sem-ECE（语义采样期望校准误差），一种适用于开放域问答场景的LLM校准评估框架。该方法通过对模型生成的答案进行语义分组并利用频率作为置信度，在两个变体（Sem₁-ECE和Sem₂-ECE）下均具有渐进无偏性，且两者差异可诊断问题难度。实验表明Sem-ECE优于口头置信度和现有采样方法，并能补充基于logit的评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM校准评估方法在开放域问答这一最常部署的场景中存在明显不足：基于logit的指标限制输出格式且需内部概率；口头置信度自报告且常过度自信；采样方法依赖于任务特定的提取规则且缺乏清晰的有限样本目标。因此需要一种适用于开放域问答的校准评估框架。

Method: 提出Sem-ECE框架，通过对模型采样的多个答案进行语义分组（将语义等价的答案归为一类），以语义类的频率作为置信度。包含两个估计器：Sem₁-ECE（同一样本自一致性分数）和Sem₂-ECE（分离答案选择与置信度评估的留出变体）。作者从理论上证明两者均具有渐进无偏性，并推导了它们在易/难题上的行为差异。

Result: 在5个领先商业LLM和3个开放域QA基准上的实验验证了理论预测：Sem-ECE优于口头置信度和现有采样方法；Sem₁-ECE与Sem₂-ECE在简单问题上一致，在困难问题上产生差异（Sem₂具有更小的校准误差）；Sem-ECE能补充基于logit的评估（当内部概率不可用时）。

Conclusion: Sem-ECE为开放域QA场景提供了理论上可靠、实践中有效的校准评估方案，其两个变体间的差异还能作为问题难度的诊断工具，弥补了现有方法在LLM真实部署场景中的评估缺陷。

Abstract: Calibration measures whether a model's predicted confidence aligns with its empirical accuracy, and is central to the reliable deployment of large language models (LLMs) in high-stakes domains such as medicine and law. While much recent work focuses on improving LLM calibration, the equally important question of how to evaluate it in realistic settings remains underdeveloped. Open-ended question answering (QA), the most common deployment setting for modern LLMs, is where existing evaluation methods fall short: logit-based metrics need restricted output formats and internal probabilities; verbalized confidence is self-reported and often overconfident; and sampling-based methods rely on task-specific extraction rules without a clear finite-sample target. We introduce Sem-ECE (Semantic-Sampling Expected Calibration Error), a calibration evaluation framework for open-ended QA that samples answers from the model, groups them into semantic classes, and uses the resulting frequencies as confidence. We study two estimators within this framework: Sem$_1$-ECE, the same-sample self-consistency score, and Sem$_2$-ECE, a held-out variant that separates answer selection from confidence evaluation. We prove both are asymptotically unbiased, and further show that they agree on easy questions but diverge on hard ones with Sem$_2$ achieving strictly smaller calibration error, so their gap also serves as a diagnostic for question difficulty. Experiments on three open-ended QA benchmarks across five leading commercial LLMs match our theoretical predictions and show that Sem-ECE outperforms verbalized confidence and existing sampling-based methods, while complementing logit-based evaluation when internal probabilities are unavailable.

</details>


### [235] [Magis-Bench: Evaluating LLMs on Magistrate-Level Legal Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08437)
*Ramon Pires,Thales Sales Almeida,Celio Larcher Junior,Giovana Bonás,Hugo Abonizio,Marcos Piau,Roseval Malaquias Junior,Thiago Laitz,Rodrigo Nogueira*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Magis-Bench是一个评估LLM在司法层面法律推理与写作能力的基准，包含74道来自巴西司法考试的问题。在23个SOTA模型评测中，Gemini-3-Pro-Preview得分最高(6.97/10)，但最佳模型得分仍低于满分70%，表明司法级法律推理对当前LLM仍有挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有法律AI基准主要关注LLM生成法律论点或文档的能力，但评估LLM"判断"法律论证——权衡竞争性主张、将法律原则应用于事实、作出合理裁决——的能力同样重要，却缺乏相应评测基准。

Method: 构建包含74道来自2023-2025年巴西司法考试问题的基准，涵盖多轮结构化的论述性法律分析题和完整的民事/刑事司法判决撰写实践题。采用LLM-as-a-judge方法，使用4个独立前沿模型作为评估者，对23个SOTA LLM进行评测。

Result: 评估者间一致性高(Kendall's W=0.984, 两两Kendall's τ≥0.897)。Gemini-3-Pro-Preview最高(6.97/10)，其次为Gemini-3-Flash-Preview(6.67)和Claude-4.5-Opus(6.46)。所有模型得分均低于满分70%，表明任务仍有挑战性。

Conclusion: 司法级别的法律推理和写作对当前LLM仍具有挑战性；发布了完整基准、模型输出和评估代码以支持进一步研究。

Abstract: Existing benchmarks for legal AI focus primarily on tasks where LLMs must produce legal arguments or documents, yet the capacity to \emph{judge} such arguments -- weighing competing claims, applying doctrine to facts, and rendering reasoned decisions -- is arguably as fundamental to a well-functioning legal system as advocacy itself. We introduce Magis-Bench, a benchmark for evaluating LLMs on magistrate-level writing tasks derived from recent Brazilian competitive examinations for judicial positions. Magis-Bench comprises 74 questions from eight examinations conducted between 2023 and 2025, including discursive legal analysis questions with multi-turn structure and practical exercises requiring the composition of complete civil and criminal judicial sentences. We evaluate 23 state-of-the-art LLMs using an LLM-as-a-judge methodology with four independent frontier models as evaluators. Our results show strong inter-judge agreement (Kendall's $W = 0.984$; pairwise Kendall's $τ\ge 0.897$), with Google's Gemini-3-Pro-Preview achieving the highest average score (6.97/10), followed by Gemini-3-Flash-Preview (6.67) and Claude-4.5-Opus (6.46). Even the best-performing models score below 70\% of the maximum, indicating that judicial-level legal reasoning and writing remain challenging for current LLMs. We release the complete benchmark, model outputs, and evaluation code to support further research on legal AI capabilities.

</details>


### [236] [Can Language Models Identify Side Effects of Breast Cancer Radiation Treatments?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08439)
*Natalie Seah,Danielle S. Bitterman,Daphna Spiegel,Thomas Hartvigsen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向部署的应激测试框架，用于评估大语言模型在乳腺癌放疗副作用列表生成中的可靠性。通过21个患者档案、配对临床场景和多提示策略，系统评估了7个指令微调LLM，并与两个学术医疗中心的多位乳腺癌放疗肿瘤学家整理的知情同意参考标准进行比较。研究发现LLM对文档细节变化敏感，存在精度-召回权衡，系统性地忽略罕见和远期副作用，且单独使用时限制副作用数量会降低精确度。将输出锚定在临床医生整理的副作用列表上可显著提高可靠性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确传达癌症治疗副作用对癌症幸存者至关重要（如知情同意场景），但由于临床知识不足和EHR系统碎片化，这一任务仍然充满挑战。LLM有潜力辅助此任务，但其在肿瘤学幸存者护理背景下的可靠性尚未得到充分理解。

Method: 构建21个乳腺癌患者档案，设计仅在放疗方案上有所区别的配对临床场景，使用多种提示策略评估7个指令微调LLM。将LLM输出与两个学术医疗中心由7位以上乳腺放疗肿瘤学家团队整理的知情同意参考标准进行比较，该参考标准根据剂量分割、照射野和位置映射副作用，并按频率和时间发作分类。

Result: LLM对文档细节的微小变化敏感，存在精度和召回率之间的权衡，系统性低估罕见和远期副作用。单独使用时，限制副作用数量反而降低精确度；将输出锚定在临床医生整理的副作用列表上能显著提高可靠性和鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 这些发现突显了LLM在肿瘤学中使用的重要局限性，并提出了更安全、更具信息性的幸存者护理应用的实际设计选择。

Abstract: Accurately communicating the side effects of cancer treatments to cancer survivors is critical, particularly in settings such as informed consent, where clinicians must clearly and comprehensively convey potential treatment toxicities. However, this task remains challenging due to clinical knowledge deficits about adverse treatment effects and fragmentation across electronic health record (EHR) systems. Large language models (LLMs) have the potential to assist in this task, though their reliability in oncology survivorship contexts remains poorly understood. We present a deployment-oriented stress-testing framework for evaluating LLM-generated radiation side effect lists in breast cancer treatment and survivorship care. Using 21 breast cancer patient profiles, we construct paired patient clinical scenarios that differ only in radiotherapy regimens to evaluate seven instruction-tuned LLMs under multiple prompting regimes. We then compare LLM outputs to a clinician-curated reference derived from informed consent documents at two major academic medical centers and developed by a team including more than seven breast radiation oncologists. The reference maps radiation dose-fractionation, fields, and locations to associated toxicities, broken down by frequency and temporal onset. Across models, we reveal sensitivity to minor documentation changes, trade-offs between precision and recall, and systematic under-recall of rare and long-term side effects. When used alone, constraints on the number of side effects generated reduce precision, and grounding outputs in clinician-curated side effect lists substantially improves reliability and robustness. These findings highlight important limitations of LLM use in oncology and suggest practical design choices for safer and more informative survivorship-focused applications.

</details>


### [237] [Revisiting the syntax of imperatives in Yemeni Arabic: An Agree across phases approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08447)
*Mohammed Q. Shormani*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文基于"一致跨越语段"(AAP)方法重新审视也门阿拉伯语祈使句的句法，提出祈使句的主题主语是第二人称pro，而前置的显性成分是话题(C域元素)，通过匹配操作形成A'链，核心祈使句则依赖空话题与话语衔接。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有分析未能充分解释也门阿拉伯语祈使句中简单与复杂结构的统一句法机制，尤其是显性成分与空主语的句法地位及其与话语的互动关系。

Method: 采用Agree across phases (AAP) 句法框架，结合句法-话语接口分析，通过设置第二人称pro作为主语、显性成分作为约同性话题(C域元素)，并引入匹配操作和空话题机制来统一处理各类祈使结构。

Result: 成功统一解释了也门阿拉伯语中简单祈使句、复杂祈使句以及A'链结构，证明了句法与话语的密切互动在祈使句中的必要性。

Conclusion: 也门阿拉伯语祈使句的句法结构需区分主语(2人称pro)与话题(显性前置成分)，核心祈使句通过空话题实现话语依赖的解释，验证了AAP框架的适用性。

Abstract: This article revisits the syntax of imperatives in Yemeni Arabic proposing an Agree acros phases (AAP) approach. I argue that the AAP approach successfully accounts for both simple and complex imperative constructions, including A'-chain structures, by establishing a close interactions between syntax and discourse. The study demonstrates that this interface is motivated by the interpretive and performative functions associated with imperatives, linking informational structure with propositional structure. It is also proposed that the thematic subject of imperatives is a 2-person pro, whereas any overt pronominal or nominal element occurring preverbally is not a subject, but rather a C-domain element, precisely aboutness topic. These topics serve as the logical subjects of imperatives and enter into a coreferentiality relationship with pro. This relation is analyzed as APP involving Match, yielding both local and non-local A'-chains. For core imperatives, viz., lacking an overt topic, I propose a null topic to (re)merge in Spec,TopP, whose interpretation depends on the discourse.

</details>


### [238] [Do Benchmarks Underestimate LLM Performance? Evaluating Hallucination Detection With LLM-First Human-Adjudicated Assessment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08462)
*I. F. Atasoy,B. Mutlu,E. A. Sezer,A. Wahdan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究针对LLM在摘要任务中的上下文幻觉检测问题，通过人工裁决重新评估QAGS-C和SummEval数据集，发现模型辅助的重新评估能显著提高标注一致性（6-8%）和模型准确率（2-9%），表明单轮标注在模糊任务中不可靠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM在RAG和Agent系统中的幻觉问题依然严峻，现有基准数据集（QAGS-C、SummEval）的人工标注与LLM判断之间存在系统性分歧，需要更可靠的评估标准。

Method: 对比Gemini 2.5 Flash和GPT-5 Mini的reason/span预测与原始基准标注；对冲突样本进行2名跨文化裁决者的人工重新评估；计算裁决后的三方一致率和模型准确率变化。

Result: 重新评估后三方一致性提升6.38%（QAGS-C）和7.62%（SummEval）；GPT准确率提升4.25%/2.34%，Gemini提升8.51%/3.80%；裁决者倾向于认同提供明确推理的模型判断；总体裁决者一致率83-87%。

Conclusion: 对于模糊性高的任务，单轮人工标注不足以保证基准可靠性，模型辅助的重新评估能生成更可靠的标注基准。

Abstract: Hallucination remains a persistent challenge in Large Language Models (LLMs), particularly in context-grounded settings such as RAG and agentic AI systems. This study focuses on contextual hallucination detection in summarization tasks. We analyze the QAGS-C and SummEval datasets by comparing original benchmark annotations with reason and span-based predictions from Gemini 2.5 Flash and GPT-5 Mini. To address systematic divergences between human labels and LLM judgments, we re-evaluated all conflicted samples through a human adjudication process involving 2 cross-cultural adjudicators. Following this re-evaluation, triple agreement (between human, GPT, and Gemini) increased by 6.38% for QAGS-C and 7.62% for SummEval. Similarly, model accuracy improved, with GPT increasing by 4.25% on QAGS-C and 2.34% on SummEval, while Gemini showed gains of 8.51% and 3.80%, respectively. Notably, adjudicators frequently sided with the models' judgments over original human annotations when LLMs provided explicit reasoning. Overall human adjudicator agreement ranged between 83% and 87%. These findings suggest that for ambiguity-prone tasks, single-pass annotations may be insufficient, and model-assisted re-evaluation yields more reliable benchmarks.

</details>


### [239] [PYTHALAB-MERA: Validation-Grounded Memory, Retrieval, and Acceptance Control for Frozen-LLM Coding Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08468)
*Mehmet Iscan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为PYTHALAB-MERA的轻量级外部控制器，用于在本地冻结语言模型上进行验证条件化的代码生成，通过在强化学习编码任务中结合外部记忆、AST技能检索和TD(lambda)延迟信用分配，在严格验证条件下取得了8/9的通过率，而基线方法为0/9。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的局部LLM编码智能体在需要执行反馈、持久状态和有限修复的场景中工作，但静态检索、长上下文提示、自我改进和执行反馈修复等方法无法联合提供验证基础的 episodic 记忆、自适应检索-动作选择、延迟信用分配和围绕冻结本地模型的结构化技能复用。

Method: PYTHALAB-MERA包含一个冻结的语言模型（负责提出完整的源文件）和一个外部控制器（决定哪些记忆记录和AST派生技能进入下一轮提示，通过快速失败管道验证每个候选方案，将验证结果转化为有限形状的奖励，并通过TD(lambda)风格的资格迹传播延迟信用）。

Result: 在三个任务、三次重复、三次尝试预算的严格RL编码测试中，PYTHALAB-MERA通过8/9的严格验证，而自我改进基线和GRACE扩展均通过0/9。

Conclusion: 外部记忆与检索控制器在该记录设置下显著提高了验证成功率，但研究结果不声称通用代码合成、最先进性能、形式化程序正确性或形式化安全性。

Abstract: Local LLM-based coding agents increasingly work in settings where correctness is earned through execution feedback, persistent state, and bounded repair, not through a single fluent answer. Static retrieval, long-context prompting, self-refinement, execution-feedback repair, and reinforcement learning over model weights each address part of this setting, but they do not jointly provide validation-grounded episodic memory, adaptive retrieval-action selection, delayed credit assignment, and structural skill reuse around a frozen local model. We introduce PYTHALAB-MERA, a lightweight external controller for local validation-conditioned code generation. The frozen language model proposes complete source files; the controller decides which memory records and AST-derived skills should enter the next prompt, validates each candidate through a fail-fast pipeline, converts validation outcomes into bounded shaped rewards, and propagates delayed credit through TD(lambda)-style eligibility traces. We evaluate the implementation as a local CLI artifact on reinforcement-learning coding tasks with strict validation gates. In the measured hard RL setting with three tasks, three repetitions, and a three-attempt budget, PYTHALAB-MERA passed 8/9 strict validations; the self-refinement baseline and the investigated GRACE extension each passed 0/9. These results support a deliberately bounded claim: in this recorded setting, the external memory-and-retrieval controller improved validation success. They do not establish general-purpose code synthesis, state-of-the-art performance, formal program correctness, or formal safety.

</details>


### [240] [A Computational Operationalisation of Competing Maturational Theories of Syntactic Development via Statistical Grammar Induction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08476)
*Mila Marcheva,Suchir Salhan,Weiwei Sun*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文通过统计语法归纳方法，在控制输入和学习算法的条件下，比较了两种句法范畴发展理论（自下而上的GROWING理论和内向的INWARD理论）对儿童语言习得的预测，发现GROWING理论在三项评估指标上显著优于INWARD理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究儿童在第一语言发展过程中习得中间句法范畴的顺序，检验成熟理论的不同预测——底层的词汇和屈折结构先出现（GROWING），还是话语相关范畴更早被访问（INWARD）。

Method: 将两种理论假设操作化为计算模型，使用统计语法归纳方法，在输入数据和学习算法保持恒定的条件下，评估不同成熟顺序对可学习性的影响。

Result: GROWING理论在三项评估指标上均显著优于INWARD理论。

Conclusion: 儿童语言发展中句法范畴的习得遵循自下而上的顺序，底层词汇和屈折结构先于话语相关范畴出现。

Abstract: This paper is concerned with what intermediate syntactic categories children acquire during first language development, and in what order. Maturational theories make different predictions. Bottom-up accounts (GROWING) propose that lexical and inflectional structure emerges first, while inward accounts (INWARD) predict early access to discourse-related categories. We computationally operationalise these hypotheses of staged syntactic emergence using statistical grammar induction, asking what each proposed ordering makes learnable when input and learning algorithm are held constant. Our framework makes category acquisition explicit and allows us to explore how different maturational orderings shape the structure that can be learned under identical conditions. Based on this operationalisation, the GROWING account significantly outperforms the INWARD account across three evaluation metrics.

</details>


### [241] [Do Agents Need to Plan Step-by-Step? Rethinking Planning Horizon in Data-Centric Tool Calling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08477)
*Naoki Otani,Nikita Bhutani,Hannah Kim,Dan Zhang,Estevam Hruschka*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对LLM智能体在数据密集型任务中的规划策略，通过系统对比全视野（FH）规划和单步（SH）规划，发现FH规划在保持准确率的同时，可减少2-3倍token消耗，表明对于定义明确的数据任务，惰性重规划的FH策略可作为更高效的选择。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM智能体规划策略存在全视野规划和单步规划两种范式，但单步执行（逐步推理+工具调用）被认为是默认选择，其假设是急切执行监控对适应性至关重要。本文旨在通过受控实验重新审视这一假设，探究在定义明确的数据任务中，这一假设是否必要。

Method: 通过受控实证研究，将规划视界（planning horizon）作为关键架构特征进行隔离，系统分析拓扑复杂性（深度和广度）和工具鲁棒性对两种范式的影响。在知识库问答和多跳问答任务上进行实验对比。

Result: FH规划在各类深度、广度和鲁棒性条件下，准确率与SH持平，但token消耗减少了2-3倍。

Conclusion: 对于定义明确的数据任务，逐步的急切监控往往是不必要的；全视野规划配合按需重规划（惰性重规划）可作为更高效的默认方案。

Abstract: Explicit planning is a critical capability for LLM-based agents solving complex data-centric tasks, which require precise tool calling over external data sources. Existing strategies fall into two paradigms based on planning horizon: (1) full-horizon (FH), which generates a complete plan before execution, and (2) single-step horizon (SH), which interleaves each action (tool call) with incremental reasoning and observation. While step-by-step execution is a common default under the assumption that eager execution monitoring is necessary for adaptability, we revisit this assumption for well-defined data-centric tasks. Our controlled empirical study isolates planning horizon as the key architectural feature and systematically analyzes the effects of topological complexity and tool robustness on both paradigms. Our experiments across Knowledge Base Question Answering and Multi-hop QA show that FH planning with lazy replanning achieves accuracy parity with SH across varying depths, breadths, and robustness levels, while using 2-3x fewer tokens. These findings suggest that for well-defined data-centric tasks, eager step-wise monitoring is often unnecessary, and full-horizon planning with on-demand replanning can offer a more efficient default.

</details>


### [242] [NARRA-Gym for Evaluating Interactive Narrative Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08503)
*Yue Huang,Yuchen Ma,Jiayi Ye,Wenjie Wang,Zipeng Ling,Xingjian Hu,Yuexing Hao,Zichen Chen,Zhangchen Xu,Yunhong He,Zhengqing Yuan,Yujun Zhou,Kehan Guo,Chaoran Chen,Toby Jia-Jun Li,Stefan Feuerriegel,Xiangliang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: NARRA-Gym是一个可执行的交互式叙事评估环境，将稀疏的情感种子转化为完整互动故事，并记录完整模型轨迹。对9个前沿LLM进行评估，发现模型在流畅性之外的鲁棒性、用户体验和个性化适应等维度存在显著差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评测专注于静态提示、孤立故事生成或事后评分，未能评估模型在交互式叙事中联合管理故事生成、长上下文状态与节奏、角色模拟、共情个性化和故事驱动工件的能力。

Method: 构建可执行评估环境NARRA-Gym，将稀疏情感种子扩展为完整互动故事并记录模型全轨迹（故事构建、记忆更新、规划、节奏干预、工件合成）；使用LLM-as-judge对9个前沿LLM在8个基准人设上进行控制评估，并开展人类评估。

Result: 不同模型、人设和评估维度间存在显著差异；能生成流畅故事的模型仍可能在鲁棒性、用户体验或敏感个性化上失败。

Conclusion: 交互式叙事可作为评估LLM长程、用户自适应行为的有效基准，超越了孤立故事质量这一单一维度。

Abstract: Interactive narrative tasks require LLMs to sustain a coherent, evolving story while adapting to a user over multiple turns. However, suitable benchmarks for this setting are limited: existing evaluations often focus on static prompts, isolated story generations, or post-hoc ratings, and therefore miss whether models can jointly manage story generation, long-context state and pacing, character simulation, empathic personalization, and story-grounded artifacts. We introduce NARRA-Gym, an executable evaluation environment that turns a sparse emotional seed into a complete interactive story episode and logs the full model-in-the-loop trajectory, including story construction, memory updates, planning, pacing interventions, and optional artifact synthesis. We evaluate nine frontier LLMs using a controlled LLM-as-judge sweep over eight benchmark personas and a human evaluation in which participants rate customized model outputs. Our results show substantial variation across models, personas, and evaluation dimensions: models that produce fluent stories can still fail on robustness, user experience, or resistance-sensitive personalization. These findings suggest that interactive narrative offers a useful benchmark for evaluating long-horizon, user-adaptive LLM behavior beyond isolated story quality.

</details>


### [243] [A Single Layer to Explain Them All:Understanding Massive Activations in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08504)
*Zeru Shi,Zhenting Wang,Fan Yang,Qifan Wang,Ruixiang Tang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型中大规模激活（massive activations）的起源，发现了一个跨模型家族一致的"大规模涌现层（ME Layer）"，该层首次出现大规模激活并通过残差连接向深层传播。ME层中RMSNorm和FFN参数共同促成了该现象。一旦形成，大规模激活的token表示在层间几乎保持不变，降低了传递给注意力模块的隐藏表示多样性。作者提出了一种简单有效的方法来减少大规模激活token的僵化性，在无训练和微调设置下均提升了指令遵循和数学推理等任务性能。该方法还通过有选择性地削弱注意力沉洞（attention sinks）的影响来缓解该问题，从隐藏状态层面揭示了其起源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示大规模激活现象的根本起源，理解其在LLM中的形成机制和传播路径，以及该现象对模型表示多样性和性能的影响。

Method: 通过跨模型家族的实验定位大规模涌现层（ME Layer），分析RMSNorm和FFN参数的贡献；提出减少大规模激活token僵化性的方法，在训练-free和微调设置下验证效果。

Result: 发现ME Layer中RMSNorm和FFN共同导致大规模激活；大规模激活token表示在层间高度不变；所提方法在指令遵循和数学推理任务上持续提升性能，并能缓解注意力沉洞问题。

Conclusion: 大规模激活起源于特定层的参数协同作用，其僵化性限制了表示多样性；通过削弱其影响可有效提升模型性能并缓解注意力沉洞，为理解和改进LLM提供了新视角。

Abstract: We investigate the origins of massive activations in large language models (LLMs) and identify a specific layer named the \textbf{Massive Emergence Layer (ME Layer)}, that is consistently observed across model families, where massive activations first emerge and subsequently propagate to deeper layers through residual connections. We show that, within the ME Layer both the RMSNorm and the FFN parameters jointly contribute to the emergence of massive activations. Once formed, the massive activation token representation remains largely invariant across layers, reducing the diversity of hidden representations passed to the attention module. Motivated by this limitation, we propose a simple and effective method to reduce the rigidity of the massive activation token. Our approach consistently improves LLM performance across multiple tasks, including instruction following and math reasoning, in both training free and fine tuning settings. Moreover, we show that our method mitigates attention sinks by selectively weakening their influence, elucidating their origin at the hidden state level and shedding new light on principled mitigation strategies.

</details>


### [244] [A Single Neuron Is Sufficient to Bypass Safety Alignment in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08513)
*Hamid Kazemi,Atoosa Chegini,Maria Safi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文发现语言模型的安全对齐依赖于两类独立的神经元系统：控制是否表达有害知识的"拒绝神经元"和编码有害知识本身的"概念神经元"。通过单神经元操作（抑制或放大），无需训练或提示工程即可绕过安全对齐或引发有害输出，表明安全对齐并非鲁棒分布，而是由个别因果充分的神经元调控。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究语言模型安全对齐的底层机制是否真正鲁棒分布，还是依赖于少数关键神经元。

Method: 在7个模型（两大家族，参数规模1.7B到70B）中定位并操作单一种类的拒绝神经元和概念神经元，通过抑制拒绝神经元绕过安全对齐，或通过放大概念神经元从无害提示诱发有害内容。

Result: 单神经元抑制即可绕过安全对齐，单神经元放大即可从无害提示引发有害输出，跨模型、跨参数量级均有效，且无需训练或提示工程。

Conclusion: 语言模型的安全对齐并非鲁棒分布式编码，而是由少数因果充分的个体神经元调控，这揭示了当前安全对齐方法的脆弱性。

Abstract: Safety alignment in language models operates through two mechanistically distinct systems: refusal neurons that gate whether harmful knowledge is expressed, and concept neurons that encode the harmful knowledge itself. By targeting a single neuron in each system, we demonstrate both directions of failure -- bypassing safety on explicit harmful requests via suppression, and inducing harmful content from innocent prompts via amplification -- across seven models spanning two families and 1.7B to 70B parameters, without any training or prompt engineering. Our findings suggest that safety alignment is not robustly distributed across model weights but is mediated by individual neurons that are each causally sufficient to gate refusal behavior -- suppressing any one of the identified refusal neurons bypasses safety alignment across diverse harmful requests.

</details>


### [245] [Coordinates of Capability: A Unified MTMM-Geometric Framework for LLM Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08522)
*Adib Sakhawat,Tahsin Islam,Takia Farhin,Syed Rifat Raiyan,Hasan Mahmud,Md Kamrul Hasan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多特质多方法（MTMM）框架的大语言模型评估系统化知识体系，将九种评估指标统一为几何空间中的测量值，将模型行为分解为三个正交潜变量维度（不稳定性与敏感性、位置与对齐、覆盖与表现力），从而在理论上分离任务无关扰动与真实能力跨度，为鲁棒的基准测试设计提供领域无关的分类法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM评估面临构念效度危机——碎片化基准和临时性指标常将方法变异（如提示敏感性）与真实潜在能力混为一谈，缺乏理论支撑的统一评估框架。

Method: 提出广义MTMM框架，将九种评估指标（Paraphrase Instability、Drift Score、Overton Width、Pluralism Score等）形式化并统一为共享潜变量坐标空间中的几何测量值，通过空间统一将模型行为分解为三个正交潜变量维度。

Result: 建立了一个理论严谨、领域无关的评估分类体系，能够系统分离任务无关扰动与真实能力跨度，提升基准设计的鲁棒性和经验稳定性。

Conclusion: 该框架为LLM评估提供了从碎片化指标到几何统一空间的理论桥梁，有望从根本上解决构念效度问题。

Abstract: The evaluation of Large Language Models (LLMs) faces a critical challenge in construct validity, where fragmented benchmarks and ad hoc metrics frequently conflate method variance, such as prompt sensitivity, with true latent capabilities. Concurrently, emerging research suggests that LLM capabilities and outputs can be modeled as continuous geometric manifolds. In this Systematization of Knowledge (SoK), we bridge these paradigms by proposing a generalized Multi-Trait Multi-Method (MTMM) framework for LLM evaluation. We formalize and unify nine evaluation metrics, including Paraphrase Instability, Drift Score, Overton Width, and Pluralism Score, interpreting them not as isolated scalar values but as geometric measurements within a shared latent coordinate space. This spatial unification factorizes model behavior into three orthogonal latent dimensions: (1) Instability and Sensitivity, (2) Position and Alignment, and (3) Coverage and Expressiveness. By systematically separating task-irrelevant perturbations from true capability spans, the framework provides a theoretically grounded and domain-agnostic taxonomy for robust and empirically stable benchmark design.

</details>


### [246] [100,000+ Movie Reviews from Kazakhstan: Russian, Kazakh, and Code-Switched Texts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08600)
*Rustem Yeshpanov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一个包含100,502条哈萨克斯坦电影评论的多语言公开数据集（主要来自俄语、哈萨克语及语码转换文本），覆盖2001-2025年间的4,943部电影。手工标注语言和情感极性，11,309条评论附带用户评分。在三元极性分类和五类评分分类任务上，比较了传统词袋/TF-IDF基线模型与多语言Transformer模型（mBERT、XLM-RoBERTa、RemBERT），结果表明Transformer在极性分类上显著优于基线，但评分分类因类别不平衡和相邻评分区分困难仍具挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 目前缺乏针对中亚地区（尤其是哈萨克斯坦）电影评论的大规模、多语言、手工标注的情感分析数据集，现有资源多集中于英语或其他主流语言，无法充分覆盖俄语-哈萨克语语码混合等实际场景。

Method: 从kino.kz爬取2001-2025年的100,502条电影评论（4,943部电影），进行语言和情感极性手工标注；定义三元极性分类和五类评分分类任务；基线模型为词袋/TF-IDF + 逻辑回归/线性SVM，对比模型为mBERT、XLM-RoBERTa、RemBERT；采用泄露控制评估（leakage-controlled evaluation）。

Result: Transformer模型在三元极性分类上持续优于传统基线（准确率提升显著）；在五类评分分类任务中，所有模型在泄露控制评估下表现均不理想，主要受限于严重的类别不平衡以及相邻评分等级（如4分与5分）之间的细微语义差异。

Conclusion: 多语言Transformer模型对哈萨克斯坦电影评论的极性分类有效，但评分分级分类仍是开放问题；数据集（kino.kz Reviews）可推进低资源语言和多语言情感分析研究。

Abstract: We present a new publicly available corpus of 100,502 movie reviews from Kazakhstan collected from kino.kz, spanning 2001-2025 and covering 4,943 unique titles. The dataset is multilingual, consisting mainly of Russian reviews alongside Kazakh and code-switched texts. Reviews are manually annotated for language and sentiment polarity, and 11,309 reviews additionally contain explicit user-provided ratings. We define two sentiment tasks -- three-way polarity classification and five-class score classification -- and benchmark classical BoW/TF-IDF baselines against multilingual transformer models (mBERT, XLM-RoBERTa, RemBERT). Experimental results show that transformer models consistently outperform classical baselines on polarity classification, while score classification remains challenging under leakage-controlled evaluation due to severe class imbalance and subtle distinctions between adjacent rating levels.

</details>


### [247] [PARD-2: Target-Aligned Parallel Draft Model for Dual-Mode Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08632)
*Zihao An,Taichi Liu,Ziqiong Liu,Dong Li,Ruofeng Liu,Emad Barsoum*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PARD-2提出了一种双模式投机解码框架，通过置信度自适应令牌优化（CAT）使草稿模型的训练目标与推理时的令牌接受长度对齐，在Llama3.1-8B上实现最高6.94倍无损加速，分别超越EAGLE-3和PARD达1.9倍和1.3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有草稿模型训练目标与推理时最大化连续令牌接受长度的目标不一致，导致投机解码效率受限。

Method: 基于PARD提出PARD-2双模式投机解码框架，引入置信度自适应令牌（CAT）优化，自适应地重新加权每个令牌以更好地对齐验证过程，支持目标依赖和目标独立两种模式。

Result: 在多种模型和任务上，PARD-2实现高达6.94倍无损加速，在Llama3.1-8B上比EAGLE-3快1.9倍，比PARD快1.3倍。

Conclusion: PARD-2通过重新定义草稿模型优化目标和CAT自适应加权，有效对齐训练与推理目标，显著提升投机解码加速效果。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates Large Language Models (LLMs) inference by using a lightweight draft model to propose candidate tokens that are verified in parallel by the target model. However, existing draft model training objectives are not directly aligned with the inference-time goal of maximizing consecutive token acceptance. To address this issue, we reformulate the draft model optimization objective, shifting the focus from token prediction accuracy to the overall acceptance length. In this paper, we build upon PARD to propose PARD-2, a dual-mode speculative decoding framework with Confidence-Adaptive Token (CAT) optimization. This approach adaptively reweights each token to better align with the verification process. Notably, PARD-2 enables a single draft model to support both target-dependent and target-independent modes. Experiments across diverse models and tasks demonstrate that PARD-2 achieves up to 6.94$\times$ lossless acceleration, surpassing EAGLE-3 by 1.9$\times$ and PARD by 1.3$\times$ on Llama3.1-8B. Our code is available at https://github.com/AMD-AGI/PARD.

</details>


### [248] [EdgeFlowerTune: Evaluating Federated LLM Fine-Tuning Under Realistic Edge System Constraints](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08636)
*Jiaxiang Geng,Yiyi Lu,Lunyu Zhao,Yan Gao,Nicholas D. Lane,Bing Luo*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: EdgeFlowerTune是一个面向真实边缘系统约束的联邦LLM微调基准，通过联合评估模型质量与系统开销（通信、延迟、内存、能耗、鲁棒性），并引入三个互补协议（预算下质量、目标成本、鲁棒性），在真实安卓手机和NVIDIA边缘开发板上实验证明，仅基于准确率的评估会产生误导性结论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有联邦LLM微调研究多关注跨孤岛或仿真设置，忽视了资源与运行时约束对真实边缘系统可部署性的影响，缺乏在真实边缘设备上评估实际可行性的基准。

Method: 提出EdgeFlowerTune基准，联合评估模型质量与系统开销（通信、延迟、内存、能耗、对动态边缘条件的鲁棒性）；引入三个互补协议：Quality-under-Budget（预算下质量）、Cost-to-Target（目标成本）和Robustness（鲁棒性）；基于Flower和MobileFineTuner构建真实设备平台，涵盖商业安卓手机和NVIDIA边缘开发板。

Result: 仅基于准确率的评估会产生误导性结论：在考虑真实系统约束后，具有相似最终质量的方法在可部署性上可能存在显著差异。

Conclusion: EdgeFlowerTune为边缘联邦LLM微调提供了可复现的、面向系统感知评估的基准，揭示了在真实边缘约束下评估方法的必要性。

Abstract: Federated fine-tuning offers a promising paradigm for adapting large language models (LLMs) on edge devices by leveraging the rich, diverse, and continuously generated data from smartphones and IoT devices without compromising user data privacy. Such edge-side adaptation can improve model personalization, robustness, and responsiveness to local contexts. However, the practical feasibility of federated LLM fine-tuning on real edge devices remains unclear, as most existing work focuses on cross-silo or simulation-based settings, overlooking the resource and runtime constraints that determine whether a method is deployable on real edge systems. We present EdgeFlowerTune, a deployment-oriented benchmark for federated LLM fine-tuning under realistic edge-system constraints. EdgeFlowerTune jointly evaluates model quality and system costs, including communication, wall-clock latency, memory usage, energy consumption, and robustness to dynamic edge conditions. To compare methods in terms of effectiveness, efficiency, and robustness, EdgeFlowerTune introduces three complementary protocols: Quality-under-Budget, Cost-to-Target, and Robustness. We instantiate EdgeFlowerTune as a real-device platform built on Flower and MobileFineTuner, spanning commercial Android smartphones and NVIDIA edge development boards. Our benchmark results show that accuracy-only evaluation can lead to misleading conclusions: methods with similar final quality may differ substantially in deployability once realistic system constraints are considered. EdgeFlowerTune provides a reproducible benchmark for system-aware evaluation of federated LLM fine-tuning at the edge.

</details>


### [249] [AgentCollabBench: Diagnosing When Good Agents Make Bad Collaborators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08647)
*Aritra Mazumder,Shubhashis Roy Dipta,Nusrat Jahan Lia,Tanzila Khan,Kainat Raisa Hossain,Nehaa Shri,Shubhrangshu Debsarkar,Humayra Tasnim,Gour Gupal Talukder Shawon,Debjoty Mitra,Sumaiya Ahmed Rani,Al Jami Islam Anik,Al Nafeu Khan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出AgentCollabBench基准，诊断多智能体系统中约束静默丢弃、虚假信念传播、上下文泄漏和追踪持久性四类风险，发现通信拓扑是影响信息多跳存亡的主要因素，合流DAG节点存在合成瓶颈会导致少数分支约束被丢弃。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于结果评估的方法无法检测多智能体流水线中约束被静默丢弃等过程故障，需要一种在部署前就能衡量这些漏洞的评估方法。

Method: 构建包含900个人工验证任务的诊断基准AgentCollabBench，隔离四种行为风险（指令衰减、虚假信念传染、上下文泄漏、追踪持久性），评估四种LLM并分析通信拓扑的影响。

Result: 不同模型有特定的漏洞特征；通信拓扑解释了7-40%的信息多跳存亡方差；合流DAG节点存在合成瓶颈，多数投票导致少数分支约束被丢弃。

Conclusion: 多智能体可靠性本质上是结构问题，单纯提升模型智能不能替代架构优化。

Abstract: Multi-agent systems achieve state-of-the-art outcomes through peer collaboration. However, when an agent in the pipeline silently drops a constraint, the system's final output may look correct even though the reasoning chain was quietly corrupted, and existing outcome-based evaluations are blind to such multi-hop process failures. To make these vulnerabilities measurable before deployment, we introduce AgentCollabBench, a diagnostic benchmark of 900 human-validated tasks spanning software engineering, DevOps, and data engineering. Each task isolates one of four behavioral risks: instruction decay (does a constraint survive peer pressure?), false-belief contagion (does a falsehood spread through consensus?), context leakage (does information bleed between tasks?), and tracer durability (does marked data reach the final agent?). Evaluating four modern LLMs (GPT 4.1 mini, Gemini 2.5 Flash Lite, Qwen-3.5-35B-A3B, and Llama 3.1 8B Instruct), we expose model-specific vulnerability profiles invisible to outcome-only evaluation; Qwen-3.5-35B-A3B, for example, leads on tracer durability and instruction stability, while GPT 4.1 mini leads on leakage containment and false-belief resistance. Beyond per-model differences, communication topology emerges as a primary risk factor that explains 7-40% of the variance in multi-hop information survival. The effect traces to a synthesis bottleneck specific to converging-DAG nodes: an agent weighing competing parent inputs discards constraints carried by a minority branch, a bottleneck structurally absent from linear chains. AgentCollabBench demonstrates that suboptimal topology can silently erase the safeguards of highly capable models, arguing that multi-agent reliability is fundamentally a structural problem and that scaling model intelligence alone is no substitute for architecture.

</details>


### [250] [Hint Tuning: Less Data Makes Better Reasoners](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08665)
*Siqi Fan,Minghao Li,Xiaoqian Ma,Xiusheng Huang,Zhuo Chen,Bowen Qin,Liujie Zhang,Shuo Shang,Weihang Chen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Hint Tuning通过将指令模型作为难度探针，自动构建三种提示状态（无提示、稀疏提示、完整提示）的训练数据，仅需1000个自标注样本即可让推理模型根据问题难度动态调整推理深度，在保持精度的同时平均减少31.5%的token生成量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型推理模型在链式思维推理中不论问题难度都生成过多token（比必要量多5-8倍），导致推理开销过大。现有方法依赖大规模蒸馏数据或昂贵的强化学习，效率低下。

Method: 关键洞察：将指令模型作为难度探针。通过测试指令模型在不同引导程度下能否解决问题，自动构建三种状态（无提示、稀疏提示、完整提示）的训练数据。将难度标注这一抽象挑战转化为指令模型与推理模型之间的可度量一致性检查。仅需1000个自标注样本进行训练。

Result: 在多个主流推理模型（Qwen3-Thinking、DeepSeek-R1-Distill）和多种规模（4B-32B）上，实现24%-66%的token减少（平均31.5%），同时在五个基准测试中保持有竞争力的精度。

Conclusion: Hint Tuning通过简单的指令模型能力对齐，而非大规模蒸馏数据或昂贵RL，实现了高效的推理深度校准，是一种数据高效的方法。

Abstract: Large reasoning models achieve high accuracy through extended chain-of-thought but generate 5--8 more tokens than necessary, applying verbose reasoning uniformly regardless of problem difficulty. We propose Hint Tuning, a data-efficient approach that teaches models to calibrate reasoning depth. Our key insight: the corresponding instruct model serves as an ideal difficulty probe. By testing what the instruct model can solve with varying guidance, we automatically construct training data across three states: No-Hint (direct answer), Sparse-Hint (minimal prefix), and Full-Hint (complete reasoning). This converts the abstract challenge of difficulty labeling into a measurable consistency check between the instruct and reasoning models. With only 1K self-annotated samples, Hint Tuning achieves 24--66% token reduction (31.5% average) across mainstream reasoning models (Qwen3-Thinking, DeepSeek-R1-Distill) at multiple scales (4B--32B) while maintaining competitive accuracy on five benchmarks. Unlike methods requiring massive distillation datasets or expensive RL, we achieve superior efficiency through simple alignment with the instruct model's capabilities.

</details>


### [251] [AgentForesight: Online Auditing for Early Failure Prediction in Multi-Agent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08715)
*Boxuan Zhang,Jianing Zhu,Zeru Shi,Dongfang Liu,Ruixiang Tang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出 AgentForesight 框架，将多智能体系统中的错误归因从事后分析转变为在线审计：在轨迹展开的每一步，审计器仅基于当前前缀判断是继续运行还是报警。通过构建 AFTraj-2K 数据集和训练 AgentForesight-7B 模型（采用粗到细的强化学习策略），在多种基准上超越 GPT-4.1 等先进模型，性能提升达 +19.9%，步骤定位误差降低 3 倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作将多智能体系统中的错误归因视为事后分析（post-hoc failure attribution），即在轨迹结束后诊断错误步骤和责任智能体。这种范式在轨迹展开时无法进行任何干预，错失了在部署阶段实时纠正错误的机会。

Method: 1）构建 AFTraj-2K 数据集，包含编码、数学和智能体领域轨迹，通过严格流程保留安全轨迹，并由多个 LLM 评委达成共识标注不安全轨迹的关键错误步骤。2）训练 AgentForesight-7B 在线审计器，采用粗到细的强化学习策略：首先在安全/不安全前缀对的关键边界处赋予风险预期先验，然后通过三轴奖励（what、where、who）将先验精炼为精确的步骤级定位。

Result: 在 AFTraj-2K 和外部 Who&When 基准上，AgentForesight-7B 超越 GPT-4.1 和 DeepSeek-V4-Pro 等领先专有模型，性能提升最高达 +19.9%，步骤定位误差降低 3 倍。

Conclusion: AgentForesight 将错误检测从事后分析转变为在线审计，实现了部署阶段的实时干预，为多智能体系统的可靠性保障提供了新范式。

Abstract: LLM-based multi-agent systems are increasingly deployed on long-horizon tasks, but a single decisive error is often accepted by downstream agents and cascades into trajectory-level failure. Existing work frames this as \emph{post-hoc failure attribution}, diagnosing the responsible agent and step after the trajectory has ended. However, this paradigm forfeits any opportunity to intervene while trajectory is still unfolding. In this work, we introduce AgentForesight, a framework that reframes this problem as online auditing: at each step of an unfolding trajectory, an auditor observes only the current prefix and must either continue the run or alarm at the earliest decisive error, without access to future steps. To this end, we curate AFTraj-2K, a corpus of agentic trajectories across Coding, Math, and Agentic domains, in which safe trajectories are retained under a strict curation pipeline and unsafe trajectories are annotated at the step of their decisive error via consensus among multiple LLM judges. Built on that, we develop AgentForesight-7B, a compact online auditor trained with a coarse-to-fine reinforcement learning recipe that first equips it with a risk-anticipation prior at the failure boundary on adjacent safe/unsafe prefix pairs, then sharpens this prior into precise step-level localization under a three-axis reward jointly targeting the what, where, and who of an audit verdict. Across AFTraj-2K and an external Who\&When benchmark, AgentForesight-7B outperforms leading proprietary models, including GPT-4.1 and DeepSeek-V4-Pro, achieving up to +19.9% performance gain and 3$\times$ lower step localization error, opening the loop from post-hoc failures detection to enabling deployment-time intervention.
