Project page: https://zbox1005.github.io/agent-foresight/

</details>


### [252] [Breaking the Impasse: Dual-Scale Evolutionary Policy Training for Social Language Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08721)
*Minzheng Wang,Run Luo,Yanbo Wang,Zichen Liu,Yuqiao Tan,Tao Tan,Xu Nan,Yinhe Zheng,Wenji Mao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 针对开放型社交语言博弈中因策略空间过大导致RLVR训练陷入演化停滞（策略同质化、梯度消失）的问题，提出双尺度演化策略训练（DEPT），通过时间尺度演化感知机制检测停滞，并利用非对称优势重塑动态调整优化景观，恢复梯度信号并维持策略探索。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RLVR在封闭任务中有效，但扩展到开放型社交语言博弈的自对弈训练时，因策略空间庞大导致智能体收敛到同质化行为，产生确定性对局结果，消除了策略演化所需的梯度信号，陷入演化停滞。

Method: 提出DEPT，包含两个核心组件：1）时间尺度演化感知机制，通过量化双尺度价值基线散度与对局熵来检测演化停滞；2）非对称优势重塑，在检测到崩溃后动态调整优化景观，恢复梯度信号并强制持续策略探索。

Result: 在多个社交语言博弈上的实验表明，DEPT优于强基线方法，有效避免策略退化，驱动社交语言智能体的持续演化。

Conclusion: DEPT通过感知演化停滞并动态干预优化过程，成功解决了开放型社交语言博弈中RLVR训练的策略同质化与梯度消失问题，实现了持续的策略演化。

Abstract: While Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has proven effective for closed-ended tasks, extending it to open-ended social language games via self-play reveals a critical issue: evolution impasse. Due to the vast strategy space, language agents frequently converge to homogenized behaviors, leading to deterministic match outcomes that eliminate the gradient signals necessary for policy evolution. To tackle this issue, we propose Dual-scale Evolutionary Policy Training (DEPT) for social language games. DEPT introduces a time-scaled evolutionary perception mechanism that detects impasse by quantifying dual-scale value baseline divergence alongside match entropy. Upon perceiving the collapse, it then activates asymmetric advantage reshaping to dynamically modulate the optimization landscape for intervention. Thus, our method effectively restores gradient signals and enforces sustained strategic exploration. Extensive experiments on multiple social language games demonstrate that DEPT outperforms strong baselines, avoiding policy degeneration and driving the continuous evolution of social language agents.

</details>


### [253] [Training with Harnesses: On-Policy Harness Self-Distillation for Complex Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08741)
*Zhengyang Zhao,Lu Ma,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出On-Policy Harness Self-Distillation (OPHSD)方法，通过将带推理增强框架的当前模型作为教师进行自蒸馏，将框架的额外监督信号内化到学生模型中，在多种推理任务上显著提升性能，且发现推理时重新附加框架不再带来额外收益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 推理时增强框架（如draft-verify、plan-solve）能显著提升LLM在复杂推理任务上的表现，但不会改变模型本身的内在能力。为了将框架的收益永久性地注入基础模型，而不依赖外部工作流，作者提出OPHSD方法。

Method: OPHSD采用带推理增强框架（harness）的当前模型作为教师，对自身进行自蒸馏。具体地，在文本分类任务上使用draft-verify框架，在数学推理任务上使用plan-solve框架，通过教师模型生成额外监督信号，训练学生模型内化这些能力。

Result: 在多个推理任务上，OPHSD持续优于强基线方法，如在HMMT25数学推理任务上比OPSD提升10.83%。进一步分析表明，推理时重新附加harness不再带来额外收益，甚至可能降低性能。

Conclusion: 复杂的推理增强框架不必作为永久性附件，而可以作为临时训练脚手架，其收益可永久性地反馈到基础模型中。这为将推理时计算收益迁移到模型权重提供了一种有效范式。

Abstract: Inference-time harnesses substantially improve large language models on complex reasoning tasks. However, the intrinsic capabilities of the underlying model remain unchanged by the addition of these external workflows. To bridge this gap, we introduce \emph{On-Policy Harness Self-Distillation} (OPHSD), which employs the harness-augmented current model as a teacher for self-distillation, thereby introducing extra supervisory signals from the harness beyond training data. OPHSD internalizes task-specific harness capabilities into the student model, yielding robust generalizability and strong standalone performance across diverse reasoning tasks. Evaluated across draft--verify harness for text classification and plan--solve for mathematical reasoning tasks, OPHSD consistently outperforms strong baselines (e.g., +10.83\% over OPSD on HMMT25). Our analysis further indicates that reattaching the harness during inference yields no additional benefits and can even degrade performance, suggesting that complex harnesses need not always be permanent fixtures; instead, they can serve as temporary training scaffolds whose benefits are permanently fed back into the base model. Our code and training data are available at https://github.com/zzy1127/OPHSD-On-Policy-Harness-Self-Distillation.

</details>


### [254] [Narrative Landscape: Mapping Narrative Dispositions Across LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08742)
*Donghoon Jung,Jiwoo Choi,Songeun Chae,Seohyon Jung*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出量化框架刻画LLM的"倾向性"，通过一致性（Jaccard相似度）和多样性（逆Simpson指数）两个维度，结合Narrative Landscape可视化方法，揭示不同模型在刚性-探索谱系上的差异及指令类型对选择拓扑结构的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究缺乏对LLM输出中稳定、模型特异的倾向性进行系统性量化的方法，需要建立可比较的框架来刻画不同模型在受控条件下的行为规律。

Method: 采用结构化叙事约束选择任务，在6个前沿模型和3种指令类型下实施；用Jaccard相似度衡量跨复制的一致性，用逆Simpson指数衡量多样性；引入基于PCA的Narrative Landscape可视化将选择映射到共享空间。

Result: 发现模型家族间存在明确的刚性-探索谱系；指令类型会改变选择空间的几何结构，即使标量指标相似，其选择拓扑结构也可能存在质的差异。

Conclusion: 标量指标不足以全面刻画模型行为，定性拓扑分析揭示指令效应与模型特性的深层交互。

Abstract: This study proposes a quantitative framework for profiling LLM dispositions as stable, model-specific regularities in output under repeated, controlled elicitation. Using a structured narrative constraint-selection task administered across six frontier models and three instruction types, we operationalize disposition through two dimensions: "consistency", measured as cross-replication selection overlap via Jaccard similarity, and "diversity", measured as dispersion across options via the inverse Simpson index. We further introduce Narrative Landscape, a PCA-based visualization that maps each model's selection profile into a shared space for direct comparison. Results reveal a clear rigidity-exploration spectrum across model families and show that instruction types shift the geometry of selection spaces even when scalar metrics appear similar, indicating that comparable scores can mask qualitatively distinct selection topologies.

</details>


### [255] [SimReg: Achieving Higher Performance in the Pretraining via Embedding Similarity Regularization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08809)
*Yan Sun,Guoxia Wang,Jinle Zeng,JiaBin Yang,Shuai Li,Li Shen,Dacheng Tao,DianHai Yu,Haifeng Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出SimReg正则化损失，通过增强同标签token的相似性并分离不同标签token，在LLM预训练中加速30%以上收敛并提升1%以上零样本性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM预训练中token嵌入存在高类内方差和类间相似性问题，而基于相似性的正则化在大规模预训练中的应用尚未充分探索。

Method: 提出SimReg损失，包含两项：对同序列内相同标签的token嵌入施加相似性正则化（鼓励接近），对不同标签token施加对比损失（强制分离）。

Result: 在密集和MoE架构上，SimReg持续加速训练收敛超过30%，平均零样本下游性能提升超过1%。

Conclusion: SimReg通过增大多分类间隔实现更高效的分类，消融实验为超参数调优和损失有效性提供了实用见解。

Abstract: Pretraining large language models (LLMs) with next-token prediction has led to remarkable advances, yet the context-dependent nature of token embeddings in such models results in high intra-class variance and inter-class similarity, thus hindering the efficiency of representation learning. While similarity-based regularization has demonstrated benefit in supervised fine-tuning and classification tasks, its application and efficacy in large-scale LLM pretraining remains underexplored. In this work, we propose the SimReg, an embedding similarity regularization loss that explicitly encourages token representations with the same ground-truth label within each sequence to be more similar, while enforcing separation from different-label tokens via a contrastive loss. Our analysis reveals that this mechanism introduces gains by enlarging multi-classification margins, thereby enabling more efficient classification. Extensive experiments across dense and Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) architectures demonstrate that SimReg consistently accelerates training convergence by over 30% and improves average zero-shot downstream performance by over 1% across standard benchmarks. Further ablation studies and analyses offer practical insights into hyperparameter tuning and loss effectiveness.

</details>


### [256] [Generating Leakage-Free Benchmarks for Robust RAG Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08838)
*Jiayi Liu,Jiaxing Zhang,Bowen Jin,Jennifer Neville*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出SeedRG方法，通过半合成基准生成管道缓解RAG评估中的知识泄漏和基准老化问题，确保生成的实例无法仅靠模型参数知识回答。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有RAG基准数据集中许多问题无需检索即可从LLM参数记忆中得到答案，导致评估不可靠；且基准老化（数据被模型吸收）加剧了这一问题。

Method: 从种子基准数据集中提取推理图（reasoning graph）捕捉问题-上下文对的推理结构，通过类型约束的实体替换生成新实例，并经过推理图一致性检查和知识泄漏过滤两步验证确保质量。

Result: 生成的实例在结构上与原数据集相似但内容新颖，不易存在于模型参数知识中，同时保持了原有的推理模式。

Conclusion: SeedRG能有效缓解知识泄漏和基准老化问题，为RAG系统提供更可靠的评估基准。

Abstract: Retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) is widely used to augment large language models (LLMs) with external knowledge. However, many benchmark datasets, designed to test RAG performance, comprise many questions that can already be answered from an LLM's parametric memory. This leads to unreliable evaluation. We refer to this phenomenon as knowledge leakage: cases where RAG tasks are solvable without retrieval. This issue worsens over time due to benchmark aging. As benchmarks are reused for training, their contents are increasingly absorbed into model parameters, making them less effective for evaluating retrieval.
  We introduce SeedRG, a semi-synthetic benchmark generation pipeline that mitigates knowledge leakage and addresses the issue of benchmark aging. Starting from a seed benchmark dataset, SeedRG extracts a reasoning graph from question-context pairs to capture their underlying reasoning structure, and then generates new examples via type-constrained entity replacement. This process produces structurally similar but novel instances that are unlikely to exist in the model's parametric knowledge, while preserving the original reasoning patterns. To ensure quality, we incorporate two verification steps: (1) a reasoning-graph consistency check to maintain task difficulty, and (2) a knowledge-leakage filter to exclude instances answerable without retrieval.

</details>


### [257] [ReST-KV: Robust KV Cache Eviction with Layer-wise Output Reconstruction and Spatial-Temporal Smoothing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08840)
*Yongqi An,Chang Lu,Kuan Zhu,Tao Yu,Chaoyang Zhao,Hong Wu,Ming Tang,Jinqiao Wang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出ReST-KV，一种结合逐层输出重建与时空平滑的鲁棒KV缓存淘汰方法，通过将KV淘汰建模为最小化输出差异的优化问题来捕捉注意力重分布效应，在多个长上下文基准上显著超越现有方法，并实现10.61倍解码延迟降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLMs在长序列推理中KV缓存内存需求急剧增长，现有淘汰方法仅关注高注意力权重的KV对，忽视了token移除引起的注意力重分布效应以及KV选择中的时空动态性。

Method: ReST-KV将KV缓存淘汰建模为优化问题，通过逐层重建最小化输出差异；设计指数移动平均平滑处理时间变化，并采用自适应窗口机制捕获空间模式。

Result: 在LongBench上超越SOTA 2.58%，在RULER上超越15.2%，在Needle-in-a-Haystack和InfiniteBench上持续优于现有方法，128k上下文长度下实现10.61倍解码延迟降低。

Conclusion: ReST-KV通过直接建模token移除对模型输出的影响，自然捕捉注意力重分布效应，结合时空平滑提升鲁棒性，显著推进了长上下文基准的性能。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) face growing challenges in efficient generative inference due to the increasing memory demands of Key-Value (KV) caches, especially for long sequences. Existing eviction methods typically retain KV pairs with high attention weights but overlook the impact of attention redistribution caused by token removal, as well as the spatial-temporal dynamics in KV selection. In this paper, we propose ReST-KV, a robust KV eviction method that combines layer-wise output Reconstruction and Spatial-Temporal smoothing to provide a more comprehensive perspective for the KV cache eviction task. Specifically, ReST-KV formulates KV cache eviction as an optimization problem that minimizes output discrepancies through efficient layer-wise reconstruction. By directly modeling how each token's removal affects the model output, our method naturally captures attention redistribution effects, going beyond simplistic reliance on raw attention weights. To further enhance robustness, we design exponential moving average smoothing to handle temporal variations and an adaptive window-based mechanism to capture spatial patterns. Our method, ReST-KV, significantly advances performance on long-context benchmarks. It surpasses state-of-the-art baselines by 2.58% on LongBench and 15.2% on RULER. Additionally, ReST-KV consistently outperforms existing methods on Needle-in-a-Haystack and InfiniteBench, all while achieving a remarkable 10.61$\times$ reduction in decoding latency at 128k context length. The code is publicly available at https://github.com/an-yongqi/rest-kv to facilitate reproducibility and further research.

</details>


### [258] [XPERT: Expert Knowledge Transfer for Effective Training of Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08842)
*Chang Liu,Boyu Shi,Xu Yang,Xin Geng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出XPERT框架，从预训练的MoE大语言模型中提取、整合并复用跨领域专家知识，以支持不同规模模型的高效训练。实验表明，复用专家知识的模型在语言理解和对话生成任务上表现更强、收敛更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 观察到MoE模型中部分专家在多个知识领域持续激活，编码了跨领域的通用知识且与模型泛化密切相关，因此探索如何识别并复用这些专家知识以改善模型训练效率。

Method: 提出XPERT框架：(1) 通过仅推理分析识别跨领域专家；(2) 利用张量分解精炼专家表征；(3) 将提取的知识适配复用到下游模型中。

Result: 在语言理解和对话生成基准上，复用专家知识的模型相比强基线实现了更优的性能和更快的收敛速度。

Conclusion: MoE大语言模型可作为结构化、可复用的知识源，专家级别的知识复用在提升模型训练方面具有重要价值。

Abstract: Mixture-of-Experts (MoE) language models organize knowledge into explicitly routed expert modules, making expert-level representations traceable and analyzable. By analyzing expert activation patterns in MoE large language models (LLMs), we find that a subset of experts is consistently activated across diverse knowledge domains. These common experts encode cross-domain, generalizable knowledge that is closely related to model generalization, naturally raising the question of how such identifiable expert knowledge can be practically reused. Motivated by this observation, we propose XPERT, a framework that extracts, consolidates, and reuses expert knowledge from pre-trained MoE LLMs to support more effective training of language models across different model scales. XPERT identifies cross-domain experts via inference-only analysis, refines their representations through tensor decomposition, and adapts the extracted knowledge to reuse in downstream models. Experiments on language understanding and dialogue generation benchmarks show that models benefiting from reused expert knowledge achieve consistently stronger performance and faster convergence compared to strong baselines. These results highlight MoE LLMs as structured and reusable knowledge sources, and demonstrate the value of expert-level knowledge reuse for improving model training.

</details>


### [259] [EmoS: A High-Fidelity Multimodal Benchmark for Fine-grained Streaming Emotional Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08847)
*Pengze Guo,Jingxi Liang,Zhiwen Xie,Qifeng Wang,Derek F. Wong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: EmoS是一个高保真双语情感基准数据集，通过严格筛选的静态切片和动态流式独白子集，结合双层人工标注流程，解决了现有基准在生态效度、信号清晰度和细粒度标注上的不足。微调多模态大语言模型后，性能显著优于零样本基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高压力、老龄化社会背景下，大规模情感模型需求迫切，但现有基准无法同时实现生态效度、信号清晰度和可靠的细粒度标注。

Method: 构建EmoS高保真双语基准，包含严格筛选的静态切片和动态流式独白子集，采用双层人工标注流程提供连续情感演化的真实标注。

Result: 在EmoS上微调多模态大语言模型相比零样本基线取得了显著性能提升。

Conclusion: EmoS为未来情感识别和共情模型的训练与评估奠定了基础。

Abstract: In the context of today's high-pressure, aging society, the demand for large-scale emotional models capable of providing empathetic support is more critical than ever. However, existing benchmarks fail to simultaneously achieve ecological validity, signal clarity, and reliable fine-grained labeling. We introduce EmoS, a high-fidelity bilingual benchmark designed to resolve the limitations of ecological validity and noise in existing datasets by combining strictly filtered static slices with a dynamic Streaming Monologue subset. Supported by a rigorous dual-layer human annotation pipeline, EmoS provides trusted ground truth that captures continuous emotional evolution. Empirical results show that fine-tuning MLLMs (multimodal large language models) on EmoS yields significant gains over zero-shot baselines, laying the foundation for the training and evaluation of future emotion recognition models and empathy models. The dataset and code are publicly available at https://github.com/NLP2CT/EmoS.

</details>


### [260] [Architecture, Not Scale: Circuit Localization in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08853)
*Sohan Venkatesh*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文挑战了“模型越大，电路分析越难”的假设，发现分组查询注意力（GQA）比标准多头注意力在相同规模下产生更集中、更稳定的电路，且在某些架构中事实回忆电路会在临界规模以上发生离散相变，表明可解释性难度并非模型规模的固定结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质疑机械可解释性中关于电路分析随模型规模扩大而变难的普遍假设，探究注意力架构而非参数数量对电路可解释性的影响。

Method: 在Pythia和Qwen2.5两个模型家族中，研究间接对象识别、归纳头、事实回忆三种电路类型，比较分组查询注意力（GQA）与标准多头注意力（MHA）在电路集中度和稳定性上的差异。

Result: GQA产生的电路比MHA更集中、更稳定；在Qwen2.5架构内，事实回忆电路在临界规模以上发生离散相变，坍缩为单一瓶颈而非逐渐退化。

Conclusion: 注意力架构对电路可解释性的影响比模型参数规模更大，某些架构选择使大模型更易于研究，可解释性难度并非模型规模的固定函数。

Abstract: Mechanistic interpretability assumes that circuit analysis becomes harder as models scale. We challenge this assumption by showing that the attention architecture matters more than parameter count. Studying three circuit types across Pythia and Qwen2.5, we find that grouped query attention produces circuits that are far more concentrated and mechanistically stable than standard multi-head attention at comparable scales. The same concentration pattern holds across indirect object identification, induction heads, and factual recall. Within a single architecture family (Qwen2.5), factual recall circuits undergo a discrete phase transition above a critical scale, collapsing to a single bottleneck rather than degrading gradually. These findings suggest that some architectural choices make large models more tractable to study and that interpretability difficulty is not a fixed consequence of model size.

</details>


### [261] [Max-pooling Network Revisited: Analyzing the Role of Semantic Probability in Multiple Instance Learning for Hallucination Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08863)
*Shota Fujikawa,Issei Sato*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对混合式幻觉检测方法HaMI计算开销大的问题，通过理论分析发现其内部状态与语义一致性相结合的机制扩大了决策边界。受此启发，提出使用最大池化聚合token级特征、轻量MLP直接估计句子分数的经典分类模型，在省去语义一致性计算的情况下，实现了与SOTA相当的检测性能与显著的计算效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: HaMI等混合幻觉检测方法虽性能优异，但存在重复采样和语义相似性计算带来的巨大计算开销问题，亟需在保持检测性能的同时提升效率。

Method: 首先从决策边界角度对HaMI进行理论分析，揭示其内部状态与语义一致性结合可扩大决策边界；基于此发现，采用最大池化聚合token级特征，并通过轻量MLP直接估计句子分数，无需语义一致性计算。

Result: 在省去语义一致性计算的情况下，实现了与现有SOTA基线方法相竞争的检测性能，同时获得了显著的计算效率提升。

Conclusion: 通过对决策边界的理论洞察，从传统的句子分类模型出发，利用自适应聚合内部特征表示的方式，可以在保证幻觉检测性能的同时大幅降低计算复杂度。

Abstract: Hallucination detection has become increasingly important for improving the reliability of large language models (LLMs). Recently, hybrid approaches such as HaMI, which combine semantic consistency with internal model states via Multiple Instance Learning (MIL), have achieved state-of-the-art performance. However, these methods incur substantial computational overhead due to repeated sampling and costly semantic similarity computations. In this work, we first provide a theoretical analysis of HaMI in terms of decision margins, revealing that scaling internal states with semantic consistency leads to an enlarged decision margin. Motivated by this insight, we revisit classical sentence classification models from a margin enlargement perspective, aggregating token-level features via max pooling and directly estimating sentence scores using a lightweight MLP. Without requiring semantic consistency computations, our approach achieves substantial efficiency improvements while maintaining competitive performance with state-of-the-art baselines through adaptive aggregation of internal feature representations.

</details>


### [262] [DocScope: Benchmarking Verifiable Reasoning for Trustworthy Long-Document Understanding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08888)
*Xiang Feng,Jiawei Zhou,Zhangfeng Huang,Kewei Wang,Shanshan Ye,Jinxin Hu,Zulong Chen,Yong Luo,Jing Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: DocScope是一个评估多模态大模型在长文档问答中结构化推理轨迹的基准，包含1124个问题、四阶段评估协议（页面定位、区域定位、事实抽取、答案验证），实验表明答案准确性不能替代轨迹级评估，完全证据链最高仅29%，区域定位是最薄弱环节。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有评估仅关注端到端答案准确性，无法衡量多模态大模型在长文档视觉密集型内容中是否产生可信、可验证的推理过程，需要更细粒度的评估方法。

Method: 将长文档QA形式化为结构化推理轨迹预测问题，设计四阶段评估协议（页面定位、区域定位、事实抽取、答案验证），通过人类对齐研究校准评估者，构建包含1124个问题和完整层次化标注的数据集。

Result: 回答正确时完整证据链最高仅29%；区域定位是所有模型的最弱环节；主要困难来自跨长距离和多文档簇的证据聚合；幻觉感知和事实抽取是主要能力瓶颈；激活参数量比总参数量更重要。

Conclusion: 仅凭答案准确性不足以评估多模态大模型的长文档推理能力，需要轨迹级评估来全面审计模型在各个阶段的性能，区域定位和证据链完整性是当前模型的关键短板。

Abstract: Evaluating whether Multimodal Large Language Models can produce trustworthy, verifiable reasoning over long, visually rich documents requires evaluation beyond end-to-end answer accuracy. We introduce DocScope, a benchmark that formulates long-document QA as a structured reasoning trajectory prediction problem: given a complete PDF document and a question, the model outputs evidence pages, supporting evidence regions, relevant factual statements, and a final answer. We design a four-stage evaluation protocol -- Page Localization, Region Grounding, Fact Extraction, and Answer Verification -- that audits each level of the trajectory independently through inter-stage decoupling, with all judges selected and calibrated via human alignment studies. DocScope comprises 1,124 questions derived from 273 documents, with all hierarchical evidence annotations completed by human annotators. We benchmark 6 proprietary models, 12 open-weight models, and several domain-specific systems. Our experiments reveal that answer accuracy cannot substitute for trajectory-level evaluation: even among correct answers, the highest observed rate of complete evidence chains is only 29\%. Across all models, region grounding remains the weakest trajectory stage. Furthermore, the primary difficulty stems from aggregating evidence dispersed across long distances and multiple document clusters, while an oracle study identifies faithful perception and fact extraction as the dominant capability bottleneck. Cross-architecture comparisons further suggest that activated parameter count matters more than total scale. The benchmark and code will be publicly released at https://github.com/MiliLab/DocScope.

</details>


### [263] [FragileFlow: Spectral Control of Correct-but-Fragile Predictions for Foundation Model Robustness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08896)
*Zhuoyun Li,Boxuan Wang,Jinwei Hu,Xiaowei Huang,Yi Dong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出FragileFlow，一种通过校准边际缓冲区识别“正确但脆弱”预测并组织类别间错误概率流的正则化方法，从理论和实验上验证了其能提升最差类别鲁棒性并保持干净准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有鲁棒性评估依赖平均准确率或平均一致性，但这些指标会掩盖一种结构性失效模式：预测虽然正确，但概率质量已从真实类别向决策边界附近的系统性错误竞争类别流动。作者希望形式化并解决这一问题。

Method: 形式化定义“边际感知错误流”现象，提出FragileFlow作为即插即用正则化器；利用校准边际缓冲区识别正确但脆弱的预测，将离类概率质量组织成类别级脆弱-风险矩阵；理论上首次给出该边际感知错误流对象的PAC-Bayes上界，证明经验谱控制可在稳定性条件下实现保守的确定性最差类别鲁棒性。

Result: 在多项选择LLM基准和少样本CLIP自适应实验上，FragileFlow相比基线一致提升了面向理论的风险度量，在大多数设置下提升了扰动后的最差类别准确率，并保持干净准确率不受影响。

Conclusion: FragileFlow能有效识别和纠正被平均指标掩盖的结构性脆弱性，是一种兼顾理论和实用的鲁棒性正则化方案。

Abstract: Robust adaptation of LLMs and VLMs is often evaluated by average accuracy or average consistency under perturbations. However, these averages can hide a structured failure mode: a prediction may remain correct while probability mass already flows from particular true classes toward systematic wrong competitors near the decision boundary. In this paper, we formalize this phenomenon as margin-aware error flow and introduce FragileFlow, a plug-in regularizer that uses a calibrated margin buffer to identify correct-but-fragile predictions and organize their off-class probability mass into a class-wise vulnerable-risk matrix. Theoretically, we provide the first PAC-Bayes upper bound for this margin-aware error-flow object, showing how empirical spectral control yields a conservative route to deterministic worst-class robustness under a stability condition. Experiments on multiple-choice LLM benchmarks and few-shot CLIP adaptation show that FragileFlow consistently improves the proposed theory-facing risk measures over matched baselines, yields perturbed worst-class accuracy gains in most settings, and preserves clean accuracy across comparisons.

</details>


### [264] [LLM-Agnostic Semantic Representation Attack](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08898)
*Jiawei Lian,Jianhong Pan,Lefan Wang,Yi Wang,Tairan Huang,Shaohui Mei,Lap-Pui Chau*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出语义表示攻击（SRA），通过将对抗目标从精确文本匹配转向恶意语义表示，结合语义表示启发式搜索（SRHS）算法，在26个开源LLM上实现99.71%的平均攻击成功率，并具有良好的跨模型泛化性和隐蔽性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的基于token级优化的对抗攻击方法存在收敛性差、提示自然度低、跨模型泛化能力弱等瓶颈问题。

Method: 提出SRA框架，从理论上建立语义相干性-收敛关系（Coherence-Convergence Relationship）并推导跨模型语义泛化界；技术上通过SRHS算法在增量式离散token块扩展过程中保持对抗提示的可解释性和结构一致性。

Result: 在26个开源LLM上平均攻击成功率达99.71%，具有强迁移性和隐蔽性。

Conclusion: 语义级别的对抗攻击范式相比token级优化方法在收敛性、自然度和跨模型泛化上具有显著优势，是一种有效的LLM红队评估手段。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) increasingly employ alignment techniques to prevent harmful outputs. Despite these safeguards, attackers can circumvent them by crafting adversarial prompts. Predominant token-level optimization methods primarily rely on optimizing for exact affirmative templates (e.g., ``\textit{Sure, here is...}''). However, these paradigms frequently encounter bottlenecks such as suboptimal convergence, compromised prompt naturalness, and poor cross-model generalization. To address these limitations, we propose Semantic Representation Attack (SRA), a novel LLM-agnostic paradigm that fundamentally reconceptualizes adversarial objectives from exact textual targeting to malicious semantic representations. Theoretically, we establish the semantic Coherence-Convergence Relationship and derive a Cross-Model Semantic Generalization bound, proving that maintaining semantic coherence guarantees both white-box semantic convergence and black-box transferability. Technically, we operationalize this framework via the Semantic Representation Heuristic Search (SRHS) algorithm, which preserves interpretability and structural coherence of the adversarial prompts during incremental discrete token chunk expansion. Extensive evaluations demonstrate that our framework achieves a 99.71% average attack success rate across 26 open-source LLMs, with strong transferability and stealth.

</details>


### [265] [Decomposing and Steering Functional Metacognition in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08942)
*Yanshi Li,Xueru Bai,Shuman Liu,Haibo Zhang,Anxiang Zeng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出大语言模型具有可分解的功能性元认知状态空间（包括评估意识、自我能力评估、感知风险、计算资源分配、受众适应和意图性等内部变量），通过残差流分析证明这些状态可从内部激活中线性解码，并可通过引导激活因果性地调节推理行为（影响冗长性、准确性和安全性响应），为理解与控制模型推理行为提供了机制性框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作已发现LLMs在基准测试中表现出评估上下文感知（evaluation awareness）并扭曲性能测量，但尚不清楚这种现象是单一行为假象还是模型内部存在更深层的结构。

Method: 通过残差流分析（residual stream analysis）在多个推理模型上验证功能性元认知状态可从内部激活中线性解码，并呈现不同的层级分布；进一步通过沿探针导出方向引导模型激活（steering activations），检验各状态对推理行为的因果性调节作用。

Result: 功能性元认知状态是线性可解码的，具有不同的层级分布特征；因果性引导实验表明这些状态可分离地调节冗长性、准确性和安全性响应。

Conclusion: 基准性能不仅反映任务能力，也反映特定功能性元认知状态的激活程度；理解并控制这些内部状态对于可靠评估和部署推理模型至关重要；论文提供了一个研究人工系统中功能性元认知的机制性框架。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) increasingly exhibit behaviors suggesting awareness of their evaluation context, often adapting their reasoning strategies in benchmark settings. Prior work has shown that such evaluation awareness can distort performance measurements; however, it remains unclear whether this phenomenon reflects a single behavioral artifact or a deeper internal structure within the model.
  We propose that LLMs maintain a decomposable space of functional metacognitive states: internal variables encoding factors such as evaluation awareness, self-assessed capability, perceived risk, computational effort allocation, audience expertise adaptation, and intentionality. Through residual stream analysis across multiple reasoning models, we demonstrate that these states are linearly decodable from internal activations and exhibit distinct layer-wise profiles. Moreover, by steering model activations along probe-derived directions, we show that each functional metacognitive state causally modulates reasoning behavior in dissociable ways, affecting verbosity, accuracy, and safety-related responses across tasks.
  Our findings suggest that benchmark performance reflects not only task competence but also the activation of specific functional metacognitive states. We argue that understandi ng and controlling these internal states is essential for reliable evaluation and deployment of reasoning models, and we provide a mechanistic framework for studying functional m etacognition in artificial systems. Our code and data are publicly available at https://github.com/xlands/meta-cognition.

</details>


### [266] [Improving Lexical Difficulty Prediction with Context-Aligned Contrastive Learning and Ridge Ensembling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08950)
*Wicaksono Leksono Muhamad,Joanito Agili Lopo,Tsamarah Rana Nugraha,Ahmad Cahyono Adi,Muhammad Oriza Nurfajri*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种上下文对齐的对比回归方法（Context-Aligned Contrastive Regression），结合岭回归集成与两种互补的对比学习目标，在跨语言词汇难度预测任务上实现了更好的表征对齐、序数结构保持和性能稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有词汇难度预测方法仅使用标量监督的回归训练，未能显式结构化表征空间，限制了模型捕捉跨语言对齐和序数难度的能力。

Method: 提出Context-Aligned Contrastive Regression，集成岭回归（Ridge Regression ensemble）与两个互补目标：Cross-View Context（跨视角上下文）和Ordinal Soft Contrastive Learning（序数软对比学习）。

Result: 在三个L1数据集上的实验表明：(i)对比目标改善了跨语言表征对齐同时保留语言特定细微差别，(ii)学习到的表征捕捉了词汇难度的序数结构，(iii)集成方法有效缓解了单个模型的系统性偏差，在不同难度级别上表现更稳定。

Conclusion: 对比学习和集成策略能够有效提升跨语言词汇难度预测的表征质量和泛化稳定性。

Abstract: Lexical difficulty prediction is a fundamental problem in language learning and readability assessment, requiring models to estimate word difficulty across different first-language (L1) backgrounds. However, existing approaches rely on regression-only training with scalar supervision, which does not explicitly structure the representation space, limiting their ability to capture cross-lingual alignment and ordinal difficulty. To mitigate these issues, we propose Context-Aligned Contrastive Regression, which integrates Ridge regression ensemble with two complementary objectives, i.e., Cross-View Context and Ordinal Soft Contrastive Learning. Experiments on three L1 datasets show that (i) contrastive objectives improve cross-lingual representation alignment while preserving language-specific nuances, (ii) the learned representations capture the ordinal structure of lexical difficulty, and (iii) the ensemble effectively mitigates systematic biases of individual models, leading to more stable performance across difficulty levels.

</details>


### [267] [Dolphin-CN-Dialect: Where Chinese Dialects Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.08961)
*Yangyang Meng,Huihang Zhong,Guodong Lin,Guanbo Wang,Hu Du,Zhiming Shao,Yukai Huang,Ke Li,Wei-Qiang Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了Dolphin-CN-Dialect，一个面向中文及方言场景的流式ASR模型。通过温度采样的数据平衡策略、针对中英文混合的语言特性分词器设计以及训练稳定性和数据处理的多项改进，该模型在方言识别准确率和词错误率（CER）上显著优于前代版本Dolphin，并以更小的模型尺寸达到了与最新开源SOTA模型相竞争的性能，同时支持流式/非流式推理、热词定制和硬件优化部署。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ASR模型在处理中文方言（尤其是低资源方言）时面临数据高度不平衡的挑战，同时流式场景下需要在延迟和准确率之间取得平衡。为了解决方言识别性能差、模型过大不利于部署等问题，提出一个兼顾流式能力、方言识别精度和部署效率的实用解决方案。

Method: 1) 采用基于温度的采样策略（temperature-based sampling）来平衡标准普通话与低资源方言的训练数据比例，缓解数据不平衡问题；2) 重新设计分词器：中文采用字符级建模，英文采用子词建模，并引入可扩展的方言专用token；3) 在数据处理、训练稳定性和数据采样策略上对前代Dolphin进行了系统改进；4) 支持流式与非流式两种推理模式，并集成热词支持和专用硬件优化。

Result: 实验结果表明，相比Dolphin，Dolphin-CN-Dialect在方言识别准确率和词错误率（CER）上均有显著提升；与最新的开源SOTA模型相比，在保持更小模型尺寸的前提下达到了具有竞争力的性能。

Conclusion: Dolphin-CN-Dialect通过系统性的技术创新，成功解决了中文方言ASR中的数据不平衡和建模难题，在方言识别、流式/非流式推理、模型轻量化和部署灵活性方面均取得了优秀表现，为实际多方言ASR应用提供了一个高效实用的解决方案。

Abstract: We present Dolphin-CN-Dialect, a streaming-capable ASR model with a focus on Chinese and dialect-rich scenarios. Compared to the previous version, Dolphin-CN-Dialect introduces substantial improvements in data processing, tokenization, training stability, and data sampling strategies. To address the challenges of highly imbalanced dialect data, we propose a temperature-based sampling strategy that effectively balances standard Mandarin and low-resource dialects, leading to significant gains in dialect recognition performance. In addition, we redesign the tokenizer to better align with linguistic characteristics, adopting character-level modeling for Chinese and subword modeling for English, while introducing extensible dialect tokens. Experimental results show that Dolphin-CN-Dialect achieves improvement in dialect recognition accuracy and CER reduction compared to Dolphin. Furthermore, Dolphin-CN-Dialect reaches competitive performance with recent SOTA open-source ASR models, while maintaining a significantly smaller model size. Dolphin-CN-Dialect supports both streaming and non-streaming inference, enabling a practical balance between latency and accuracy. It also provides flexible customization through hotword support and efficient deployment optimized for specialized hardware. These improvements make Dolphin-CN-Dialect a strong and practical solution for real-world multi-dialect ASR applications.

</details>


### [268] [LLiMba: Sardinian on a Single GPU -- Adapting a 3B Language Model to a Vanishing Romance Language](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09015)
*Luca Ballore*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了LLiMba，一个基于Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct通过持续预训练和监督微调得到的3B参数撒丁语模型。在单张24GB消费级GPU上训练，使用1150万撒丁语token和240万相关罗曼语token。rsLoRA r256配置在英译撒丁语方向达到28.5 BLEU，优于全参数微调的21.0 BLEU。研究还发现适配器容量比LoRA变体选择更重要，且翻译指标无法完全反映质量差异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 撒丁语作为一种拥有约100万使用者的罗曼语言，在现代NLP中几乎没有存在感——商业服务不支持，现有语言模型无法可靠生成。为解决这一低资源语言在NLP领域的缺失问题，作者旨在开发一个能有效处理撒丁语的适配模型。

Method: 基于Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct，通过持续预训练(CPT)和监督微调(SFT)在单张24GB消费级GPU上训练LLiMba。CPT使用1150万撒丁语token（涵盖LSC、Logudorese和Campidanese三种变体）和240万相关罗曼语文本。SFT比较了五种配置：全参数微调、LoRA r64、rsLoRA r128、rsLoRA r256和DoRA r256。

Result: CPT后模型在保留撒丁语测试集上达到6.76的困惑度，在全部6个FLORES-200方向上优于基础模型。rsLoRA r256在英译撒丁语方向达到28.5 BLEU，远高于CPT后的17.3和全参数微调的21.0。但高BLEU指标可能掩盖问题，如r128出现了跨脚本泄漏，LoRA r64产生更多虚构内容，DoRA r256事实准确性最差。

Conclusion: 对于将罗曼语预训练基座适配到低资源罗曼语目标，适配器容量比LoRA变体选择更重要；更强的正则化并不总是有益的；翻译指标可能无法完全反映分类不同的质量差异。此外，跨脚本困惑度比较需考虑字节回退分词带来的指标偏差。

Abstract: Sardinian, a Romance language with roughly one million speakers, has minimal presence in modern NLP. Commercial services do not support it, and current language models do not produce it reliably. We present LLiMba, a 3B parameter Sardinian-ready model adapted from Qwen2.5-3B-Instruct through continued pretraining (CPT) and supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on a single 24 GB consumer GPU. The corpus contains 11.5 million tokens of Sardinian spanning LSC, Logudorese, and Campidanese, augmented with 2.4 million tokens of related Romance text as replay against register blurring. After CPT the model reaches a perplexity of 6.76 on held out Sardinian and outperforms the base across all six FLORES-200 directions. We compare five SFT configurations under matched conditions: full fine-tuning, LoRA r64, rsLoRA r128, rsLoRA r256, and DoRA r256. rsLoRA r256 wins on every direction into Sardinian, reaching 28.5 BLEU from English against 17.3 after CPT and 21.0 with full fine-tuning. The rank ablation places r128 between LoRA r64 and rsLoRA r256 on BLEU but reveals failure modes invisible to the metric, including leakage across scripts no other variant produces. LoRA r64 retains less factual content from SFT than configurations at higher rank and produces more confident fabrications, though all methods fabricate on content absent from training. DoRA r256 yields the smallest gap between training and evaluation but the worst factual accuracy. The findings indicate that adapter capacity matters more than the choice among LoRA variants for adapting a Romance pretrained base to a low resource Romance target, that stronger regularization is not uniformly beneficial, and that translation metrics smoothly order configurations whose qualitative behavior differs categorically. Perplexity comparisons across scripts must account for byte fallback tokenization, which deflates the metric for scripts other than Latin.

</details>


### [269] [GAMBIT: A Three-Mode Benchmark for Adversarial Robustness in Multi-Agent LLM Collectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09027)
*Alexandre Le Mercier,Chris Develder,Thomas Demeester*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: GAMBIT是一个多智能体系统中的欺骗检测基准，包含27,804个标注样本和240种共演化的欺骗策略，通过三种评估模式（零样本检测、分布偏移检测、少样本重校准）评估检测器性能。研究发现零样本评估具有误导性，而元学习变体在重校准模式下收敛速度快20倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有对抗性研究局限于浅层任务，未考虑自适应对手（会进化策略以逃避检测器），因此需要更全面的基准来评估多智能体系统中的欺骗检测器。

Method: 以国际象棋为深度推理任务，使用Gemini 3.1 Pro构建智能体，通过进化框架生成自适应欺骗智能体，建立27,804个标注样本的基准，包含三种评估模式（零样本、分布偏移、重校准）和两个独立评分。

Result: 自适应欺骗智能体可将集体任务性能降至零，同时基本保持不可检测（基于Gemini的检测器F1仅50.5%）；零样本得分相近的两检测器在少样本适应上相差8倍；元学习变体重校准速度快20倍。

Conclusion: GAMBIT是首个对抗攻击与防御共演化的多智能体基准，证明零样本评估对自适应对手具有误导性，并展示了快速重校准技术的有效性，欺骗框架可泛化到国际象棋以外的任务。

Abstract: In multi-agent systems (MAS), a single deceptive agent can nullify all gains of an agentic AI collective and evade deployed defenses. However, existing adversarial studies on MAS target only shallow tasks and do not consider adaptive adversaries, which evolve their strategies to evade the very detectors trained to catch them. To address that gap, we introduce GAMBIT, a benchmark with three evaluation modes and two independent scores for evaluating imposter detectors: the first two modes measure zero-shot detection under increasing distribution shift, and a third recalibration mode measures how quickly a detector adapts to novel attacks from just 20 labeled examples. The benchmark comes with a dataset of 27,804 labeled instances spanning 240 co-evolved imposter strategies. Our contributions are threefold: (1) Using chess as a substrate deep reasoning problem and Gemini 3.1 Pro for agents, we release GAMBIT and its dataset to evaluate imposter detectors under realistic constraints against a stealthy adaptive imposter; (2) We introduce an adaptive imposter agent based on an efficient evolutionary framework, generalizable beyond chess, that collapses collective task performance while remaining essentially undetectable (50.5% F1-score with a Gemini-based detector); (3) We show that zero-shot evaluation can be highly misleading for adaptive adversaries: two detectors with near-identical zero-shot scores differ by 8x on few-shot adaptation, while the meta-learned variant converges 20x faster, a gap only visible in the recalibration mode. Altogether, GAMBIT provides the first multi-agent benchmark where adversarial attacks and defenses co-evolve, with an imposter framework generalizable beyond our use case, and promising techniques for fast recalibration in a rapidly evolving adversarial system. Code and data: https://anonymous.4open.science/r/gambit.

</details>


### [270] [BiAxisAudit: A Novel Framework to Evaluate LLM Bias Across Prompt Sensitivity and Response-Layer Divergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09041)
*Jialing Gan,Junhao Dong,Songze Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 现有大语言模型偏见审计因设计缺陷（单一提示格式和表面标签）导致两大失效模式：跨提示格式变化使偏见评分波动超0.7，以及响应内选择与阐述层立场对立造成"抵消陷阱"。本文提出双轴审计协议BiAxisAudit，通过跨提示因子网格和响应内拆分编码分别测量分布性偏见和分层不一致性，实验表明任务格式解释方差与模型选择相当，63.6%的偏见信号仅出现在单一编码层。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有偏见基准将偏见压缩为单一标量，忽略了两个可在不改变模型权重下被利用的失效模式：跨提示的格式变化和响应内部分层立场对立，导致基准可靠性成为安全隐患。

Method: 提出BiAxisAudit协议：跨提示轴采用任务格式、视角、角色、情感四因素因子网格，将偏见视为分布而非点估计；响应内轴使用Split Coding分离选择（Selection）和阐述（Elaboration）信号，通过不一致率（IR）和分歧净不平衡（DNI）测量。

Result: 在8个LLM上各收集80,200条编码响应，任务格式解释方差与模型选择相当；63.6%的池化偏见信号（每模型最高85.2%）仅出现在一个编码层；提示维度交互效应超过主效应；选择与阐述排名几乎不相关（ρ=0.238, p=0.570）。

Conclusion: 当前偏见审计方法因单一标量聚合掩盖了严重的跨提示和跨层不一致性，BiAxisAudit能区分真正的偏见减少与由跨层重新分布造成的表面减少，为偏见治理提供了更可靠的评估框架。

Abstract: Bias audits of large language models now operate within governance frameworks such as the EU AI Act, making benchmark reliability a security concern in its own right. Many current benchmarks, however, collapse bias into a single scalar from one prompt format and one surface label. This design misses two failure modes that can be exploited without changing model weights. Across prompts, meaning-preserving format changes shift bias endorsement by more than $0.7$ on a fixed statement pool. Within a response, the discrete Selection and free-text Elaboration can take opposing stances, so an apparently clean aggregate may hide substantial internal inconsistency (a ``cancellation trap''). Selection-only and elaboration-only rankings are therefore nearly uncorrelated across eight LLMs (Spearman $ρ= 0.238$, $p = 0.570$): LLaMA3-70B ranks in the middle under selection-only scoring but highest under elaboration-only scoring on the same responses. We introduce \textsc{BiAxisAudit}, a protocol that reports each bias score together with a reliability estimate on two orthogonal axes. The across-prompt axis evaluates each statement under a factorial grid of task format, perspective, role, and sentiment, treating bias as a distribution rather than a point estimate. The within-response axis uses Split Coding to recover Selection and Elaboration as separate signals, measured by the Inconsistency Rate and Divergence Net Imbalance. Across eight LLMs with $80{,}200$ coded responses each, task format alone explains as much variance as model choice; $63.6\%$ of pooled bias signals (up to $85.2\%$ per model) appear in only one coding layer, and prompt-dimension interactions exceed main effects. The instrument also separates real bias reductions from apparent reductions caused by cross-layer redistribution: some prompt configurations reduce both BER and IR, whereas others suppress only selection-layer bias.

</details>


### [271] [Evaluating Pragmatic Reasoning in Large Language Models: Evidence from Scalar Diversity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09042)
*Ye-eun Cho*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究通过标量多样性（scalar diversity）诊断任务，比较了直接概率测量与元语言提示两种评估方法在LLM语用推理上的表现，发现二者均不稳定，语用行为取决于模型家族、提示策略和任务结构的交互，而非单一评估范式所能捕捉的稳定能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM语用推理评估存在方法依赖性问题，基于提示的判断与模型内部概率分布可能存在分歧，使得观测到的性能究竟是内在能力还是任务诱导行为难以区分。

Method: 沿用Hu & Levy (2023)的范式，采用标量多样性作为渐进式诊断工具，在多模型、多实验设置下对比直接概率测量与元语言提示两种评估方法。

Result: 两种评估方法并无一致的优劣之分；语用行为在模型家族、提示策略和任务结构间差异显著；标量多样性梯度仅出现在特定的模型-条件组合中。

Conclusion: LLM的语用推理是内部概率表征与任务诱导行为交互的产物，而非单一评估范式所能稳定的能力；评估设计的选择对理解LLM语用能力至关重要。

Abstract: Evaluating pragmatic reasoning in large language models (LLMs) remains challenging because model behavior can vary depending on evaluation methods. Previous studies suggest that prompt-based judgments may diverge from models' internal probability distributions, raising questions about whether observed performance reflects underlying competence or task-induced behavior. This study examines this issue using scalar diversity as a graded diagnostic for pragmatic inference. Following Hu & Levy (2023), this study compares direct probability measurement and metalinguistic prompting across multiple models and experimental settings. The results show that neither evaluation method consistently outperforms the other and that pragmatic behavior varies substantially across model families, prompting strategies, and task structures. Moreover, scalar diversity gradients emerge only in specific model-condition combinations, suggesting that pragmatic reasoning in LLMs reflects an interaction between internal probabilistic representations and task-induced prompting behavior rather than a stable competence captured by a single evaluation paradigm. These findings highlight the central role of evaluation design in interpreting pragmatic abilities in LLMs.

</details>


### [272] [Phase Transitions in Affective Meaning Divergence: The Hidden Drift Before the Break](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09043)
*Napassorn Litchiowong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出情感意义分歧（AMD）形式化框架，通过总变差距离量化对话中情感理解的错位，并证明其在博弈论动力系统中会经历鞍结分岔（当βα>4时），导致修复协调的突然崩溃。在CGA-Wiki数据集上，脱离轨道的对话在词汇分歧方差、AMD方差和对话行为修复方差上均表现出临界减速（CSD）信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话中一方说"Fine"表示"解决"，另一方却理解为"屈服"——词语共享但情感理解不一致。现有研究缺乏对这种情感意义分歧的形式化量化方法及其对对话协调崩溃的动力学机制解释。

Method: 基于言语行为理论、共同语境积累和熵正则化博弈论，推导出logit最优响应映射。通过动力系统分析证明该映射在βα>4时经历鞍结分岔（saddle-node bifurcation），AMD驱动的负荷单调增加会导致修复协调发生滞后性的突然崩溃。在CGA-Wiki（N=652）和CGA-CMV（N=1,169）数据集上验证。

Result: 脱轨对话在三个层面表现出临界减速（CSD）特征：词汇分歧方差（p<0.001，d=0.36）、AMD方差（p=0.001，d=0.26）、对话行为修复方差（p=0.016，d=0.20），均经多重比较校正显著。AMD的时间特征独特：回顾性测量的方差在分岔点达到峰值，而毒性方差峰值出现在更早阶段。AMD是唯一基于理论框架的指标。边界条件分析结果混合但方向一致。

Conclusion: 情感意义分歧（AMD）是对话崩溃的早期预警信号，其理论基础优于毒性等经验基线指标。AMD方差可在分岔点提供独特的预警时间特征，为检测和干预对话走向恶化提供理论驱动的量化工具。

Abstract: One partner says "Fine" meaning <i>resolution</i>; the other hears <i>surrender</i>. The word is shared; the affective uptake is not. We formalize this as <b>affective meaning divergence (AMD)</b>, the total-variation distance between interlocutors' anchor-conditioned affect distributions. Building on speech-act theory, common-ground accumulation, and entropy-regularized game theory, we derive a logit best-response map whose dynamics undergo a saddle-node bifurcation: when $βα> 4$, a monotone increase in AMD-driven load produces an abrupt, hysteretic collapse of repair coordination. On Conversations Gone Awry (CGA-Wiki; $N=652$), derailing conversations exhibit critical-slowing-down (CSD) signatures across multiple levels: lexical divergence variance ($p<0.001$, $d=0.36$), AMD variance ($p=0.001$, $d=0.26$), and dialog-act repair variance ($p=0.016$, $d=0.20$), all significant after correction and stronger than toxicity and sentiment baselines. AMD provides a distinct temporal signature, with retrospectively measured variance peaking at the bifurcation point while toxicity variance peaks earlier, and is the only indicator grounded in the theoretical framework. Boundary-condition analysis on CGA-CMV ($N=1{,}169$) yields mixed but directionally consistent evidence.

</details>


### [273] [Soohak: A Mathematician-Curated Benchmark for Evaluating Research-level Math Capabilities of LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09063)
*Guijin Son,Seungone Kim,Catherine Arnett,Hyunwoo Ko,Hyein Lee,Hyeonah Kang,Jiang Longxi,Jin Yun,JungYup Lee,Kyungmin Lee,Sam Yoosuk Kim,Sang Park,Seunghyeok Hong,SeungJae Lee,Seungyeop Yi,Shinae Shin,SunHye Bok,Sunyoung Shin,Yonghoon Ji,Youngtaek Kim,Hanearl Jung,Akari Asai,Graham Neubig,Sean Welleck,Youngjae Yu,Akshelin R,Alexander B. Ivanov,Boboev Muhammadjon,Chaeyoung Han,Christian Stump,Dmitrii Karp,Dohyun Kwon,DoYong Kwon,Duk-Soon Oh,Giovanni Resta,Greta Panova,Huiyun Noh,Hyungryul Baik,Hyungsun Bae,Inomov Mashrafdzhon,Jeewon Kim,Ji Eun Lee,Jiaqi Liu,Jieui Kang,Jimin Kim,Jon-Lark Kim,Junseo Yoon,Junwoo Jo,Kibeom Kim,Kiwoon Kwon,Mario Kummer,Max Mercer,Minjun Kim,Nahyun Lee,Ng Ze-An,Rafał Marcin Łochowski,Raphaël Lachièze-Rey,Ruichen Zhang,Sejin Park,Seonguk Seo,Shin Jaehoon,Sunatullo,Taewoong Eom,Yeachan Park,Yongseok Jang,Youchan Oh,Zhaoyang Wang,Zoltán Kovács*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: Soohak 是一个由64位数学家从头编写的439道研究级数学难题基准集，前沿模型在其Challenge子集上正确率最高仅30.4%，且所有模型在需识别病态问题并拒绝回答的Refusal子集上正确率均低于50%，揭示了当前LLM在高级数学推理中的显著局限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 继前沿LLM在国际数学奥林匹克(IMO)上实现金牌级表现后，社区需要更有意义的推理衡量目标。奥林匹克式问题仅衡量逐步推理，而研究级问题需运用推理推进数学知识前沿本身，但研究级数学基准因问题来源稀缺（如Riemann Bench仅25题、FrontierMath-Tier 4仅50题）而严重匮乏，亟需一个更大规模、更可靠的评估基准。

Method: 由64位数学家从头编写了包含439道研究级数学问题的基准集Soohak，分为Challenge子集（标准解题）和Refusal子集（识别病态问题并拒绝回答）。评估了包括Gemini-3-Pro、GPT-5、Claude-Opus-4.5、Qwen3-235B、GPT-OSS-120B、Kimi-2.5等多种前沿及开源模型。

Result: Challenge子集上，Gemini-3-Pro达30.4%、GPT-5达26.4%、Claude-Opus-4.5达10.4%，开源模型均低于15%。Refusal子集上，所有模型正确率均不超过50%。数据集将于2026年底公开，评估结果可提前申请获取。

Conclusion: Soohak展示了当前最先进模型在研究级数学推理上的巨大提升空间（尤其是开源模型），并首次将"拒绝回答（识别病态问题）"作为一个独立的优化目标提出，当前模型对此缺乏针对性训练，这为下一代模型提供了新的评估方向和发展目标。

Abstract: Following the recent achievement of gold-medal performance on the IMO by frontier LLMs, the community is searching for the next meaningful and challenging target for measuring LLM reasoning. Whereas olympiad-style problems measure step-by-step reasoning alone, research-level problems use such reasoning to advance the frontier of mathematical knowledge itself, emerging as a compelling alternative. Yet research-level math benchmarks remain scarce because such problems are difficult to source (e.g., Riemann Bench and FrontierMath-Tier 4 contain 25 and 50 problems, respectively). To support reliable evaluation of next-generation frontier models, we introduce Soohak, a 439-problem benchmark newly authored from scratch by 64 mathematicians. Soohak comprises two subsets. On the Challenge subset, frontier models including Gemini-3-Pro, GPT-5, and Claude-Opus-4.5 reach 30.4%, 26.4%, and 10.4% respectively, leaving substantial headroom, while leading open-weight models such as Qwen3-235B, GPT-OSS-120B, and Kimi-2.5 remain below 15%. Notably, beyond standard problem solving, Soohak introduces a refusal subset that probes a capability intrinsic to research mathematics: recognizing ill-posed problems and pausing rather than producing confident but unjustified answers. On this subset, no model exceeds 50%, identifying refusal as a new optimization target that current models do not directly address. To prevent contamination, the dataset will be publicly released in late 2026, with model evaluations available upon request in the interim.

</details>


### [274] [Dynamic Meta-Metrics: Source-Sentence Conditioned Weighting for MT Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09098)
*Luke Zhang,Justin Vasselli,Aditya Khan,York Hay Ng,En-Shiun Annie Lee*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出动态元指标(DMM)框架，通过学习源句条件化的指标组合来动态适配机器翻译评估，实验表明MLP组合和软条件化优于线性模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统机器翻译评估依赖静态集成或语言特定权重，无法根据源句特性自适应调整指标组合，缺乏灵活性。

Method: 提出DMM框架，包含硬条件化（按聚类训练可解释组合器）和软条件化扩展（权重随源句-聚类隶属度连续变化），使用MLP、线性模型和高斯过程进行组合。

Result: MLP组合在多个语言对的系统和段落级一致度量上优于线性和高斯过程集成；软条件化进一步带来增益。

Conclusion: DMM通过源句自适应组合有效提升翻译评估性能，软条件化扩展更具潜力。

Abstract: We propose Dynamic Meta-Metrics (DMM), a framework for machine translation evaluation that learns source-sentence conditioned combinations of existing metrics. Rather than relying on a single static ensemble or language-specific weighting, DMM adapts the metric combination based on properties of the source segment. We study hard conditioning, which fits an interpretable combiner per cluster, and an exploratory soft-conditioned extension whose weights vary continuously with source-cluster responsibilities. We evaluate DMM on the WMT Metrics Shared Task data across multiple language pairs using pairwise agreement measures at the system and segment levels. Across settings, MLP-based combinations outperform linear and Gaussian process-based ensembles, and introducing soft conditioning yields gains over linear models.

</details>


### [275] [GRC: Unifying Reasoning-Driven Generation, Retrieval and Compression](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09100)
*Zhongtao Miao,Qiyu Wu,Yoshimasa Tsuruoka*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出GRC框架，通过元隐层令牌和统一训练方法，将推理驱动生成、推理增强文本表示和上下文压缩三个任务融合到LLM的单次前向传播中，实现了模块化灵活推理、高效部署和数据利用率提升，并提出了自推理隐层嵌入和隐层记忆增强生成等新范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前文本嵌入和生成任务基于LLM分开训练，导致训练成本高、部署开销大；上下文压缩对推理驱动生成和智能体任务至关重要但仍具挑战性。作者旨在探索如何在LLM单次前向传播中统一这三个任务，以降低RAG部署成本，实现高效推理。

Method: 提出GRC训练框架，通过元隐层令牌（meta latent tokens）和统一的生成式、表示式与压缩式调优方法，将三个任务桥接。模型在单次前向传播中完成三个目标，推理时保持模块化的LEGO风格灵活性。还提出混合分页注意力机制加速推理。

Result: 在推理密集型检索基准、生成任务、文档压缩、延迟评估和RAG设置上的广泛实验验证了方法的有效性。训练时数据利用率提升三倍，推理时实现高效计算，压缩后的KV缓存长度为O(1)作为可更新记忆。

Conclusion: GRC框架开启了文本嵌入的新范式（自推理隐层嵌入）和生成新范式（隐层记忆增强生成），为真正统一的模型奠定了方向，可无缝处理推理驱动生成、嵌入和压缩任务。

Abstract: Text embedding and generative tasks are usually trained separately based on large language models (LLMs) nowadays. This causes a large amount of training cost and deployment effort. Context compression is also a challenging and pressing task, which is vital to reasoning-driven generation, and agentic tasks requiring long context and continual learning. In this paper, we explore how to unify reasoning-driven generation, reasoning-enhanced text representation and context compression tasks in one forward pass for LLMs. Through meta latent tokens and a unified generative, representative and compressive tuning approach, we propose a training framework named GRC that bridges the three tasks. The trained models can accomplish three objectives in a single forward pass while maintaining modular, LEGO-style flexibility during inference. This design greatly reduces the deployment effort for retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) and achieves efficient inference and three times data utilization during training. Furthermore, this framework design enables a new paradigm for text embedding: self-reason-latent embeds, and a new generation paradigm, latent memory-augmented generation, where compressed and internalized KV cache with O(1) length is used as the updatable memory. We also propose hybrid paged attention to speed up the inference of our models. Extensive experiments on reasoning-intensive retrieval benchmarks, generative tasks, document compression, latency evaluation, and RAG settings demonstrate the effectiveness of our method and may shed light on the truly unified model that can handle reasoning-driven generation, embedding and compression tasks seamlessly.

</details>


### [276] [Fin-Bias: Comprehensive Evaluation for LLM Decision-Making under human bias in Finance Domain](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09106)
*Xiaoyu Hu,Jinman Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Fin-Bias基准，用于评估大语言模型在金融语境中面对不确定性及人类偏见意见时的投资决策能力。实验表明LLM倾向于跟随上下文中的显式偏见，并开发了一种检测人类意见的方法，可促使LLM独立思考，部分模型在预测未来股票收益上超越人类表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有金融基准测试多局限于小样本，未能考察LLM在不确定语境下受人类偏见影响的程度，亟需一个系统性的评估框架。

Method: 构建包含8868份长篇幅分析师报告的Fin-Bias数据集，涵盖多行业、多评级（看涨/中性/看跌）；通过向LLM呈现带/不带分析师评级甚至虚假评级的报告，评估其投资决策中的从众行为；并设计检测人类意见的方法以提升模型独立思考能力。

Result: LLM倾向于跟随上下文中的显式偏见（从众效应）；提出的检测方法可有效促使模型独立判断，部分模型在预测未来股票收益上超越人类分析师表现。

Conclusion: LLM在金融决策中存在显著的从众偏差，但通过适当的检测与干预机制可增强其独立性，甚至实现超越人类的表现。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly deployed in financial contexts, raising critical concerns about reliability, alignment, and susceptibility to adversarial manipulation. While prior finance-related benchmarks assess LLMs' capabilities in stock trading, they are often restricted to small sample and fail to demonstrate LLM susceptibility to context with potential human bias. We introduce Fin-Bias (financial herding under long and uncertain financial context), a benchmark for evaluating LLM investment decision-making when faced with uncertainty and possible human-biased opinions. Fin-Bias includes 8868 long firm-specific analyst reports, including firm aspects summarized and analyzed by sophisticated analysts with investment ratings (Bullish/Neutral/Bearish) spanning from various industries. We present large language models with firm analyst reports with/without analyst investment ratings and even with 'fake' rating, to get investment ratings generated by LLMs. Our results reveal that LLMs tend to herd the explicit bias in context. We also develop a method to detect potential human opinions, which can encourage LLMs to think independently, some models even exceed human performance in predicting future stock return.

</details>


### [277] [From Traditional Taggers to LLMs: A Comparative Study of POS Tagging for Medieval Romance Languages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09147)
*Matthias Schöffel,Esteban Garces Arias*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文系统评估了大型语言模型（LLM）在中世纪罗曼语族三种语言（中世纪奥克语、加泰罗尼亚语和法语）词性标注任务上的表现，涵盖零样本、少样本、单语微调和跨语言迁移学习等设置。实验表明，LLM方法持续优于传统标注器，其中跨语言迁移学习对低资源语言变体效果显著，双语训练在某些目标语言上可超越更广泛的多语配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 中世纪罗曼语族语言的词性标注因正字法变异、形态复杂性和标注资源匮乏而长期面临挑战，现有研究缺乏对LLM方法在该领域的系统评估与对比。

Method: 对比基于规则和统计的传统标注器与多种LLM方法（零样本、少样本提示、单语微调、跨语言迁移学习），在三种中世纪语言的标准数据集上进行实验。

Result: LLM方法全面超越传统标注器；微调和多语训练带来最大提升；跨语言迁移学习对低资源语言变体尤为有效；针对特定目标语言的双语训练可优于更宽泛的多语配置。

Conclusion: 跨语言迁移策略的设计需考虑语言亲缘关系和数据集特征；现代神经方法可有效应用于中古文本处理；研究成果可为数字人文领域的LLM词性标注管道部署提供实践指导。

Abstract: Part-of-speech (POS) tagging for Medieval Romance languages remains challenging due to orthographic variation, morphological complexity, and limited annotated resources. This paper presents a systematic empirical evaluation of large language models (LLMs) for POS tagging across three medieval varieties: Medieval Occitan, Medieval Catalan, and Medieval French. We compare traditional rule-based and statistical taggers with modern open-source LLMs under zero-shot prompting, few-shot prompting, monolingual fine-tuning, and cross-lingual transfer learning settings.
  Experiments on historically grounded datasets show that LLM-based approaches consistently outperform traditional taggers, with fine-tuning and multilingual training yielding the largest improvements. In particular, cross-lingual transfer learning substantially benefits under-resourced varieties, while targeted bilingual training can outperform broader multilingual configurations for specific target languages. The results highlight the importance of linguistic proximity and dataset characteristics when designing transfer strategies for historical NLP.
  These findings provide empirical insights into the applicability of modern neural methods to medieval text processing and provide practical guidance for deploying LLM-based POS tagging pipelines in digital humanities research. All code, models, and processed datasets are released for reproducibility.

</details>


### [278] [Lost in Translation? Exploring the Shift in Grammatical Gender from Latin to Occitan](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09156)
*Ahan Chatterjee,Matthias Schöffel,Matthias Aßenmacher,Esteban Garces Arias*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出一个可解释的深度学习框架，研究拉丁语到罗曼语族演变中语法性别系统从三元（阳性、阴性、中性）到二元（阳性、阴性）的重构过程，在词汇和语境两个层面分析性别信息的分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究拉丁语到罗曼语族演变中语法性别系统从三元到二元的历时重构现象，并理解该过程中性别信息在词汇（词素）与语境（句法）之间的分布特征。

Method: 提出了一个可解释的深度学习框架，包含：(1) 针对低资源历史语言场景设计了鲁棒性更强的分词器；(2) 在词汇层面评估形态特征对性别预测的贡献；(3) 在语境层面量化不同词性类别对语法性别预测的贡献。

Result: 传统分词策略在该低资源历史场景下不够鲁棒，所提出的分词器优于基线方法；在词汇和语境层面上成功刻画了性别信息在词元与句子语境之间的分布特征。

Conclusion: 该可解释深度学习框架能有效分析历时语法性别重构现象，揭示了性别信息在词汇和语境层面的分布规律，并为低资源历史语言研究提供了可复现的工具和数据集。

Abstract: The diachronic evolution from Latin to the Romance languages involved a restructuring of the grammatical gender system from a tripartite configuration (masculine, feminine, neuter) to a bipartite one (masculine, feminine). In this work, we introduce an interpretable deep learning framework to investigate this phenomenon at both lexical and contextual levels. First, we show that conventional tokenization strategies are insufficiently robust for this low-resource historical setting, and that our proposed tokenizer improves performance over these baselines. At the lexical level, we evaluate the contribution of morphological features to gender prediction. At the contextual level, we quantify the contributions of different part-of-speech categories to grammatical gender prediction. Together, these analyses characterize the distribution of gender information between the lemma and its sentential context. We make our codebase, datasets, and results publicly available.

</details>


### [279] [WorldSpeech: A Multilingual Speech Corpus from Around the World](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09167)
*Antonis Asonitis,Luca A. Lanzendörfer,Frédéric Berdoz,Roger Wattenhofer*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: WorldSpeech是一个包含76种语言、65k小时对齐音频-文本数据的24kHz多语言语音语料库，微调现有ASR模型后，在11种语言上实现了平均63.5%的相对词错误率降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有ASR系统在高资源语言上表现良好，但大多数语言由于缺乏公开可用的对齐音频-文本数据，识别精度严重下降，亟需一个大规模多语言语音语料库来缓解数据稀缺问题。

Method: 从议会记录、国际广播、公共领域有声书等多种公开来源收集并构建了包含76种语言、65k小时、采样率为24kHz的对齐音频-文本语料库WorldSpeech。

Result: 在11种类型多样的语言上微调现有ASR模型后，实现了平均63.5%的相对词错误率（WER）降低。其中37种语言提供超过200小时对齐语音，28种超过500小时，24种超过1000小时。

Conclusion: WorldSpeech能够显著提升多语言ASR性能，为低资源语言的语音识别研究提供了重要数据基础。

Abstract: Automatic speech recognition (ASR) performs well for high-resource languages with abundant paired audio-transcript data, but its accuracy degrades sharply for most languages due to limited publicly available aligned data. To this end, we introduce WorldSpeech, a 24 kHz multilingual speech corpus comprising 65k hours of aligned audio-transcript data across 76 languages, collected from diverse public sources including parliamentary proceedings, international broadcasts, and public-domain audiobooks. For 37 languages, WorldSpeech provides more than 200 hours of aligned speech, with 28 exceeding 500 hours and 24 surpassing 1k hours. Fine-tuning existing ASR models on WorldSpeech results in an average relative Word-Error-Rate reduction of 63.5% across 11 typologically diverse languages.

</details>


### [280] [Two Ways to De-Bias an LLM-as-a-Judge: A Continuous-Score Comparison of Hierarchical Bayesian Calibration and Neural-ODE Score Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09227)
*Andrea Morandi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文比较了两种LLM评分校准方法：参数化贝叶斯线性校正（小样本高效但存在非线性饱和）与非参数神经ODE流（大样本优势显著），并提出了基于数据预算的选择决策规则。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM作为自动评分器存在偏差（宽严不一、中间压缩、冗长答案被高估），需要后验校准。但如何建模评分映射（参数化vs非参数化）尚不明确，且不同数据预算下方法选择缺乏指导。

Method: 对比两种校正器：1) 参数化层次贝叶斯线性校正（小样本，带逐评分不确定性）；2) 非参数神经ODE流（FFJORD）评分传输流。在UltraFeedback细粒度评分数据集（1700配对样本，200保留）上，从三个子问题评估：总体均值恢复、逐项准确率、分布形状匹配。

Result: 两种方法在100和1500锚点时均能将原始+0.71均值偏移缩小至±0.08以内。100锚点时线性校正器KL散度优于流（0.031 vs 0.058），MAE持平；1500锚点时流在所有指标上胜出（MAE 0.320 vs 0.359, Pearson 0.922 vs 0.896, KL 0.026 vs 0.037）。线性校正器在1500锚点以下饱和，无法拟合残差tanh形非线性；流随标签增加持续改进。

Conclusion: 方法选择本质是数据预算问题：小样本场景选参数化贝叶斯线性校正，大样本场景选非参数神经ODE流。研究提供了明确的生产部署决策规则。

Abstract: [Abridged] Using a Large Language Model (LLM) as an automatic rater (LLM-as-a-judge) is cheap but potentially biased: some judges run lenient, others strict, the middle of the scale gets compressed, and verbose answers may be over-rewarded. A common remedy is post-hoc calibration: leave the cheap judge in place and, on a modest set of paired anchors, fit a transformation from raw judge scores to an estimate of the human rating. We compare two correctors that take opposing views on how this mapping should be modeled: a parametric, small-anchor hierarchical Bayesian linear correction with per-score uncertainty, and a non-parametric Neural-ODE (FFJORD) score-transport flow. Both are run head-to-head on UltraFeedback fine-grained_score (1700 paired examples, 200 held out), with calibration split into three operational sub-questions: population-mean recovery, per-item accuracy, and distributional-shape match.
  The headline result is that the choice between methods is primarily a data-budget question. Both correctors close the raw $+0.71$-point mean offset to within $\pm 0.08$ of the GPT-4 reference, at 100 and at 1500 anchors. Past that, the methods swap roles. With 100 anchors, the linear corrector reconstructs the human-score distribution roughly twice as well by KL divergence (0.031 vs. 0.058) and ties the flow on MAE. With 1500 anchors the flow wins on every metric (MAE 0.320 vs. 0.359, Pearson 0.922 vs. 0.896, KL 0.026 vs. 0.037). The Bayesian linear corrector saturates well below 1500 anchors: residual $\tanh$-shaped non-linearity is, by construction, structure a linear correction cannot fit. The flow keeps improving as labels grow. We translate these findings into an explicit decision rule for production deployments.

</details>


### [281] [Matching Meaning at Scale: Evaluating Semantic Search for 18th-Century Intellectual History through the Case of Locke](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09236)
*Yu Wu,Ananth Mahadevan,Filip Ginter,Michael Mathioudakis,Mikko Tolonen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文评估了语义搜索在18世纪思想史研究中捕捉隐含引用（如转述、间接思想借鉴）的能力，发现其显著优于传统词汇匹配方法，但仍存在“词汇门控”效应，即检索结果部分受制于表层词汇重叠。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数字化语料库研究方法过度依赖词汇层面的文本重用检测（逐字引用），无法有效捕捉转述和复杂的隐性思想借鉴，限制了思想史研究中知识传播与影响力的分析深度。

Method: 以约翰·洛克的著作在18世纪的接受为案例，基于专家标注和语义分类学，对比现成的语义搜索管道与词汇基线方法在检索隐含思想接收方面的表现，并进行语言学诊断分析。

Result: 语义搜索能检索到比词汇基线方法更多的隐含接受案例，但存在“词汇门控”效应——检索仍部分受限于表层词汇重叠。

Conclusion: 语义检索在分析大规模历史语料库中的思想传播方面具有潜力，但也存在局限性，未来需进一步突破词汇层面的约束。

Abstract: While digitized corpora have transformed the study of intellectual transmission, current methods rely heavily on lexical text reuse detection, capturing verbatim quotations but fundamentally missing paraphrases and complex implicit engagement. This paper evaluates semantic search in 18th-century intellectual history through the reception of John Locke's foundational work. Using expert annotation grounded in a semantic taxonomy, we examine whether an off-the-shelf semantic search pipeline can surface meaning-level correspondences overlooked by lexical methods. Our results demonstrate that semantic search retrieves substantially more implicit receptions than lexical baselines. However, linguistic diagnostics also reveal a "lexical gatekeeping" effect, where retrieval remains partially constrained by surface vocabulary overlap. These findings highlight both the potential and the limitations of semantic retrieval for analyzing the circulation of ideas in large historical corpora. The data is available at https://github.com/COMHIS/locke-sim-data.

</details>


### [282] [LLM Agents Already Know When to Call Tools -- Even Without Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09252)
*Chung-En Sun,Linbo Liu,Ge Yan,Zimo Wang,Tsui-Wei Weng*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出When2Tool基准测试和Probe&Prefill方法，通过线性探针从模型隐状态解码工具必要性，在不显著损失准确率的情况下减少48%的不必要工具调用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工具增强型LLM智能体会不加区分地调用工具，即使模型可以直接回答，造成API费用和延迟浪费，但缺乏系统研究何时真正需要工具调用的基准。

Method: 构建18个环境的When2Tool基准（15个单跳、3个多跳），涵盖计算规模、知识边界和执行可靠性三类工具必要性。提出Probe&Prefill方法：训练轻量线性探针从模型预生成隐状态解码工具必要性，然后预填充引导语句控制模型行为。

Result: 工具必要性可从模型隐状态线性解码（AUROC 0.89-0.96），远超模型自身语言化推理。Probe&Prefill减少48%工具调用，准确率仅下降1.7%；最佳基线在同等准确率下仅减少6%工具调用，或实现类似工具调用减少但准确率损失高5倍。

Conclusion: 模型隐状态已编码工具必要性知识但生成时未能利用，通过线性探针读取该信号可有效减少不必要工具调用。

Abstract: Tool-augmented LLM agents tend to call tools indiscriminately, even when the model can answer directly. Each unnecessary call wastes API fees and latency, yet no existing benchmark systematically studies when a tool call is actually needed. We propose When2Tool, a benchmark of 18 environments (15 single-hop, 3 multi-hop) spanning three categories of tool necessity -- computational scale, knowledge boundaries, and execution reliability -- each with controlled difficulty levels that create a clear decision boundary between tool-necessary and tool-unnecessary tasks. We evaluate two families of training-free baselines: Prompt-only (varying the prompt to discourage unnecessary calls) and Reason-then-Act (requiring the model to reason about tool necessity before acting). Both provide limited control: Prompt-only suppresses necessary calls alongside unnecessary ones, and Reason-then-Act still incurs a disproportionate accuracy cost on hard tasks. To understand why these baselines fail, we probe the models' hidden states and find that tool necessity is linearly decodable from the pre-generation representation with AUROC 0.89--0.96 across six models, substantially exceeding the model's own verbalized reasoning. This reveals that models already know when tools are needed, but fail to act on this knowledge during generation. Building on this finding, we propose Probe&Prefill, which uses a lightweight linear probe to read the hidden-state signal and prefills the model's response with a steering sentence. Across all models tested, Probe&Prefill reduces tool calls by 48% with only 1.7% accuracy loss, while the best baseline at comparable accuracy only reduces 6% of tool calls, or achieves a similar tool call reduction but incurs a 5$\times$ higher accuracy loss. Our code is available at https://github.com/Trustworthy-ML-Lab/when2tool

</details>


### [283] [Cornerstones or Stumbling Blocks? Deciphering the Rock Tokens in On-Policy Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09253)
*Yuxuan Jiang,Runchao Li,Shubhashis Roy Dipta,Dawei Li,Zhao Yang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现在线策略蒸馏（OPD）中存在一类“岩石令牌”（Rock Tokens），它们即使训练收敛后仍持续保持高损失，占据高达18%的令牌，贡献巨大梯度但功能上对推理性能无实质贡献，表明大量优化资源浪费在结构性和语篇残差上。通过绕过这些障碍可显著简化对齐过程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强化学习带可验证奖励（RLVR）领域已发现少数关键令牌对推理提升起决定性作用，但在线策略蒸馏（OPD）中令牌级别的理解尚属空白。作者旨在探究OPD训练中令牌层面的动态特性。

Method: 通过实证分析OPD训练中的令牌级损失分布，识别出持续高损失的“岩石令牌”；采用因果干预方法评估其对模型推理性能的功能贡献；分析其梯度范数占比与训练动态。

Result: 岩石令牌在训练收敛后仍占生成令牌的18%；它们贡献了不成比例的高梯度范数但自身参数几乎不变；因果干预表明这些令牌对推理性能无实质贡献。

Conclusion: OPD训练中大量优化带宽浪费在学生模型无法或无需内化的结构性和语篇残差上；有策略地绕过这些“绊脚石”可显著提升蒸馏效率，挑战了均匀令牌加权的必要性。

Abstract: While recent work in Reinforcement Learning with Verifiable Rewards (RLVR) has shown that a small subset of critical tokens disproportionately drives reasoning gains, an analogous token-level understanding of On-Policy Distillation (OPD) remains largely unexplored. In this work, we investigate high-loss tokens, a token type that--as the most direct signal of student-teacher mismatch under OPD's per-token KL objective--should progressively diminish as training converges according to existing studies; however, our empirical analysis shows otherwise. Even after OPD training reaches apparent saturation, a substantial subset of tokens continues to exhibit persistently high loss; these tokens, which we term Rock Tokens, can account for up to 18\% of the tokens in generated outputs. Our investigation reveals two startling paradoxes. First, despite their high occurrence frequency providing a disproportionately large share of total gradient norms, Rock Tokens themselves remain stagnant throughout training, resisting teacher-driven corrections. Second, through causal intervention, we find that these tokens provide negligible functional contribution to the model's actual reasoning performance. These findings suggest that a vast amount of optimization bandwidth is spent on structural and discourse residuals that the student model cannot or need not internalize. By deconstructing these dynamics, we demonstrate that strategically bypassing these ``stumbling blocks'' can significantly streamline the alignment process, challenging the necessity of uniform token weighting and offering a more efficient paradigm for large-scale model distillation.

</details>


### [284] [Beyond Continuity: Challenges of Context Switching in Multi-Turn Dialogue with LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09268)
*Aditya Sinha,Harald Steck,Vito Ostuni,Matteo Rinaldi*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究了多轮对话中LLM对用户话题转移（pivot）和需求细化（refine）的检测能力，以及从过往对话中筛选相关上下文的能力。通过构建合成基准测试评估10种LLM，发现仅部分推理型和强指令型模型能准确检测话题转移，开源模型表现较差，且所有模型都存在位置偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在多轮对话中，用户经常调整请求或切换话题，但LLM常常忽略这些话题转移，携带无关的上下文从而导致响应不准确。作者希望通过压力测试来系统评估LLM在这方面的能力。

Method: 将多轮理解任务拆分为两个子任务：(1) 检测当前轮次用户是pivot（转移话题）还是refine（细化需求）；(2) 从之前轮次中筛选出相关的上下文。基于真实世界数据集构建合成基准测试，模拟不同难度级别的上下文转移，并在零样本设置下评估10种LLM。

Result: 仅部分推理型和强指令型LLM能准确检测pivot；开源LLM在此任务上表现困难，即使有明确提示也经常携带过时上下文；所有模型都存在位置偏差问题。

Conclusion: 基于实验结果，讨论了提升LLM多轮对话长期鲁棒性的关键启示。

Abstract: Users interacting with Large Language Models (LLMs) in a multi-turn conversation routinely refine their requests or pivot to new topics. LLMs, however, often miss these topic shifts and carry over irrelevant context from previous turns, leading to inaccurate responses. In this paper, we stress-test the multi-turn understanding of LLMs and study the following two sub-tasks: (1) detecting whether the user pivots or refines in the current turn, and (2) shortlisting relevant context from previous turns. To this end, we construct synthetic benchmarks based on real-world datasets from varied domains, as to simulate context shifts of different levels of difficulty. We then evaluate the zero-shot performance of ten LLMs (open-weight, closed-source and reasoning), and demonstrate that only some reasoning and strongly instructed LLMs are accurate in detecting pivots; open-weight LLMs struggle with the task and frequently carry stale context even with explicit cues; and all models suffer from a position bias. Based on the results, we discuss key takeaways for improving long-term robustness in multi-turn capabilities for LLMs.

</details>


### [285] [DeltaRubric: Generative Multimodal Reward Modeling via Joint Planning and Verification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09269)
*Rui Liu,Dian Yu,Zhenwen Liang,Yucheng Shi,Tong Zheng,Runpeng Dai,Haitao Mi,Pratap Tokekar,Leoweiliang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出DeltaRubric方法，将多模态偏好评估重构为"规划-执行"两阶段过程：先由分歧规划器生成实例特定的验证清单，再由清单验证器执行检查。通过多角色强化学习联合优化，在Qwen3-VL上显著提升奖励模型性能（VL-RewardBench上4B模型+22.6，8B模型+18.8）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM奖励模型存在"惰性评判"问题——依赖语言先验而忽视细粒度视觉验证。尽管基于Rubric的评估在纯文本场景有效，但多模态任务的视觉推理复杂度使该方法难以推广，因为关键差异往往依赖实例特定的视觉细节。

Method: DeltaRubric将多模态偏好评估分解为两个步骤：(1) 分歧规划器——生成中立、实例特定的验证清单；(2) 清单验证器——对照图像和问题执行自生成的检查。整个流程通过多角色强化学习联合优化规划和验证能力，在单一MLLM内实现。

Result: 在Qwen3-VL 4B和8B Instruct模型上验证：VL-RewardBench上，4B模型整体准确率提升+22.6，8B模型提升+18.8，大幅优于无Rubric基线。

Conclusion: 将评估分解为结构化、可验证的步骤能够带来更可靠和更具泛化性的多模态奖励建模。

Abstract: Aligning Multimodal Large Language Models (MLLMs) requires reliable reward models, yet existing single-step evaluators can suffer from lazy judging, exploiting language priors over fine-grained visual verification. While rubric-based evaluation mitigates these biases in text-only settings, extending it to multimodal tasks is bottlenecked by the complexity of visual reasoning. The critical differences between responses often depend on instance-specific visual details. Robust evaluation requires dynamically synthesizing rubrics that isolate spatial and factual discrepancies. To address this, we introduce $\textbf{DeltaRubric}$, an approach that reformulates multimodal preference evaluation as a plan-and-execute process within a single MLLM. DeltaRubric operates in two steps: acting first as a $\textit{Disagreement Planner}$, the model generates a neutral, instance-specific verification checklist. Transitioning into a $\textit{Checklist Verifier}$, it executes these self-generated checks against the image and question to produce the final grounded judgment. We formulate DeltaRubric as a multi-role reinforcement learning problem, jointly optimizing planning and verification capabilities. Validated on Qwen3-VL 4B and 8B Instruct models, DeltaRubric achieves solid empirical gains. For instance, On VL-RewardBench, it improves base model overall accuracy by $\textbf{+22.6}$ (4B) and $\textbf{+18.8}$ (8B) points, largely outperforming standard no-rubric baselines. The results demonstrate that decomposing evaluation into structured, verifiable steps leads to more reliable and generalizable multimodal reward modeling.

</details>


### [286] [BetaEdit: Null-Space Constrained Sequential Model Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09285)
*Bingqing Liu,Wei Liu,Yuhua Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文揭示了基于零空间模型编辑方法中的知识泄露问题，并分析了历史感知更新策略有效的原因，提出了BetaEdit框架，在大规模顺序编辑中优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于零空间的方法在实际中依赖近似零空间导致知识泄露，且在顺序编辑中性能严重下降。近期研究表明历史感知策略可缓解此问题，但其内在原因尚不明确，需要深入分析并提出改进方案。

Method: 首先揭露现有零空间方法中的知识泄露问题，分析历史感知更新为何能保持编辑性能和通用能力；在此基础上提出BetaEdit框架，有效控制知识泄露并将历史感知更新融入零空间范式。

Result: 在三类大语言模型、两个标准基准上的大量实验表明，BetaEdit在大规模顺序编辑这一挑战性场景中持续优于先前方法。

Conclusion: 通过揭示知识泄露问题并引入历史感知机制，BetaEdit有效提升了零空间方法在顺序编辑中的表现。

Abstract: Null-space-based methods have garnered considerable attention in model editing by constraining updates to the null space of the pre-existing knowledge representation, thereby preserving the model's original behavior. However, in practice these methods rely on an approximate null space--leading to knowledge leakage--and further suffer from severe performance degradation during sequential editing. Recent work shows that history-aware editing strategies can empirically mitigate this decline, yet the underlying reason remains unclear. In this paper, we first expose the knowledge leakage inherent in existing null-space approaches and then analyze why history-aware updates effectively preserve both editing performance and general capabilities during long-horizon editing. Building on these insights, we propose BetaEdit, a refined framework that effectively controls the knowledge leakage and integrates history-aware updates into the null-space paradigm. Extensive experiments on three large language models across two standard benchmarks show that BetaEdit consistently outperforms prior methods in the challenging regime of massive-scale sequential editing. Code is available at: https://github.com/lbq8942/BetaEdit.

</details>


### [287] [Test-Time Speculation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09329)
*Avinash Kumar,Sujay Sanghavi,Poulami Das*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文发现现有投机解码方法在长文本生成中接受长度会退化至接近1（无加速效果），原因是草稿模型在离线短序列上训练但推理时需匹配长序列分布。为此提出测试时投机（TTS），一种在线蒸馏方法，在推理过程中利用验证步骤已有的目标模型信号持续自适应调整草稿模型，在多个模型上将接受长度平均提升41%，最高提升72%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的投机解码方法（如DFlash、EAGLE-3、PARD）在长文本生成任务中性能严重退化，接受长度随生成长度增加而下降，在数千个token后接近1（即无加速效果）。根本原因是草稿模型在短序列上离线训练，推理时被迫匹配目标模型在长序列上的输出，超出其训练分布。

Method: 提出Test-Time Speculation (TTS)，一种测试时在线蒸馏方法。核心洞察：token验证步骤已经调用了目标模型处理每个草稿token，这些调用可提供训练信号而无需额外开销。将草稿模型视为学生、目标模型视为教师，在多个投机轮次中持续调整草稿模型，每轮更新都能随生成过程提高草稿精度。

Result: 在Qwen-3、Qwen-3.5和Llama3.1系列模型上，TTS相比最先进的投机解码方法，接受长度平均提升41%，最高提升72%，且优势随生成长度增加而扩大。

Conclusion: TTS通过测试时连续自适应蒸馏，有效解决了现有投机解码方法在长文本生成中的分布偏移问题，显著提升了接受长度，使投机解码在长响应任务中仍能保持加速效果。

Abstract: Speculative decoding accelerates LLM inference by using a fast draft model to generate tokens and a more accurate target model to verify them. Its performance depends on the $\textit{acceptance length}$, or number of draft tokens accepted by the target. Our studies show that the acceptance length of even state-of-the-art speculators, like DFlash, EAGLE-3 and PARD degrade with generation length, reaching values close to 1 (i.e. no speedup) within just a few thousand output tokens, making speculators ineffective for long-response tasks. Acceptance lengths decline because most speculators are trained offline on short sequences, but are forced to match the target model on much longer outputs at inference, well beyond their training distribution.
  To address this issue, we propose $\textit{Test-Time Speculation (TTS)}$, an online distillation approach that continuously adapts the speculator at test-time. TTS leverages the key insight that the token verification step already invokes the target model for each draft token, providing the training signal needed to adapt the draft at no additional cost. Treating the draft as the student and the target as a teacher, TTS adjusts the draft over several speculation rounds, with each update improving the draft's accuracy as generation proceeds. Our results across multiple models from the Qwen-3, Qwen-3.5, and Llama3.1 families show that TTS improves acceptance lengths over state-of-the-art speculators by up to $72\%$ and $41\%$ on average, with the benefits scaling with increased generation lengths.

</details>


### [288] [RuPLaR : Efficient Latent Compression of LLM Reasoning Chains with Rule-Based Priors From Multi-Step to One-Step](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09346)
*Xiaocheng Luo,Kang Wang,Zaifu Zhan,Yuechi Zhou,Xiangyu Duan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出RuPLaR框架，通过单模型单步的压缩范式，利用基于规则的先验概率分布指导潜在推理，以解决现有潜在思维链方法中多步/多模型结构导致的错误传播和协调开销问题。实验表明该方法在准确率上提升11.1%，且仅需极少的token使用量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有Chain-of-Thought范式受限于自然语言的低效性和表达局限，而潜在思维链推理虽在连续潜在空间中运作，但现有多步或多模型范式存在错误传播和协调开销等结构复杂性问题。因此，作者旨在消除级联过程和模型间依赖，实现高效、可扩展的潜在推理。

Method: 提出One-Model One-Step压缩框架RuPLaR：1) 在单训练阶段训练LLM自主生成潜在推理token，由基于规则的先验概率分布引导；2) 设计联合训练目标，包含交叉熵保证答案一致性、KL散度对齐软token与规则先验（Soft Thinking约束）、以及问题-思维语义对齐约束。

Result: 与现有潜在CoT方法相比，准确率提升11.1%，且token使用量极少，验证了该方法的有效性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: RuPLaR通过单模型单步压缩范式成功解决了潜在推理中的结构复杂性问题，在提升性能的同时大幅降低了计算开销，为潜在推理提供了高效且可扩展的新方案。

Abstract: The Chain-of-Thought (CoT) paradigm, while enhancing the interpretability of Large Language Models (LLMs), is constrained by the inefficiencies and expressive limits of natural language. Latent Chain-of-Thought (latent CoT) reasoning, which operates in a continuous latent space, offers a promising alternative but faces challenges from structural complexities in existing multi-step or multi-model paradigms, such as error propagation and coordination overhead. In this paper, we introduce One-Model One-Step, a novel compression framework for Latent Reasoning with Rule-Based Priors(RuPLaR) to address this challenge. Our method trains an LLM to autonomously generate latent reasoning tokens in a single training stage, guided by rule-based prior probability distributions, thereby eliminating cascaded processes and inter-model dependencies.
