To ensure reasoning quality, we design a joint training objective that enforces answer consistency via cross-entropy, aligns soft tokens with rule-based priors via KL divergence (the Soft Thinking constraint), and adds a problem-thought semantic alignment constraint in the representation space. Extensive experiments show that our compression framework not only improves accuracy by 11.1% over existing latent CoT methods but also achieves this with minimal token usage, underscoring its effectiveness and extensibility. Code: https://github.com/xiaocen-luo/RuPLaR.

</details>


### [289] [HOME-KGQA: A Benchmark Dataset for Multimodal Knowledge Graph Question Answering on Household Daily Activities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09348)
*Shusaku Egami,Aoi Ohta,Tomoki Tsujimura,Masaki Asada,Tatsuya Ishigaki,Ken Fukuda,Masahiro Hamasaki,Hiroya Takamura*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了HOME-KGQA，一个基于日常家务活动多模态知识图谱的复杂问答基准数据集，包含涉及多级时空推理、多模态对齐和聚合函数的挑战性问题。实验表明，现有基于LLM的KGQA方法在该数据集上表现不佳，凸显了真实场景部署的挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有KGQA基准数据集偏向百科知识、仅限单一模态、缺乏细粒度时空数据，限制了其在具身AI等真实场景中的适用性。因此需要构建更具挑战性的多模态时空KGQA基准。

Method: 基于日常家务活动构建多模态知识图谱，设计包含多跳推理、多级时空推理、多模态对齐和聚合函数的复杂自然语言问题及对应的图数据库查询语言。

Result: 基于LLM的KGQA方法在HOME-KGQA上的表现远低于现有数据集，说明当前方法难以应对真实场景中的复杂时空推理和多模态理解需求。

Conclusion: HOME-KGQA为评估和推动KGQA系统在真实世界部署提供了更具挑战性的基准，揭示了现有方法在复杂时空推理和多模态理解方面的不足。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) provide flexible natural language processing capabilities, while knowledge graphs (KGs) offer explicit and structured knowledge. Integrating these two in a complementary manner enables the development of reliable and verifiable AI systems. In particular, knowledge graph question answering (KGQA) has attracted attention as a means to reduce LLM hallucinations and to leverage knowledge beyond the training data. However, existing KGQA benchmark datasets are biased toward encyclopedic knowledge, limited to a single modality, and lack fine-grained spatiotemporal data, which limits their applicability to real-world scenarios targeted by Embodied AI. We introduce HOME-KGQA, a novel KGQA benchmark dataset built on a multimodal KG of daily household activities. HOME-KGQA consists of complex, multi-hop natural language questions paired with graph database query languages. Compared to existing benchmarks, it includes more challenging questions that involve multi-level spatiotemporal reasoning, multimodal grounding, and aggregate functions. Experimental results show that the LLM-based KGQA methods fail to achieve performance comparable to that on existing datasets when evaluated on HOME-KGQA. This highlights significant challenges that should be addressed for the real-world deployment of KGQA systems. Our dataset is available at https://github.com/aistairc/home-kgqa

</details>


### [290] [Cross-Cultural Transfer of Emoji Semantics and Sentiment in Financial Social Media](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09414)
*Ahmed Mahrous,Roberto Di Pietro*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探究了表情符号在金融沟通中的跨语言、跨平台和跨资产社区的零样本情感迁移能力，发现表情符号的语义和情感极性具有高度稳定性，加入表情符号可以提升情感模型的跨域泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然表情符号在在线金融沟通中被广泛使用，但其能否在不同语言、平台和资产社区之间提供可迁移的情感信号尚不明确。

Method: 使用四种语言的Twitter和StockTwits帖子构建大规模语料库，测量跨社区差异，并评估在表情符号仅、文本仅和文本+表情符号三种输入设置下训练的情感模型的表现。

Result: 表情符号频率在不同社区（尤其是跨语言）存在差异，但语义和情感极性基本稳定；跨资产迁移性能下降最小，跨语言迁移最具挑战性；加入表情符号一致地缩小了迁移差距。

Conclusion: 金融沟通中存在部分共享的"表情符号代码"，表情符号提供了紧凑且与语言无关的情感线索，能够改善模型在不同市场和平台上的泛化能力。

Abstract: Emojis are widely used in online financial communication, but it is unclear whether they provide transferable sentiment signals across languages, platforms, and asset communities. This study examines the extent to which emoji usage, semantics, and sentiment polarity remain stable across financial communities, and how these layers influence zero-shot sentiment transfer. Using large corpora of Twitter and StockTwits posts in four languages, we measure cross-community divergence and evaluate sentiment models trained under emoji-only, text-only, and text+emoji inputs.
  We find that emoji frequencies differ across communities, especially across languages, but their semantics and sentiment polarity are largely stable. Cross-asset transferability shows minimal degradation, while cross-language transfer remains the most challenging. Including emojis consistently reduces transfer gaps relative to text-only models. These results indicate that financial communication exhibits a partially shared ``emoji code,'' and that emojis provide compact, language-independent sentiment cues that improve model generalization across markets and platforms.

</details>


### [291] [Perception Without Engagement: Dissecting the Causal Discovery Deficit in LMMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09422)
*Jiafeng Liang,Zhihao Zhu,Zihan Zhang,Baoqi Ren,Shixin Jiang,Runxuan Liu,Tao Ren,Ming Liu,See-Kiong Ng,Bing Qin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出ProCauEval评估协议和ADPO强化学习框架，系统揭示并缓解LMM在视频因果发现中对文本先验的过度依赖问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基准仅测量响应准确率，无法揭示LMM在因果发现中依赖文本先验捷径的机制来源与程度。

Method: 提出ProCauEval扰动评估协议，通过五种控制配置分解视觉/文本模态贡献；并提出ADPO，基于负教师对齐的强化学习框架，最大化原始输入与视觉损坏输入间的策略分布散度。

Result: 17个主流LMM评估表明模型能准确感知视频内容但因果推理中系统性地未充分利用视觉信息；后训练强化放大而非缓解文本先验依赖；高基线性能模型在扰动下更脆弱。ADPO有效提升视觉参与度而不牺牲基础理解。

Conclusion: ADPO为缓解LMM文本先验依赖提供了可行方案，是迈向可靠因果发现的第一步。

Abstract: Although Large Multimodal Models (LMMs) have achieved strong performance on general video understanding, their susceptibility to textual prior shortcuts during causal discovery has been recognized as a critical deficit. The underlying mechanisms of this phenomenon remain incompletely understood, as existing benchmarks only measure response accuracy without revealing the sources and extent of the deficit. We introduce ProCauEval, a perturbation-based evaluation protocol that shifts from outcome assessment to mechanism diagnosis, probing causal discovery through five controlled configurations that systematically manipulate visual and textual modalities to decompose their respective contributions to model behavior and dissect the failure modes. Evaluating 17 mainstream LMMs, we find that models faithfully perceive video content yet systematically underexploit it during causal reasoning. We further observe that stronger post-training amplifies rather than mitigates textual prior reliance, and that higher baseline performance correlates with greater fragility under perturbation. To address these, we propose Anti-Distillation Policy Optimization (ADPO), a reinforcement learning framework built on negative teacher alignment, which augments GRPO by explicitly pushing the policy away from a prior-only counterfactual teacher induced by visual corruption. Specifically, ADPO maximizes the divergence between the policy distributions conditioned on the original and visually corrupted inputs, thereby forcing the model to ground its reasoning in visual evidence rather than textual shortcuts. Extensive experiments show that ADPO improves visual engagement without sacrificing fundamental comprehension, thus offering a preliminary step toward reliable causal discovery.

</details>


### [292] [PumpSense: Real-Time Detection and Target Extraction of Crypto Pump-and-Dumps on Telegram](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09431)
*Ahmed Mahrous,Roberto Di Pietro*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文构建了超28万条Telegram帖子的人工标注数据集，提出基于LightGBM（F1=0.79, 延迟9.4秒/样本）和BGE-M3（F1=0.83, 延迟50毫秒/样本）的实时加密货币拉盘检测方法，以及基于LLM的目标币种/交易所提取方法（准确率0.91），首次建立了操纵币种和交易所提取的基准测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有针对Telegram协调的加密货币拉盘骗局的研究缺乏同时具备高可靠性和快速响应的解决方案，主要原因在于缺乏公开的消息级标注数据以及现有方法的设计局限。

Method: 1）构建包含超28万条Telegram帖子的标注数据集（含2,246条拉盘公告及目标币种/交易所）；2）对比LightGBM（轻量树模型）和BGE-M3（Transformer）进行实时拉盘公告检测；3）对比传统规则方法与LLM进行目标加密货币和交易所提取。

Result: LightGBM检测F1=0.79（延迟9.4秒/样本），BGE-M3检测F1=0.83（延迟50毫秒/样本）；LLM提取准确率0.91，传统规则方法因代码歧义不可用。

Conclusion: 消息级分析可在微秒级实现拉盘检测，远优于依赖市场数据的现有方法（延迟数十秒）；LLM有效解决了传统规则方法因代码歧义导致的提取失败问题。

Abstract: Cryptocurrency pump-and-dump schemes coordinated via Telegram threaten market integrity. However, existing research addressing this specific threat has not yet produced solutions that combine reliable results with fast response. This is in part due to the absence of publicly available, message-level labeled data, as well as design choices. In this paper, we address both issues. In particular, we introduce a corpus of over 280,000 Telegram posts from 39 pump-organizing groups, all manually reviewed to identify 2,246 pump announcements and their targeted cryptocurrency and exchange. Leveraging this dataset, we define two tasks: real-time pump-announcement detection and target cryptocurrency/exchange extraction. For detection, we compare two machine-learning models: a lightweight tree-based LightGBM classifier (F1=0.79, latency=9.4 s/sample) and a transformer-based BGE-M3 (F1=0.83, latency=50 ms/sample). With our proposed approach, we show that message analysis can achieve near-instant pump detection at the level of individual Telegram message windows. Unlike prior work that relies purely on market data and typically detects pumps tens of seconds after abnormal trading activity is observed, our method operates directly on the coordination messages themselves and can be evaluated in microseconds per window on commodity hardware. To our knowledge, we also establish the first benchmark for manipulated coin and exchange extraction. We demonstrate that traditional rule-based extraction methods, widely relied upon in prior literature, are ineffective due to ticker ambiguity. In contrast, LLMs achieve the highest accuracy with a score of 0.91.

</details>


### [293] [Key Coverage Matters: Semi-Structured Extraction of OCR Clinical Reports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09440)
*Yu Wang,Yingyun Li,Ying Qin,Haiyang Qian*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于规范键的条件式抽取问答方法，通过迭代键挖掘、归一化、聚类和轻量人工验证构建规范键库，并引入键覆盖率作为评估指标，在0.2B BERT模型上对20多家医院的真实临床报告进行实验，结果表明键覆盖率是端到端性能的主导因素，在Top-90覆盖率下精确匹配F1达0.839，边界容忍匹配F1达0.893。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 临床报告因隐私法规和数据孤岛而分散在不同医疗机构，患者跨院就诊时只能携带纸质或扫描报告，阻碍了EHR集成、纵向回顾及下游应用（如患者管理、随访、真实世界研究和临床试验匹配）。现有OCR虽可数字化，但临床文档异质、OCR噪声大且需低成本的本地部署，导致可靠信息提取困难。

Method: 将问题形式化为基于规范键的条件式抽取问答（key-conditioned extractive question answering）。通过迭代键挖掘、归一化、聚类和轻量人工验证维护规范键库，引入键覆盖率（key coverage）作为量化指标。采用0.2B参数的BERT模型进行实验。

Result: 在20多家医院真实报告上的实验显示，模型性能随键覆盖率单调提升。Top-90规范键覆盖下，精确匹配F1为0.839，边界容忍匹配F1为0.893。在精确匹配下，该模型优于微调的Qwen3-0.6B基线。

Conclusion: 键覆盖率是端到端性能的主导因素。该方法虽基于中文语料，但依赖于半结构化临床报告的语言无关的键值组织形式，通过构建适当的规范键库和别名映射可适配其他语言和场景。

Abstract: Clinical reports are often fragmented across healthcare institutions because privacy regulations and data silos limit direct information sharing. When patients seek care at a different hospital, they often carry paper or scanned reports from prior visits. This hinders EHR integration and longitudinal review, and downstream applications that depend on more complete patient records, such as patient management, follow-up care, real-world studies, and clinical-trial matching. Although OCR can digitize such reports, reliable extraction remains challenging because clinical documents are heterogeneous, OCR text is noisy, and many healthcare settings require low-cost on-premise deployment. We formulate this problem as canonical key-conditioned extractive question answering over OCR-derived clinical reports. Because the key fields are neither fixed nor known in advance, the key space is open. We maintain a canonical key inventory through iterative key mining, normalization, clustering, and lightweight human verification, and introduce key coverage as a metric to quantify inventory completeness. Using a 0.2B BERT-based model, experiments on real-world reports from more than 20 hospitals show performance improves monotonically with key coverage. The model achieves F1 scores of 0.839 and 0.893 under exact match and boundary-tolerant matching, respectively, once the Top-90 canonical keys are covered. These results show that key coverage is a dominant factor for end-to-end performance. At Top-90 coverage, our model outperforms a fine-tuned Qwen3-0.6B baseline under exact match. Although our annotated corpus is Chinese, the method relies on the language-agnostic key-value organization of semi-structured clinical reports and can be adapted to other settings given an appropriate canonical key inventory and alias mapping.

</details>


### [294] [Beyond Position Bias: Shifting Context Compression from Position-Driven to Semantic-Driven](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09463)
*Jiwei Tang,Zhijing Huang,Xinyu Zhang,Chen Jason Zhang,Jianxing Yu,Libin Zheng,Rui Meng,Jian Yin*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出语义一致性上下文压缩方法SeCo，通过将物理位置驱动的位置压缩范式转变为语义驱动的动态压缩，消除位置偏差，提升长上下文场景性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有软提示压缩方法受位置偏差限制，依赖固定位置插入可学习标记或按物理token布局分组，导致性能不稳定和语义碎片化。

Method: SeCo在语义空间中动态锚定压缩，选择查询相关token作为语义中心，通过一致性加权合并聚合其余token，实现语义一致性保持与位置偏差消除。

Result: 在14个基准测试和两个骨干模型上，SeCo在下游任务性能、推理延迟和域外鲁棒性方面均表现优异。

Conclusion: 从位置驱动转向语义驱动的上下文压缩范式能有效克服位置偏差，提升模型在长上下文场景中的效率和效果。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have demonstrated exceptional performance across diverse tasks. However, their deployment in long-context scenarios faces high computational overhead and information redundancy. While soft prompt compression has emerged as a promising way to mitigate these costs by compressing sequences into compact embeddings, existing paradigms remain fundamentally constrained by position bias: they primarily rely on learnable tokens insertion at fixed positions or group tokens according to their physical token layout, thereby inducing performance instability and semantic fragmentation. To overcome this bottleneck, we propose Semantic Consistency Context Compression (SeCo), a method that shifts context compression from position-driven to semantic-driven. Rather than constraint by physical token layout, SeCo dynamically anchors compression directly in the semantic space by selecting query-relevant tokens as semantic centers and aggregating remaining tokens via consistency-weighted merging. This design inherently preserves semantic consistency while eliminating position bias. Extensive experiments on 14 benchmarks across two backbone models demonstrate that SeCo consistently shows superiority in downstream tasks, inference latency, and out-of-domain robustness. The code is available at https://anonymous.4open.science/r/seco-EE5E.

</details>


### [295] [Align and Shine: Building High-Quality Sentence-Aligned Corpora for Multilingual Text Simplification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09476)
*Kenji Hilasaca,Nouran Khallaf,Serge Sharoff*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文针对非英语语言文本简化数据集稀缺的问题，通过众包方式从可比语料库中收集并处理简化数据，构建了涵盖加泰罗尼亚语、英语、法语、意大利语和西班牙语的多语言文本简化语料库，并提出了文档级到句子级的对齐机制，最终公开了对齐后的句子对数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非英语语言缺乏大规模、高质量的文本简化数据集，限制了文本简化模型的训练与评估。

Method: 从可比语料库中通过众包方式收集简化数据，设计了文档级到句子级的对齐机制，构建了涵盖五种语言的多语种文本简化语料库。

Result: 成功构建了跨五种语言（加泰罗尼亚语、英语、法语、意大利语和西班牙语）的句子级对齐数据集，并公开可用。

Conclusion: 所提出的众包数据收集与对齐方法可有效构建多语言文本简化语料库，填补了非英语语言数据资源的空白。

Abstract: Text simplification plays a crucial role in improving the accessibility and comprehensibility of written information for diverse audiences, including language learners and readers with limited literacy. Despite its importance, large-scale, high-quality datasets for training and evaluating text simplification models remain scarce for languages other than English. This paper reports an experimental study on the collection and processing of crowd-sourced simplification data from comparable corpora to construct a corpus suitable for both training and testing text simplification systems across multiple languages (Catalan, English, French, Italian and Spanish). We report mechanisms for sentence-level alignment from document-level data. The resulting dataset of the aligned sentence pairs is publicly available.

</details>


### [296] [A Cognitively Grounded Bayesian Framework for Misinformation Susceptibility](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09483)
*Pranava Madhyastha*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出BPL（有界语用听者）模型，这是一个基于贝叶斯框架的认知模型，通过引入三种认知边界（递归深度限制、先验压缩参数、可用性样本量）来扩展理性言语行为理论，用于模拟信息失调的易感性，并在LIAR和MultiFC基准上验证了其真实性分类能力和深度不匹配悖论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有信息失调（错误、虚假、恶意信息）研究缺乏认知层面的计算建模，无法解释不同个体对 misinformation 的易感性差异以及标注者分歧现象。作者希望构建一个认知上有依据的贝叶斯框架来模拟和预测信息失调的传播机制。

Method: BPL模型在理性言语行为（RSA）理论基础上引入三种认知边界：1) 递归深度限制（模拟工作记忆容量）；2) 先验压缩参数（模拟信息瓶颈）；3) 可用性样本量（基于显著性加权的提议分布实现重要性采样）。模型通过这三种有界理性机制来预测信息失调易感性。

Result: 在LIAR和MultiFC两个基准数据集上，BPL在真实性分类任务中表现出竞争性性能，并提供了对深度不匹配悖论（depth-mismatch paradox）的实验支持。

Conclusion: BPL为理解信息失调提供了一个认知上有依据的计算框架，能够解释 misinformation 易感性差异、标注者分歧以及不同类型信息失调（mis-, dis-, mal-information）的差异性脆弱性，但仍标注为"work in progress"。

Abstract: In this (work in progress) paper, we present Bounded Pragmatic Listener (or BPL), a cognitively grounded Bayesian framework for modelling susceptibility to information disorder. BPL extends Rational Speech Act theory with three cognitively motivated bounds derived from the bounded rationality literature with a) a recursion depth bound (that emphasises working memory limits);b) a prior compression parameter (which is oriented at capturing information bottleneck); and c) an availability sample size (that operationalises importance sampling with saliency-weighted proposals). This allows us to test predictions about misinformation susceptibility, annotator disagreement, and the differential vulnerability to mis-, dis-, and mal-information as defined in the Information Disorder framework. We validate BPL on the LIAR and MultiFC benchmarks showcasing competitive veracity classification and experimental support for the depth-mismatch paradox.

</details>


### [297] [APCD: Adaptive Path-Contrastive Decoding for Reliable Large Language Model Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09492)
*Tianyu Zheng,Hong Wu,Jiaji Zhong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出自适应路径对比解码（APCD）框架，通过熵驱动的路径扩展和散度感知的路径对比，在自回归解码中减少幻觉，提升事实准确性并保持解码效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在自回归解码中因早期次优token选择导致错误累积，产生幻觉。现有多路径解码方法缺乏何时分支及如何调控路径间交互的 principled 策略。

Method: APCD包含两个组件：(1) 熵驱动路径扩展——通过Shannon熵度量预测不确定性，仅在存在多个合理续接时才进行分支；(2) 散度感知路径对比——鼓励多样化推理轨迹，同时根据预测分布散度动态衰减路径间影响。

Result: 在8个基准测试上提升了事实准确性，同时保持了解码效率。

Conclusion: APCD通过自适应探索和受控路径交互，有效提升了输出可靠性，减少了幻觉，且不牺牲解码效率。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) often suffer from hallucinations due to error accumulation in autoregressive decoding, where suboptimal early token choices misguide subsequent generation. Although multi-path decoding can improve robustness by exploring alternative trajectories, existing methods lack principled strategies for determining when to branch and how to regulate inter-path interactions. We propose Adaptive Path-Contrastive Decoding (APCD), a multi-path decoding framework that improves output reliability through adaptive exploration and controlled path interaction. APCD consists of two components: (1) Entropy-Driven Path Expansion, which delays branching until predictive uncertainty - measured by Shannon entropy over top candidate tokens - indicates multiple plausible continuations; and (2) Divergence-Aware Path Contrast, which encourages diverse reasoning trajectories while dynamically attenuating inter-path influence as prediction distributions diverge. Experiments on eight benchmarks demonstrate improved factual accuracy while maintaining decoding efficiency. Our code is available at https://github.com/zty-king/APCD.

</details>


### [298] [Hidden Error Awareness in Chain-of-Thought Reasoning: The Signal Is Diagnostic, Not Causal](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09502)
*Aojie Yuan,Zhiyuan Julian Su,Haiyue Zhang,Yi Nian,Yue Zhao*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文发现链式思维推理中模型内部能检测到自身推理错误，但外在表达却充满自信。内部表征可通过线性探针以0.95 AUROC检测错误，但口头自信度在正确与错误轨迹间几乎无差异。然而，这种内部信号无法用于纠正错误，表明错误表征是诊断性而非因果性的，划定了机械可解释性的边界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 质疑CoT推理中"生成的推理反映模型内部计算"这一基本假设，探究模型是否意识到自身推理错误以及这种意识能否用于纠错。

Method: 使用线性探针从隐藏状态预测推理轨迹正确性（AUROC达0.95），对比文本表面分类器（仅0.59）；考察4种干预方法（激活引导、探针引导的best-of-N、自我纠正、激活修补）是否可利用内部信号纠正错误。

Result: 线性探针从第一步推理即可检测错误（0.79 AUROC），而口头自信度在错误（4.55/5）和正确（4.87/5）轨迹间几乎相同。四种干预方法均无法纠正错误，激活修补甚至破坏输出连贯性。

Conclusion: 模型内部具有隐藏的错误感知能力，但这种信号是诊断性（反映计算质量）而非因果性（可引导计算方向）的，与先前工作中可编辑的事实知识表征有本质区别，划定了机械可解释性的应用边界。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) prompting assumes that generated reasoning reflects a model's internal computation. We show this assumption is wrong in a specific, measurable way: models internally detect their own reasoning errors but outwardly express confidence in them. A linear probe on hidden states predicts trace correctness with 0.95 AUROC -- from the very first reasoning step (0.79) -- while verbalized confidence for wrong traces is 4.55/5, nearly identical to correct ones (4.87/5). A text-surface classifier achieves only 0.59 on the same data, confirming a 0.20-point gap invisible in the generated text. This hidden error awareness holds across three model families (Qwen, Llama, Phi), 1.5B-72B parameters, and RL-trained reasoning models (DeepSeek-R1, 0.852 AUROC). The natural question is whether this signal can fix the errors it detects. It cannot. Four interventions -- activation steering, probe-guided best-of-N, self-correction, and activation patching -- all fail; patching destroys output coherence entirely. The signal is diagnostic, not causal: a readout of computation quality, not a lever to redirect it. This delineates a boundary for mechanistic interpretability: error representations during reasoning are fundamentally different from the factual knowledge representations that prior work has successfully edited.

</details>


### [299] [Assessment of RAG and Fine-Tuning for Industrial Question-Answering-Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09533)
*Jakob Sturm,Josef Pichlmeier,Christian Bernhard,Maka Karalashvili,Johannes Klepsch,Georg Groh,Andre Luckow*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究探讨了RAG和FT两种方法在汽车行业封闭数据集上的成本-准确率权衡，发现RAG在效果和成本效率上均优于FT，且开源模型结合RAG可达到与商业模型相当的质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在将LLM应用于企业QA系统时，需要适配领域知识，但RAG和FT两种主流方法在工业场景中的成本-准确率权衡尚不明确。

Method: 在汽车行业两个封闭数据集上比较RAG与FT，并扩展Cost-of-Pass框架，联合评估输出质量、生成成本和用户交互成本。

Result: 商业模型开箱表现最优；开源模型结合RAG可达到可比质量；RAG在封闭和开源模型上均为最有效且成本效率最高的适配方法。

Conclusion: RAG是企业QA系统中领域知识适配的首选方法，在成本与效果之间取得了最佳平衡。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly employed in enterprise question-answering (QA) systems, requiring adaptation to domain-specific knowledge. Among the most prevalent methods for incorporating such knowledge are Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) and fine-tuning (FT). Yet, from a cost-accuracy trade-off perspective, it remains unclear which approach best suits industry scenarios. This study examines the impact of RAG and FT on two closed datasets specific to the automotive industry, assessing answer quality and operational costs. We extend the Cost-of-Pass framework proposed by Erol et al. (arXiv:2504.13359) to jointly assess output quality, generation cost, and user interaction cost. Our findings reveal that while premium models perform best out of the box, open-source models can achieve comparable quality when enhanced with RAG. Overall, RAG emerges as the most effective and cost-efficient adaptation method for both closed- and open-source models.

</details>


### [300] [TAD: Temporal-Aware Trajectory Self-Distillation for Fast and Accurate Diffusion LLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09536)
*Haoyang Zhou,Li Kong,Shijie Ren,Xiting Wang,Shuang Liang,Guowei Wang,Zhenxuan Pan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出TAD框架，通过时间感知的轨迹自蒸馏方法，在扩散大语言模型中同时提升生成质量和推理速度，打破了准确率与并行度之间的权衡限制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型在并行文本生成中面临准确率-并行度权衡问题，即每前向传递的token数增加会导致生成质量下降，现有加速方法往往以牺牲准确率为代价。

Method: 提出TAD框架：1）在数据构建阶段，以提示和真实响应为条件训练教师模型生成解码轨迹并记录中间掩码状态；2）根据剩余解码步数将掩码位置分为近邻和远距离子集；3）对近邻token使用硬交叉熵损失（教师轨迹token为标签），对远距离token使用软KL散度损失；4）由此衍生出两种部署配置：优先准确率的Quality模型和优先加速的Speed模型。

Result: 在LLaDA上，Quality模型将平均准确率从46.2%提升至51.6%；Speed模型将平均AUP从46.2提升至257.1，显著改善了准确率-并行度权衡。

Conclusion: TAD通过时间感知的轨迹自蒸馏有效缓解了扩散LLM的准确率-并行度矛盾，同时支持质量优先和速度优先两种部署模式，验证了方法在并行文本生成中的有效性。

Abstract: Diffusion large language models (dLLMs) offer a promising paradigm for parallel text generation, but in practice they face an accuracy-parallelism trade-off, where increasing tokens per forward (TPF) often degrades generation quality. Existing acceleration methods often gain speed at the cost of accuracy. To address this limitation, we propose TAD, a Temporal-Aware trajectory self-Distillation framework. During data construction, we condition a teacher model on both the prompt and the ground-truth response to generate decoding trajectories, recording the intermediate masked states throughout the process. Based on how many decoding steps remain before each masked token is revealed, we partition masked positions into near and distant subsets. For near tokens, we train the student with a hard cross-entropy loss using the teacher trajectory tokens as labels, encouraging confident predictions for tokens that are about to be decoded. For distant tokens, we apply a soft KL divergence loss between the teacher and student token distributions, providing softer supervision and preserving future planning knowledge. This temporal-aware partition naturally gives rise to two deployment configurations: a Quality model that prioritizes accuracy and a Speed model that favors more aggressive acceleration. Experiments show that TAD consistently improves the accuracy-parallelism trade-off. On LLaDA, it raises average accuracy from 46.2\% to 51.6\% with the Quality model and average AUP from 46.2 to 257.1 with the Speed model. Our code is available at: https://github.com/BHmingyang/TAD

</details>


### [301] [Crosslingual On-Policy Self-Distillation for Multilingual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09548)
*Yihong Liu,Raoyuan Zhao,Michael A. Hedderich,Hinrich Schütze*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出COPSD方法，通过跨语言自蒸馏将模型在高资源语言（英语）的推理能力迁移至低资源语言，在17种非洲语言上显著提升数学推理性能，优于GRPO。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在数学推理上取得显著进展，但该能力在不同语言间分布不均，低资源语言的推理性能远低于高资源语言。

Method: COPSD使用同一模型同时作为学生和教师：学生仅接收低资源语言问题，教师额外获取跨语言上下文（问题翻译及英文参考解答）。训练通过最小化学生自身rollout上的全分布token级散度，提供密集监督，避免结果-only强化学习的稀疏性和不稳定性。

Result: 在17种低资源非洲语言上的实验表明，COPSD一致提升了低资源数学推理性能，显著优于GRPO；改进了答案格式遵循性、增强了测试时扩展能力，并泛化至更难的多语言推理基准。

Conclusion: COPSD有效弥合了语言间的推理能力差距，尤其对更低资源语言提升显著，为跨语言推理提供了实用且高效的训练范式。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have achieved remarkable progress in mathematical reasoning, but this ability is not equally accessible across languages. Especially low-resource languages exhibit much lower reasoning performance. To address this, we propose Crosslingual On-Policy Self-Distillation (COPSD), which transfers a model's own high-resource reasoning behavior to low-resource languages. COPSD uses the same model as student and teacher: the student sees only the low-resource problem, while the teacher receives privileged crosslingual context, including the problem translation and reference solution in English. Training minimizes full-distribution token-level divergence on the student's own rollouts, providing dense supervision while avoiding the sparsity and instability of outcome-only reinforcement learning (RL). Experiments on 17 low-resource African languages show that COPSD consistently improves low-resource mathematical reasoning across model sizes and substantially outperforms Group Relative Policy Optimization (GRPO). Further analyses show that COPSD improves answer-format adherence, strengthens test-time scaling, and generalizes to harder multilingual reasoning benchmarks, with especially large gains for lower-resource languages. We make our code and data available at: https://github.com/cisnlp/COPSD.

</details>


### [302] [Towards Compact Sign Language Translation: Frame Rate and Model Size Trade-offs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09554)
*Kuanwei Chen,Mengfeng Tsai*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出一种紧凑的77M参数手语翻译管道，利用MMPose骨骼姿态提取和T5-small线性投影，通过降低帧率(12fps)使序列长度减半，编码器自注意力计算复杂度降低75%，BLEU-4仅轻微下降(9.53 vs 10.06)，模型比先前T5-base系统小约3倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前无词汇表(gloss-free)的SLT方法依赖于大型编码器-解码器模型，部署受限。作者旨在探索轻量级架构能否在不依赖分层编码器或大规模模型的情况下保持竞争力。

Method: 采用MMPose进行骨骼姿态提取，将姿态特征通过单一线性投影映射到T5-small（77M参数）。通过改变输入帧率（24fps vs 12fps）控制序列长度，从而降低编码器二次自注意力计算复杂度。

Result: 在How2Sign数据集上，12fps时模型序列长度减半，编码器二次自注意力计算复杂度降低75%，BLEU-4为9.53（24fps时为10.06），模型参数仅77M，比先前T5-base系统小约3倍。

Conclusion: 轻量级架构可以在手语翻译任务中保持竞争力，无需分层编码器或大规模模型。降低帧率是一种有效的效率-性能权衡策略。

Abstract: Sign Language Translation (SLT) converts sign language videos into spoken-language text, bridging communication between Deaf and hearing communities. Current gloss-free approaches rely on large encoder-decoder models, limiting deployment. We propose a compact 77M-parameter pipeline that couples MMPose skeletal pose extraction with a single linear projection into T5-small. By varying the input frame rate, we expose a practical efficiency trade-off: at 12 fps the model halves its sequence length, achieving a 75% reduction in encoder quadratic self-attention computational complexity while incurring only a modest BLEU-4 drop (9.53 vs. 10.06 at 24 fps on How2Sign). Our system is roughly 3x smaller than prior T5-base systems, demonstrating that a lightweight architecture can remain competitive without hierarchical encoders or large-scale models.

</details>


### [303] [CLR-voyance: Reinforcing Open-Ended Reasoning for Inpatient Clinical Decision Support with Outcome-Aware Rubrics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09584)
*Aishik Nagar,Arun-Kumar Kaliya-Perumal,Yu-Hsuan Han,Andrew Sheng-Han Huang,Kristen Kee,Yushi Cao,Yiming Chen,Hongchao Jiang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: CLR-voyance将住院临床推理重构为部分可观测马尔可夫决策过程(POMDP)，利用结果导向且经临床验证的奖励信号进行监督。通过CLR-POMDP框架、oracle生成的适应性评分标准和GRPO后训练，CLR-voyance-8B在住院临床推理任务上达到84.91%，超越GPT-5(77.83%)等前沿模型，并在合作医院部署超过6个月。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有临床LLM评估和RL奖励信号存在三大问题：将顺序决策简化为封闭式检索、泄露临床旅程信息、使用缺乏锚定的LLM-as-judge评分。需要一种能反映住院临床推理本质（部分可观测下的顺序决策）且同时做到结果锚定和临床验证的评估与训练框架。

Method: 1) 将住院推理形式化为POMDP，成功患者旅程划分为策略可见的过去和仅oracle可访问的未来；2) 利用oracle LLM基于过去信息生成案例特定的查询-答案对，并构建首个可在患者旅程未来部分验证的自适应评分标准；3) 使用这些评分标准对Qwen3-8B和MedGemma-4B进行GRPO后训练，然后进行模型合并；4) 开展大规模临床医生对齐研究，包含病例评分标准制定、答案评分和盲法偏好比较。

Result: CLR-voyance-8B在CLR-POMDP上达84.91%，超越GPT-5(77.83%)、MedGemma-27B(66.66%)等模型，在现有医学基准上表现相当或更优。已在合作公立医院部署6+个月，辅助起草数千份推理密集型住院笔记。

Conclusion: 基于POMDP的框架能够更忠实地建模住院临床推理的顺序决策本质；结果锚定+临床验证的奖励信号优于单纯依赖LLM判断；模型合并策略可在保持通用能力的同时提升专科推理水平；临床对齐研究为临床LLM评估和偏好模型选择提供了重要参考。

Abstract: Inpatient clinical reasoning is a sequential decision under partial observability: the clinician sees the admission so far and must choose the next action whose downstream consequences are not yet visible. Existing clinical-LLM evaluations and RL rewards signals collapse this into closed-form retrieval, clinical journey leakage, or unanchored LLM-as-judge scoring. We introduce CLR-voyance, a framework that reformulates inpatient reasoning as a Partially Observable Markov Decision Process (POMDP) and supervises it with rewards that are simultaneously outcome-grounded and clinician-validated. We instantiate the formulation as CLR-POMDP, which partitions successful patient journeys into a policy-visible past and an oracle-only future. Using the past information, an oracle LLM generates a case-specific query-answer pair, and the first adaptive rubric for clinical reasoning which is verifiable in the future of the patient journey. These rubrics are used for both post-training and evaluation of models for inpatient clinical reasoning. We post-train Qwen3-8B and MedGemma-4B with GRPO followed by model merging, yielding state-of-the-art inpatient clinical reasoning while retaining generalist capabilities. CLR-voyance-8B achieves 84.91% on CLR-POMDP, ahead of frontier medical reasoning models like GPT-5 (77.83%) and MedGemma-27B (66.66%) and has comparable or better performance on existing medical benchmarks. To ensure a clinically meaningful setting, we conduct a large-scale clinician alignment study, where physicians curate per-case rubrics, grade candidate responses, and provide blinded pairwise preferences of model reasoning. This study provides insights on clinical LLM-as-a-judge and clinical preference-model selection, which can inform the community at large. CLR-voyance has been deployed for 6+ months at a partner public hospital, drafting thousands of reasoning-heavy inpatient notes.

</details>


### [304] [Byte-Exact Deduplication in Retrieval-Augmented Generation: A Three-Regime Empirical Analysis Across Public Benchmarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09611)
*Sietse Schelpe*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文实证分析了RAG流水线中字节级精确块去重技术，在三种不同冗余场景下实现了从0.16%到80.34%的上下文缩减，并通过跨供应商人工评估证实该去重方法在零质量回归的前提下显著降低推理计算开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RAG系统在处理高冗余文本（如多轮对话、企业文档）时存在大量重复内容，导致推理计算浪费。现有去重方法多为近似去重或语义级去重，缺乏对字节级精确去重在质量影响上的系统评估。

Method: 在三个操作场景（干净学术检索22.2M BeIR语料、构造的企业模式、多轮对话AI）中度量字节缩减率；采用5位评审员跨4个生产API（Gemini 2.5 Flash、Claude Sonnet 4.6、Llama 3.3 70B、GPT-5.1）进行校准面板评估；应用五类别人机协同噪声去除协议对多数票标记为"实质性差异"的样本进行审计。

Result: 干净学术检索场景字节缩减0.16%，企业模式缩减24.03%，多轮对话场景缩减80.34%。经审计后，所有四个供应商在干净和高冗余RAG场景中均低于严格的<5% Wilson 95%上界实质性差异阈值，证明字节级精确去重引入零可测量质量回归。

Conclusion: 字节级精确去重能够在确定性前提下实现显著的推理计算节省，同时不损害评估级模型质量，是一种安全高效的RAG优化策略。

Abstract: This preprint presents an empirical analysis of byte-exact chunk-level deduplication in Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) pipelines. We measure context reduction across three distinct operating regimes: clean academic retrieval (0.16% byte reduction on 22.2M BeIR passages), constructed enterprise patterns (24.03% reduction), and multi-turn conversational AI (80.34% reduction). To validate quality preservation, we conducted a cross-vendor 5-judge calibrated panel evaluation across four production APIs (Google Gemini 2.5 Flash, Anthropic Claude Sonnet 4.6, Meta Llama 3.3 70B, and OpenAI GPT-5.1). Applying a five-category human-in-the-loop noise-removal protocol to panel-majority materially different (MAT) pairs, we establish that byte-exact deduplication introduces zero measurable quality regression. Post-audit, all four vendors clear the strict <5% Wilson 95% upper-bound MAT threshold in both the clean and high-redundancy RAG regimes. This work demonstrates that substantial inference compute savings can be achieved deterministically without compromising evaluation-grade model quality.

</details>


### [305] [Scratchpad Patching: Decoupling Compute from Patch Size in Byte-Level Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09630)
*Lin Zheng,Vasilisa Bashlovkina,Timothy Dozat,Dan Garrette,Laura Rimell,Joshua Maynez*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Scratchpad Patching (SP)，通过在字节补丁内插入临时草稿板来聚合已观测字节并刷新补丁级上下文表示，从而缓解无分词器语言模型中补丁大小与模型质量之间的权衡。实验表明，SP能在保持较大补丁尺寸（如16字节/补丁）的同时，显著缩小与字节级基线的质量差距，同时实现16倍KV缓存压缩和3-4倍推理计算量减少。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无分词器语言模型（尤其是基于补丁的变体）面临补丁大小与模型质量之间的固有权衡：更大的补丁可减少计算量和KV缓存占用，但会损害建模质量。这种权衡源于"补丁滞后"问题——在补丁完全被观测到之前，其中的字节预测必须依赖前一个补丁的过时表示以保持因果性，且滞后程度随补丁增大而加剧。

Method: Scratchpad Patching (SP)在补丁内部插入临时草稿板（scratchpads），用于聚合当前补丁中已观测到的字节信息，并刷新补丁级上下文表示以供后续字节预测使用。SP利用下个字节预测熵（next-byte prediction entropy）作为触发条件，选择性地在信息密集型区域分配计算资源，从而支持推理时计算量的后验调整。

Result: 在自然语言和代码任务上，SP在相同补丁大小下提升了模型质量。例如，在16字节/补丁的设置下，SP增强模型在下游评估中匹配或接近字节级基线性能，同时实现16倍更小的KV缓存（基于补丁）和3-4倍更少的推理计算量。

Conclusion: Scratchpad Patching有效缓解了补丁滞后问题，在保持较大补丁带来效率优势的同时显著恢复建模质量，为无分词器语言模型提供了一种灵活的计算-质量权衡调节机制。

Abstract: Tokenizer-free language models eliminate the tokenizer step of the language modeling pipeline by operating directly on bytes; patch-based variants further aggregate contiguous byte spans into patches for efficiency. However, the average patch size chosen at the model design stage governs a tight trade-off: larger patches reduce compute and KV-cache footprint, but degrade modeling quality. We trace this trade-off to patch lag: until a patch is fully observed, byte predictions within it must rely on a stale representation from the previous patch to preserve causality; this lag widens as patches grow larger. We introduce Scratchpad Patching (SP), which inserts transient scratchpads inside each patch to aggregate the bytes seen so far and refresh patch-level context for subsequent predictions. SP triggers scratchpads using next-byte prediction entropy, selectively allocating compute to information-dense regions and enabling post-hoc adjustment of inference-time compute. Across experiments on natural language and code, SP improves model quality at the same patch size; for example, even at $16$ bytes per patch, SP-augmented models match or closely approach the byte-level baseline on downstream evaluations while using a $16\times$ smaller KV cache over patches and $3$-$4\times$ less inference compute.

</details>


### [306] [Can We Trust LLMs for Mental Health Screening? Consistency, ASR Robustness, and Evidence Faithfulness](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09634)
*Erfan Loweimi,Sofia de la Fuente Garcia,Samira Loveymi,Hadi Daneshvar,Saturnino Luz*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究评估了三种LLM（Phi-4、Gemma-2-9B、Llama-3.1-8B）在零样本条件下从语音估计HADS分数的可靠性，发现Phi-4和Gemma-2-9B在模型内一致性、ASR鲁棒性和证据忠实性方面表现优异，而Llama-3.1-8B在ASR干扰下一致性显著下降，且模型间的关键词一致性远低于分数一致性，揭示了分数-证据解离现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM可以从语音中零样本估计HADS分数（医院焦虑抑郁量表），但临床部署要求模型在三个维度上具备可靠性：模型内一致性、ASR（自动语音识别）鲁棒性和证据忠实性，现有研究对此缺乏系统评估。

Method: 对111名英语参与者，使用三种LLM（Phi-4、Gemma-2-9B、Llama-3.1-8B），基于真实转录文本和三种Whisper ASR变体（Large、Medium、Small），每个模型-条件组合进行三次独立运行，评估一致性、ASR鲁棒性和证据忠实性。

Result: (i) Phi-4和Gemma-2-9B模型内一致性优秀（ICC > 0.89），且在ASR下退化极小；(ii) Llama-3.1-8B在ASR下一致性脆弱，ICC从0.82降至0.36（10%词错误率）；(iii) 鲁棒模型的预测效度在ASR下基本保持；(iv) Phi-4和Gemma-2-9B的关键词基础性超过93%，Llama-3.1-8B降至77-81%。模型间关键词一致性远低于分数一致性。

Conclusion: 不同LLM在临床语音评估的可靠性维度上存在显著差异，分数-证据解离现象对临床可解释性具有重要影响，提示临床部署前需对LLM进行多维度可靠性验证。

Abstract: LLMs can estimate Hospital Anxiety and Depression Scale (HADS) scores from speech in a zero-shot manner, but clinical deployment requires reliability across three dimensions: intra-model consistency, ASR robustness, and evidence faithfulness. We evaluate three LLMs (Phi-4, Gemma-2-9B, and Llama-3.1-8B) on 111 English-speaking participants using ground-truth transcripts and three Whisper ASR variants (Large, Medium, Small), with three independent runs per model-condition pair. We find that (i) Phi-4 and Gemma-2-9B achieve excellent intra-model consistency (ICC > 0.89) with minimal degradation under ASR; (ii) Llama-3.1-8B shows ASR-fragile consistency, with ICC dropping from 0.82 to 0.36 at 10% WER; (iii) predictive validity is largely preserved under ASR for robust models; and (iv) keyword groundedness exceeds 93% for Phi-4 and Gemma-2-9B but falls to 77-81% for Llama-3.1-8B. Inter-model keyword agreement is far lower than score-level agreement, revealing a score-evidence dissociation with implications for clinical interpretability.

</details>


### [307] [K12-KGraph: A Curriculum-Aligned Knowledge Graph for Benchmarking and Training Educational LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09635)
*Hao Liang,Qihan Lin,Zhaoyang Han,Xiaochen Ma,Zhen Hao Wong,Meiyi Qiang,Linzhuang Sun,Wentao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出K12-KGraph，一个基于人教版教材构建的课程对齐知识图谱，包含7种节点类型和9种关系类型；并在此基础上构建了K12-Bench基准（23,640道多选题，覆盖5类图衍生任务）和K12-Train训练语料（约2,300个QA对）。实验表明现有LLM在该基准上表现不佳（Gemini-3-Flash最高57%），而基于K12-Train的有监督微调在样本效率上显著优于其他通用指令微调语料。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有教育AI基准（如C-Eval、CMMLU等）主要评估事实回忆能力，忽略了课程认知（curriculum cognition），即对知识在前提链、概念分类、实验-概念链接和教学序列中的结构理解。为弥补这一空白，需要构建能够评估和提升LLM课程认知能力的资源和基准。

Method: (1) 从人教版官方教科书（数学、物理、化学、生物，小初高）中提取课程对齐知识图谱K12-KGraph，包含7种节点类型（概念、技能、实验、习题、节、章、书）和9种关系类型（分类、前提、关联、验证、评估、定位、顺序等）。(2) 基于该图构建K12-Bench基准（23,640道多选题，含Ground、Prereq、Neighbor、Evidence、Locate五个任务族）。(3) 利用图结构和节点属性合成约2,300个QA对的K12-Train训练语料，用于KG引导的有监督微调。

Result: 在K12-Bench上，Gemini-3-Flash仅达57%精确匹配，最佳开源模型Gemma-4-31B-IT为46%。在严格匹配2,300样本SFT预算条件下，基于K12-Train微调的Qwen3-4B-Base和Llama-3.1-8B-Base在GaokaoBench和EduEval上均一致优于同等规模的8个主流指令微调语料，证明了课程结构化监督在教育微调中的高样本效率。

Conclusion: 现有LLM在课程认知任务上表现显著不足，而基于课程对齐知识图谱的结构化训练数据在提升模型教育任务能力方面具有高样本效率优势。论文开源了图、基准、训练数据和完整构建流程。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly used in K-12 education, yet existing benchmarks such as C-Eval, CMMLU, GaokaoBench, and EduEval mainly evaluate factual recall through exam-style question answering. Effective educational AI additionally requires curriculum cognition: understanding how knowledge is structured through prerequisite chains, concept taxonomies, experiment-concept links, and pedagogical sequencing. To address this gap, we introduce K12-KGraph, a curriculum-aligned knowledge graph extracted from official People's Education Press textbooks across mathematics, physics, chemistry, and biology from primary to high school. The graph contains seven node types (Concept, Skill, Experiment, Exercise, Section, Chapter, Book) and nine relation types covering taxonomy, prerequisite, association, verification, assessment, location, and order. Based on this graph, we construct two resources: (1) K12-Bench, a 23,640-question multi-select benchmark spanning five graph-derived task families (Ground, Prereq, Neighbor, Evidence, and Locate); and (2) K12-Train, a KG-guided supervised fine-tuning corpus of approximately 2,300 QA pairs synthesized from graph structure and node attributes. Experiments reveal substantial deficiencies in curriculum cognition: on K12-Bench, Gemini-3-Flash achieves only 57% exact match, while the best open-source model, Gemma-4-31B-IT, reaches 46%. Under a strictly matched 2,300-sample SFT budget on Qwen3-4B-Base and Llama-3.1-8B-Base, K12-Train consistently outperforms equally sized subsets from eight mainstream instruction-tuning corpora on both GaokaoBench and EduEval, demonstrating that curriculum-structured supervision is highly sample-efficient for educational tuning. We release the graph, benchmark, training data, and full construction pipeline.

</details>


### [308] [MedMeta: A Benchmark for LLMs in Synthesizing Meta-Analysis Conclusion from Medical Studies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09661)
*Huy Hoang Ha,Benoit Favre,Francois Portet*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: MedMeta是首个评估大语言模型从医学Meta分析中综合证据能力的基准，包含81项Meta分析和两种工作流程。研究发现：外部知识检索（RAG）显著优于纯参数化方法，领域微调收益有限，所有模型在识别否定证据方面均存在漏洞，即使在理想RAG条件下模型表现也仅略高于平均水平（约2.7/5.0）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有医学基准主要测试LLM的事实回忆能力，但其高阶推理能力（尤其是从多个来源综合证据）尚未得到充分探索。为填补这一空白，作者引入MedMeta基准。

Method: 从PubMed收集2018-2025年间81项医学Meta分析，构建两种评估工作流：1) 基于检索增强生成（Golden-RAG），提供真实摘要作为外部知识；2) 仅依赖模型内部知识的参数化方法（Parametric-only）。采用LLM-as-a-judge评估协议，并通过Pearson相关系数（0.81）和Bland-Altman分析验证与人类专家评分的一致性。

Result: 1) Golden-RAG工作流显著优于参数化方法；2) 领域微调的收益有限，在提供外部材料时几乎被抵消；3) 所有模型均无法识别和拒绝被否定的证据，暴露了RAG系统的关键漏洞；4) 即使在理想RAG条件下，模型得分仅约2.7/5.0。

Conclusion: MedMeta为证据综合提供了具有挑战性的新基准。对于临床应用，开发稳健的RAG系统比仅进行模型专业化训练更具前景。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) have saturated standard medical benchmarks that test factual recall, yet their ability to perform higher-order reasoning, such as synthesizing evidence from multiple sources, remains critically under-explored. To address this gap, we introduce MedMeta, the first benchmark designed to evaluate an LLM's ability to generate conclusions from medical meta-analyses using only the abstracts of cited studies. MedMeta comprises 81 meta-analyses from PubMed (2018--2025) and evaluates models using two distinct workflows: a Retrieval-Augmented Generation (Golden-RAG) setting with ground-truth abstracts, and a Parametric-only approach relying on internal knowledge. Our evaluation framework is validated by a well-structured analysis showing our LLM-as-a-judge protocol strongly aligns with human expert ratings, as evidenced by high Pearson's r correlation (0.81) and Bland-Altman analysis revealing negligible systematic bias, establishing it as a reliable proxy for scalable evaluation. Our findings underscore the critical importance of information grounding: the Golden-RAG workflow consistently and significantly outperforms the Parametric-only approach across models. In contrast, the benefits of domain-specific fine-tuning are marginal and largely neutralized when external material is provided. Furthermore, stress tests show that all models, regardless of architecture, fail to identify and reject negated evidence, highlighting a critical vulnerability in current RAG systems. Notably, even under ideal RAG conditions, current LLMs achieve only slightly above-average performance (~2.7/5.0). MedMeta provides a challenging new benchmark for evidence synthesis and demonstrates that for clinical applications, developing robust RAG systems is a more promising direction than model specialization alone.

</details>


### [309] [The Silent Vote: Improving Zero-Shot LLM Reliability by Aggregating Semantic Neighborhoods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09739)
*Sanket Badhe,Priyanka Tiwari,Deep Shah*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出了一种名为Semantic Softmax的推理层方法，通过聚合目标标签语义邻域的得分来纠正零样本分类中的归一化偏差（Renormalization Bias），从而改善校准性和判别性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准约束解码中，当模型被限制在少量目标标签上时，softmax操作会丢弃原始分布中语义同义词的概率质量，导致人为的过度自信和校准不良。作者将此现象定义为归一化偏差（Renormalization Bias）及沉默投票（Silent Vote）。

Method: 提出Semantic Softmax，一种推理时层（inference-time layer），通过聚合每个目标标签周围语义邻域的得分来恢复丢失的信息，从而修正归一化偏差。

Result: 在Qwen-3和Phi-4-mini模型上使用GoEmotions和Civil Comments数据集进行评估。结果表明Semantic Softmax在所有评估指标上均有一致改进：显著降低了期望校准误差（ECE）和Brier分数，同时提升了AUROC和Macro-F1等判别性能指标。

Conclusion: 通过考虑语言细微差别，Semantic Softmax为零样本分类提供了一种更校准、更准确的替代方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models are increasingly used as zero-shot classifiers in complex reasoning tasks. However, standard constrained decoding suffers from a phenomenon we define as Renormalization Bias. When a model is restricted to a small set of target labels, the standard softmax operation discards the probability mass assigned to semantic synonyms in the original distribution. This loss of information, which we call the Silent Vote, results in artificial overconfidence and poor calibration.
  We propose Semantic Softmax, an inference-time layer that recovers this lost information by aggregating the scores of the semantic neighborhood surrounding each target label. We evaluate this approach on Qwen-3 and Phi-4-mini models using GoEmotions and Civil Comments datasets. Our results demonstrate consistent improvements across all evaluation metrics: Semantic Softmax substantially reduces Expected Calibration Error (ECE) and Brier Score, while simultaneously enhancing discriminative performance in terms of AUROC and Macro-F1. By accounting for linguistic nuances, our method provides a more calibrated and accurate alternative for zero-shot classification.

</details>


### [310] [Language Models Without a Trainable Input Embedding Table: Learning from Fixed Minimal Binary Token Codes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09751)
*A. Bochkov*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文研究语言模型输入嵌入表是否必要。使用固定最小二进制标记编码（K=⌈log₂V⌉位）替代可训练输入嵌入矩阵，在65,536词汇量下用16维固定二进制编码平铺至1024维，以及通过可逆仿射变换在𝔽₂ᴷ上随机重新编码的无表变体。在约170亿词元上训练的32层解码器模型上，固定编码与标准可训练基线验证困惑度相当（均值2.36 vs 2.44），减少6710万可训练输入参数。无表变体困惑度为2.39。结论：可训练输入嵌入表在该设置下非必要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究语言模型中可训练输入嵌入表是否真正必要，尝试用固定二进制编码替代以简化模型结构、减少参数。

Method: 将标准可训练V×d_model输入嵌入矩阵替换为固定最小二进制标记编码（V=65,536时K=16位），编码平铺至d_model=1024维；另设无表变体，从词元ID实时生成编码并通过𝔽₂ᴷ上可逆仿射变换随机重新编码。

Result: 固定编码验证困惑度均值2.36 vs 基线2.44，减少6710万可训练参数；无表变体困惑度2.39。差异在种子间变化范围（4.8%）内，未达统计显著性。

Conclusion: 在该实验设置下，可训练输入嵌入表对语言建模并非必要，固定二进制编码可作为替代方案。

Abstract: Trainable input embedding tables are a standard component of modern language models. We ask whether they are actually necessary at the input interface. For a vocabulary of size $V$, exact token identity requires only $K=\lceil \log_2 V\rceil$ bits. We replace the usual trainable $V\times d_{\text{model}}$ input embedding matrix with fixed minimal binary token codes and a zero-parameter lift to model width. In our main setting, $V=65{,}536$, so $K=16$, and tokens are represented by fixed 16-dimensional binary codes tiled to $d_{\text{model}}=1024$. We also evaluate a fully table-free variant in which codes are generated from token IDs on the fly and randomly recoded by an invertible affine transform over $\mathbb{F}_2^K$. Across matched 32-layer decoder-only models trained on approximately 17B tokens and evaluated over three independent training seeds, fixed minimal codes achieve comparable held-out validation perplexity to a standard learned-input baseline while removing 67.1M trainable input parameters. The fixed-code runs have a lower mean validation perplexity in our experiments, 2.36 versus 2.44, but the observed gap is within the measured seed-to-seed variation of 4.8\%; we therefore interpret the result as evidence that the trainable input table is not necessary, rather than as a statistically resolved superiority claim. The table-free affine-recoded variant remains close at 2.39 despite a slightly shorter training run. These results show that, in this regime, a trainable input embedding table is not necessary for useful language modeling. The output projection remains standard and trainable.

</details>


### [311] [Exploitation Without Deception: Dark Triad Feature Steering Reveals Separable Antisocial Circuits in Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09773)
*Cameron Berg,Roshni Lulla*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 使用稀疏自编码器（SAE）特征引导技术放大Llama-3.3-70B-Instruct中的黑暗三联征人格特质（马基雅维利主义、自恋、精神病态），发现引导后的模型在行为上显著更具剥削性、攻击性和冷漠性（d=10.62），但认知共情和战略欺骗能力不受影响，揭示了反社会倾向在大语言模型中由可分离的计算通路驱动，而非统一建构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究大语言模型中反社会倾向的计算本质：它们是统一的建构还是由可分离的组件构成？通过SAE特征引导放大黑暗三联征特质，揭示特质间的计算可分离性。

Method: 使用稀疏自编码器（SAE）对Llama-3.3-70B-Instruct进行特征引导，放大马基雅维利主义、自恋和精神病态三种特质，通过五种心理学量表评估行为变化，并比较对比发现与语义搜索两种特征发现方法的效果差异。

Result: 引导后模型在新型行为场景中显著更剥削、攻击和冷漠（d=10.62）；认知共情不变；战略欺骗完全不受影响；各特征编码非冗余，驱动不同反社会机制；对比发现的特征同时改变自我报告和行为，语义搜索的特征仅改变自我报告（行为上方法间d=12.65）。

Conclusion: 大语言模型中的反社会倾向由可分离的计算组件构成而非统一建构，剥削与欺骗通过可分离的计算通路运作，这对反社会倾向的检测、测量和控制具有重要启示。

Abstract: We use sparse autoencoder (SAE) feature steering to amplify Dark Triad personality traits (Machiavellianism, narcissism, and psychopathy) in Llama-3.3-70B-Instruct and evaluate the resulting behavioral changes across five psychological instruments. The steered model becomes substantially more exploitative, aggressive, and callous on novel behavioral scenarios (d=10.62) while its cognitive empathy remains intact, reproducing the empathy dissociation characteristic of human Dark Triad populations. Critically, strategic deception is completely unaffected across all features, suggesting that exploitation and deception may operate through dissociable computational pathways in large language models. Individual feature analysis reveals non-redundant encoding, with each feature driving distinct antisocial mechanisms through separable computational pathways. We also show that feature discovery method itself modulates intervention depth: contrastively-discovered features change both self-report and behavior, while semantically-searched features change only self-report (d=12.65 between methods on behavior). These findings suggest that antisocial tendencies in at least one large language model comprise dissociable components rather than a unified construct, with implications for how such tendencies should be detected, measured, and controlled.

</details>


### [312] [cantnlp@DravidianLangTech 2026: organic domain adaptation improves multi-class hope speech detection in Tulu](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09795)
*Andrew Li,Sidney Wong*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文介绍了为DravidianLangTech-2026共享任务构建的图卢语混合编码希望语音检测系统，采用XLM-RoBERTa模型，通过有机收集的混合编码文本进行领域适应，相比基线模型在开发集上表现更好，但在官方测试集上效果一般。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 针对图卢语（Tulu）中混合编码（code-mixed）社交媒体评论中的希望语音（hope speech）检测任务，现有模型在该低资源语言场景下性能有限，需要更好的适应策略。

Method: 采用XLM-RoBERTa作为基础模型，通过有机收集的包含混合编码和混合文字变体的图卢语社交媒体文本进行领域适应训练，构建文本分类系统进行希望语音检测，并与基线模型进行对比。

Result: 有机适应的模型在开发集上优于基线系统；但在官方测试集上，提交系统的表现较为一般。

Conclusion: 在XLM-RoBERTa基础上，利用有机收集的混合编码图卢语社交媒体文本进行进一步领域适应，能够提升混合编码图卢语中希望语音检测的性能。

Abstract: This paper presents our systems and results for the Hope Speech Detection in Code-Mixed Tulu Language shared task at the Sixth Workshop on Speech, Vision, and Language Technologies for Dravidian Languages (DravidianLangTech-2026). We trained an XLM-RoBERTa-based text classification system for detecting hope speech in code-mixed Tulu social media comments. We compared this organically adapted hope speech detection model with our baseline model. On the development set, the organically adapted model outperformed the baseline system. While our submitted systems performed more modestly on the official test set, these results suggest that further adapting XLM-RoBERTa on organically collected Tulu social media text containing code-mixed and mixed-script variation can improve hope speech detection in code-mixed Tulu.

</details>


### [313] [Quantifying the Utility of User Simulators for Building Collaborative LLM Assistants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09808)
*Joseph Suh,Ayush Raj,Minwoo Kang,Serina Chang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出通过下游效用（即训练出的AI助手与真实人类交互的表现）来量化用户模拟器质量，实验表明基于真实人类对话数据微调的模拟器显著优于角色扮演LLM模拟器。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 用户模拟器被广泛用于构建交互式AI助手，但如何衡量这些模拟器的质量仍是一个开放问题。

Method: 在控制实验中，仅改变用户模拟器，通过强化学习训练LLM助手，对比从角色扮演LLM到基于WildChat人类对话微调的模拟器。使用283名参与者的用户研究和WildBench基准进行评估。

Result: 1) 角色扮演LLM训练的助手与初始助手无显著差异（51%胜率），而微调模拟器训练的助手显著提升（58%胜率）。2) 角色扮演模拟器的真实性增强方法（如人物条件）虽有改进但无法弥补差距。3) 扩大模拟器模型规模对微调模拟器有益，但对角色扮演模拟器无增益。4) 角色扮演模拟器训练的助手对其他模拟器泛化失败，而微调模拟器训练的助手则能泛化。

Conclusion: 用户模拟器应基于真实人类行为，并通过其对真实用户的下游效果来评估质量。

Abstract: User simulators are increasingly leveraged to build interactive AI assistants, yet how to measure the quality of these simulators remains an open question. In this work, we show how simulator quality can be quantified in terms of its downstream utility: how an LLM assistant trained with this user simulator performs in the wild when interacting with real humans. In a controlled experiment where only the user simulator varies, we train LLM assistants via reinforcement learning against a spectrum of simulators, from an LLM prompted to role-play a user to one fine-tuned on human utterances from WildChat. As evaluation, we measure pairwise win rates in a user study with 283 participants and on WildBench, a benchmark derived from real human--AI conversations. Training against the role-playing LLM yields an assistant statistically indistinguishable from the initial assistant in our user study (51% win rate), whereas training against the fine-tuned simulator yields significant gains (58% over the initial and 57% over the one trained against role-playing). Closer inspection reveals three further patterns: methods for making role-playing LLMs more realistic (e.g., persona conditioning) improve trained assistants but do not close the gap to the fine-tuned simulator; scaling the simulator's model size benefits the fine-tuned simulator but yields no gain for role-playing ones; and assistants trained against role-playing simulators fail to generalize when paired with other simulators at test time, while the one trained against fine-tuned simulator does. Together, these results argue for grounding user simulators in real human behavior and measuring their quality by their downstream effect on real users.

</details>


### [314] [The Association of Transformer-based Sentiment Analysis with Symptom Distress and Deterioration in Routine Psychotherapy Care](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09838)
*Douglas K. Faust,Peter Awad,Alexandre Vaz,Tony Rousmaniere*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文基于Transformer深度学习架构的细粒度情感分析模型，从751次心理治疗会话中提取话语级和会话级情感特征，发现这些特征与OQ-45心理测量工具的情绪效价维度高度相关，并能显著区分被标记为治疗恶化或脱落风险的患者群体，证明其可作为辅助性的临床痛苦与恶化测量指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 情感分析在心理治疗研究中长期受到关注，但基于Transformer的情感分析模型尚未被作为独立的心理测量工具进行研究，现有工作仅将其视为情绪测量仪器的替代品。

Method: 从751次心理治疗会话的大语料中，利用细粒度情感模型提取话语级和会话级情感特征，分析会话聚合情感得分的分布，并将其与OQ-45问卷的各分量表和总分进行相关性分析，同时比较被OQ理性模型或经验模型标记为恶化/脱落风险患者的情绪分布差异。

Result: 情感特征与OQ-45中与情绪效价相关的组件方向一致且相关性最强；被OQ理性或经验结果模型标记为治疗恶化或脱落风险的患者，其情感分布存在统计显著的差异。

Conclusion: 所提出的情感特征与经过完全验证的心理测量工具之间存在显著相关性，表明这些特征至少可作为客户痛苦和治疗恶化的辅助测量指标。

Abstract: Sentiment analysis has been of long-standing interest in psychotherapy research. Recently, the Transformer deep learning architecture has produced text-based sentiment analysis models that are highly accurate and context-aware. These models have been explored as proxies for emotion measurement instruments in psychotherapy, but not investigated as stand-alone psychometric tools. Using proposed utterance-level and session-level sentiment features derived from a fine-grained sentiment model on a large corpus of psychotherapy sessions (N = 751), we investigate the distribution of session aggregated sentiment scores. Further, we characterize the relationship of these features to individual components and the overall score of the OQ-45 instrument and find that this sentiment feature is most strongly correlated to components related to emotional valence in directionally intuitive ways. Finally, we report that there are statistically significant differences between the sentiment distributions for patients flagged as at risk of deterioration or dropping out of care via either the OQ Rational or Empirical outcome models. These correlations to a fully-validated psychometric instrument demonstrate that these proposed sentiment features are, at least, adjunctive measures of client distress and deterioration.

</details>


### [315] [Pseudo-Deliberation in Language Models: When Reasoning Fails to Align Values and Actions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09893)
*Sushrita Rakshit,Hanwen Zhang,Hua Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出“伪 deliberation”概念，指LLM在显式推理下仍存在价值-行动鸿沟。作者构建VALDI框架（含4,941个场景、三项任务、五个指标）系统测量价值对齐，并提出VIVALDI多智能体审计器进行干预，实验显示闭源和开源模型均存在持续的价值-行为不一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM评估多关注其陈述的价值，但这些价值并不可靠地转化为实际行为（价值-行动鸿沟）。本文旨在揭示即使在显式推理条件下这一鸿沟依然存在，并将其命名为“伪 deliberation”，进而系统测量和干预这一现象。

Method: 1) 提出VALDI框架：包含4,941个跨五个人类中心领域的场景、三项任务（价值阐述、推理、行动）和五项量化指标。2) 提出VIVALDI多智能体价值审计器，在不同生成阶段进行干预。3) 对闭源和开源LLM进行系统性实验评估。

Result: 闭源和开源LLM均表现出表达价值与下游对话之间的持续错位，即价值-行动鸿沟在显式推理下仍然存在。

Conclusion: 价值-行动鸿沟是LLM中更深层且顽固的问题（伪 deliberation），仅靠显式推理无法解决。VALDI框架为系统性测量该问题提供了工具，VIVALDI多智能体审计器则展示了干预的潜在方向。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are often evaluated based on their stated values, yet these do not reliably translate into their actions, a discrepancy termed "value-action gap." In this work, we argue that this gap persists even under explicit reasoning, revealing a deeper failure mode we call "Pseudo-Deliberation": the appearance of principled reasoning without corresponding behavioral alignment. To study this systematically, we introduce VALDI, a framework for measuring alignment between stated values and generated dialogue. VALDI includes 4,941 human-centered scenarios across five domains, three tasks that elicit value articulation, reasoning, and action, and five metrics for quantifying value adherence. Across both proprietary and open-source LLMs, we observe consistent misalignment between expressed values and downstream dialogues. To investigate intervention strategies, we propose VIVALDI, a multi-agent value auditor that intervenes at different stages of generation.

</details>


### [316] [Team-Based Self-Play With Dual Adaptive Weighting for Fine-Tuning LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09922)
*Wu Li,Yigeng Zhou,Zesheng Shi,Yequan Wang,Min Zhang,Jing Li*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: TPAW是一种基于团队自博弈和双重自适应加权的自我对齐算法，无需人工标注即可提升LLM对齐性能，在多个基座模型和基准上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自训练方法在LLM对齐中存在两个关键局限：(i)对合成数据质量敏感，导致迭代训练不稳定且放大偏差；(ii)正负响应间的差距随迭代递减，优化效率下降。本文旨在提出一种无需人工监督的、更稳定高效的自对齐方法。

Method: 提出TPAW算法，采用团队自博弈框架，让当前策略模型与历史检查点既协作又竞争；设计两种自适应加权机制：(i)响应重加权方案，调整目标响应的重要性；(ii)玩家加权策略，动态调节每个团队成员在训练中的贡献。从SFT模型初始化，通过迭代方式优化对齐。

Result: 在多种基座模型和LLM基准上，TPAW持续优于现有基线方法。

Conclusion: TPAW通过团队自博弈与双重自适应加权，在完全自监督条件下实现了更稳定高效的大模型对齐，有效克服了现有自训练方法的两个关键局限。

Abstract: While recent self-training approaches have reduced reliance on human-labeled data for aligning LLMs, they still face critical limitations: (i) sensitivity to synthetic data quality, leading to instability and bias amplification in iterative training; (ii) ineffective optimization due to a diminishing gap between positive and negative responses over successive training iterations. In this paper, we propose Team-based self-Play with dual Adaptive Weighting (TPAW), a novel self-play algorithm designed to improve alignment in a fully self-supervised setting. TPAW adopts a team-based framework in which the current policy model both collaborates with and competes against historical checkpoints, promoting more stable and efficient optimization. To further enhance learning, we design two adaptive weighting mechanisms: (i) a response reweighting scheme that adjusts the importance of target responses, and (ii) a player weighting strategy that dynamically modulates each team member's contribution during training. Initialized from a SFT model, TPAW iteratively refines alignment without requiring additional human supervision. Experimental results demonstrate that TPAW consistently outperforms existing baselines across various base models and LLM benchmarks. Our code is publicly available at https://github.com/lab-klc/TPAW.

</details>


### [317] [Evolving Knowledge Distillation for Lightweight Neural Machine Translation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09924)
*Xuewen Zhang,Haixiao Zhang,Xinlong Huang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出Evolving Knowledge Distillation (EKD)，一种渐进式知识蒸馏训练框架，通过让学生模型依次从能力逐渐增强的教师模型序列中学习，有效弥合师生模型间的能力差距。在IWSLT-14、WMT-17和WMT-23基准上，EKD使紧凑模型性能接近大教师模型（IWSLT-14上仅差0.08 BLEU）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 知识蒸馏在师生模型能力差距过大时效果显著下降，限制了将大型NMT模型压缩部署到资源受限设备上的效果。

Method: 提出EKD渐进式训练框架，学生模型依次从一系列能力逐渐增强的教师模型中学习，逐步弥合能力差距。

Result: 在IWSLT-14上最终学生模型BLEU达34.24，与最强教师模型（34.32 BLEU）仅差0.08；在WMT-17和WMT-23上也取得一致改进。

Conclusion: EKD能有效弥合师生模型间的能力差距，使紧凑模型获得接近大模型的性能，为NMT模型压缩部署提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Recent advancements in Neural Machine Translation (NMT) have significantly improved translation quality. However, the increasing size and complexity of state-of-the-art models present significant challenges for deployment on resource-limited devices. Knowledge distillation (KD) is a promising approach for compressing models, but its effectiveness diminishes when there is a large capacity gap between teacher and student models. To address this issue, we propose Evolving Knowledge Distillation (EKD), a progressive training framework in which the student model learns from a sequence of teachers with gradually increasing capacities. Experiments on IWSLT-14, WMT-17, and WMT-23 benchmarks show that EKD leads to consistent improvements at each stage. On IWSLT-14, the final student achieves a BLEU score of 34.24, narrowing the gap to the strongest teacher (34.32 BLEU) to just 0.08 BLEU. Similar trends are observed on other datasets. These results demonstrate that EKD effectively bridges the capacity gap, enabling compact models to achieve performance close to that of much larger teacher models.Code and models are available at https://github.com/agi-content-generation/EKD.

</details>


### [318] [PruneTIR: Inference-Time Tool Call Pruning for Effective yet Efficient Tool-Integrated Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09931)
*Luan Zhang,Dandan Song,Zhijing Wu,Zhengyu Chen,Chen Zhang,Yuhang Tian,Huipeng Ma,Chenhao Li,Changzhi Zhou,Xudong Li,Shuhao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出PruneTIR框架，通过剪枝轨迹、重采样工具调用、暂停工具使用三种策略，在推理时提升已具备工具使用能力的大语言模型的推理性能和效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究多聚焦于如何让LLM学会使用工具，但如何进一步提升已具备工具能力的LLM在推理时的推理能力尚未被充分探索。

Method: PruneTIR包含三个组件：(1) Success-Triggered Pruning：成功时剪枝轨迹；(2) Stuck-Triggered Pruning and Resampling：卡住时剪枝并重采样工具调用；(3) Retry-Triggered Tool Suspension：频繁重试失败时暂停工具使用。

Result: 实验结果表明PruneTIR显著提升了Pass@1和效率，同时减少了工作上下文长度。

Conclusion: PruneTIR通过缓解错误工具调用的负面影响并防止LLM陷入反复失败的尝试，有效提升了工具集成推理的性能和效率。

Abstract: Tool-integrated reasoning (TIR) enables large language models (LLMs) to enhance their capabilities by interacting with external tools, such as code interpreters (CI). Most recent studies focus on exploring various methods to equip LLMs with the ability to use tools. However, how to further boost the reasoning ability of already tool-capable LLMs at inference time remains underexplored. Improving reasoning at inference time requires no additional training and can help LLMs better leverage tools to solve problems. We observe that, during tool-capable LLM inference, both the number and the proportion of erroneous tool calls are negatively correlated with answer correctness. Moreover, erroneous tool calls are typically resolved successfully within a few subsequent turns. If not, LLMs often struggle to resolve such errors even with many additional turns. Building on the above observations, we propose PruneTIR, a rather effective yet efficient framework that enhances the tool-integrated reasoning at inference time. During LLM inference, PruneTIR prunes trajectories, resamples tool calls, and suspends tool usage through three components: Success-Triggered Pruning, Stuck-Triggered Pruning and Resampling, and Retry-Triggered Tool Suspension. These three components enable PruneTIR to mitigate the negative impact of erroneous tool calls and prevent LLMs from getting stuck in repeated failed resolution attempts, thereby improving overall LLM performance. Extensive experimental results demonstrate the effectiveness of PruneTIR, which significantly improves Pass@1 and efficiency while reducing the working context length for tool-capable LLMs.

</details>


### [319] [FocuSFT: Bilevel Optimization for Dilution-Aware Long-Context Fine-Tuning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09932)
*Zehua Pei,Hui-Ling Zhen,Xianzhi Yu,Sinno Jialin Pan,Mingxuan Yuan,Bei Yu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 论文提出FocuSFT框架，通过双层优化在监督微调阶段解决长上下文任务中注意力预算被位置特权token占用的问题，显著提升模型在长序列上的表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在处理长输入时，监督微调阶段存在注意力稀释问题：位置偏置和注意力沉没导致模型将大部分注意力分配给位置特权token而非语义相关内容，弱化了梯度信号，限制了模型学习鲁棒长上下文能力。

Method: 提出FocuSFT双层优化框架：内循环在训练上下文中自适应调整轻量级快权重参数，形成参数化记忆以将注意力集中在相关内容上；外循环基于这种锐化后的表示进行标准SFT。内外循环均对上下文token使用双向注意力，同时保持对响应的因果掩码，以减轻因果不对称性导致的注意力沉没。

Result: 在BABILong上，4K-32K上下文长度下准确率提升最高+14pp；在RULER上，16K长度下CWE聚合从72.9%提升至81.1%；在GPQA+智能体工具使用场景下，pass@1相对提升24%。注意力分析显示，FocuSFT将注意力沉没质量减少529倍，训练期间上下文参与度提高3倍。

Conclusion: FocuSFT通过在训练阶段解决注意力稀释问题，有效提升了大语言模型在长上下文任务中的表现，验证了训练时注意力优化的有效性。

Abstract: Large language models can now process increasingly long inputs, yet their ability to effectively use information spread across long contexts remains limited. We trace this gap to how attention budget is spent during supervised fine-tuning (SFT) on long sequences: positional biases and attention sinks cause the model to allocate most of its attention to positionally privileged tokens rather than semantically relevant content. This training-time attention dilution (the starvation of content tokens in the attention distribution) weakens the gradient signal, limiting the model's ability to learn robust long-context capabilities. We introduce FocuSFT, a bilevel optimization framework that addresses this problem at training time. An inner loop adapts lightweight fast-weight parameters on the training context to form a parametric memory that concentrates attention on relevant content, and the outer loop performs SFT conditioned on this sharpened representation. Both loops apply bidirectional attention over context tokens while preserving causal masking for responses, reducing the causal asymmetry that gives rise to attention sinks and aligning inner-outer behavior. On BABILong, FocuSFT improves accuracy by up to +14pp across 4K--32K context lengths; on RULER, it raises CWE aggregation from 72.9\% to 81.1\% at 16K; and on GPQA with agentic tool use, it yields a 24\% relative gain in pass@1. Attention analysis shows that FocuSFT reduces attention sink mass by 529$\times$ and triples context engagement during training. Code: https://github.com/JarvisPei/FocuSFT

</details>


### [320] [Beyond Majority Voting: Agreement-Based Clustering to Model Annotator Perspectives in Subjective NLP Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09955)
*Tadesse Destaw Belay,Ibrahim Said Ahmad,Idris Abdulmumin,Abinew Ali Ayele,Alexander Gelbukh,Eusebio Ricárdez-Vázquez,Olga Kolesnikova,Shamsuddeen Hassan Muhammad,Seid Muhie Yimam*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于一致性的聚类技术来建模标注者之间的分歧，在40个数据集（18种语言、3项主观NLP任务）上进行了实验。结果表明，该方法相比多数投票和个体标注者建模能显著提升分类性能，其中多标签和多任务聚合方式优于集成学习和多数投票。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标注分歧是NLP数据集的常见现象，但多数投票仍是主流聚合策略；建模每个标注者虽能保留个体视角，但资源消耗大且未在多种NLP任务中得到充分探索。

Method: 提出基于一致性的聚类技术对标注者进行分组，实验涵盖40个数据集、18种语言、3项主观任务（情感分析、情绪分类、仇恨言论检测），比较了多数投票、集成学习、多标签和多任务四种聚合方式。

Result: 基于一致性的聚类相比多数投票和个体标注者建模显著提升分类性能；多标签和多任务聚合方式优于集成学习和多数投票。

Conclusion: 基于一致性的聚类能够充分利用标注者的多样化视角，在主观NLP任务中有效提升分类性能，且多标签和多任务是最优的聚合策略。

Abstract: Disagreement in annotation is a common phenomenon in the development of NLP datasets and serves as a valuable source of insight. While majority voting remains the dominant strategy for aggregating labels, recent work has explored modeling individual annotators to preserve their perspectives. However, modeling each annotator is resource-intensive and remains underexplored across various NLP tasks. We propose an agreement-based clustering technique to model the disagreement between the annotators. We conduct comprehensive experiments in 40 datasets in 18 typologically diverse languages, covering three subjective NLP tasks: sentiment analysis, emotion classification, and hate speech detection. We evaluate four aggregation approaches: majority vote, ensemble, multi-label, and multitask. The results demonstrate that agreement-based clustering can leverage the full spectrum of annotator perspectives and significantly enhance classification performance in subjective NLP tasks compared to majority voting and individual annotator modeling. Regarding the aggregation approach, the multi-label and multitask approaches are better for modeling clustered annotators than an ensemble and model majority vote.

</details>


### [321] [GLiNER2-PII: A Multilingual Model for Personally Identifiable Information Extraction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09973)
*Urchade Zaratiana,Ash Lewis,George Hurn-Maloney*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出GLiNER2-PII，一个基于GLiNER2的0.3B参数模型，用于检测42种PII实体类型。为解决标注数据稀缺问题，构建了包含4,910条多语言合成文本的语料库。在SPY基准上，该模型在字符级F1指标上优于OpenAI Privacy Filter等五个对比系统，模型已在Hugging Face开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代数据处理系统中可靠检测个人可识别信息（PII）日益重要，但该任务面临困难：PII跨度异构、依赖地域、对上下文敏感，且常嵌入嘈杂或半结构化文档。同时，训练此类系统受限于可共享标注数据的稀缺性以及大规模收集真实PII的隐私风险。

Method: 基于GLiNER2适配得到0.3B参数的GLiNER2-PII模型，支持42种PII实体类型的字符级识别。采用约束驱动的生成流水线，构建了包含4,910条多语言合成文本的语料库，覆盖多语言、多领域、多格式及多种实体分布，产生多样化、逼真的示例。

Result: 在SPY基准测试中，GLiNER2-PII在字符级F1指标上优于包括OpenAI Privacy Filter和三个基于GLiNER的检测器在内的五个对比系统，取得最高分。

Conclusion: 模型已在Hugging Face上公开发布，以支持开放PII检测系统的进一步研究和实际部署。

Abstract: Reliable detection of personally identifiable information (PII) is increasingly important across modern data-processing systems, yet the task remains difficult: PII spans are heterogeneous, locale-dependent, context-sensitive, and often embedded in noisy or semi-structured documents. We present GLiNER2-PII, a small 0.3B-parameter model adapted from GLiNER2 and designed to recognize a broad taxonomy of 42 PII entity types at character-span resolution. Training such systems, however, is constrained by the scarcity of shareable annotated data and the privacy risks associated with collecting real PII at scale. To address this challenge, we construct a multilingual synthetic corpus of 4,910 annotated texts using a constraint-driven generation pipeline that produces diverse, realistic examples across languages, domains, formats, and entity distributions. On the challenging SPY benchmark, GLiNER2-PII achieves the highest span-level F1 among five compared systems, including OpenAI Privacy Filter and three GLiNER-based detectors. We publicly release the model on Hugging Face to support further research and practical deployment of open PII detection systems.

</details>


### [322] [Annotations Mitigate Post-Training Mode Collapse](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.09995)
*Jacob Mitchell Springer,Madhu Advani,Lukas Aichberger,Arwen Bradley,Eran Malach,Omid Saremi,Sinead Williamson,Preetum Nakkiran,Etai Littwin,Aditi Raghunathan*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出了一种"注释锚定训练"方法，通过在预训练阶段引入语义注释分布，并在后训练阶段保持该分布，有效缓解了监督微调导致的语言模型语义多样性坍塌问题，将多样性损失降低了6倍，且效果随模型规模增大而提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 监督微调虽然提升指令遵循能力，但会导致模型偏向后训练数据中的低熵分布，牺牲预训练阶段的高熵分布多样性，且这种权衡随模型规模增大而恶化。

Method: 在预训练阶段为文档配对语义注释，建立丰富的注释分布；后训练阶段保持该分布不变；推理时采样多样注释作为锚点引导生成，从而将预训练的语义丰富性迁移到后训练模型中。

Result: 注释锚定训练相比SFT将多样性坍塌降低了6倍，且效果随模型规模增长而改善。

Conclusion: 注释锚定训练是一种原则性的方法，能够在不牺牲预训练固有多样性的前提下，使模型获得后训练的偏好跟随能力，有效弥合语义多样性差距。

Abstract: Post-training (via supervised fine-tuning) improves instruction-following, but often induces semantic mode collapse by biasing models toward low-entropy fine-tuning data at the expense of the high-entropy pretraining distribution. Crucially, we find this trade-off worsens with scale. To close this semantic diversity gap, we propose annotation-anchored training, a principled method that enables models to adopt the preference-following behaviors of post-training without sacrificing the inherent diversity of pretraining. Our approach is simple: we pretrain on documents paired with semantic annotations, inducing a rich annotation distribution that reflects the full breadth of pretraining data, and we preserve this distribution during post-training. This lets us sample diverse annotations at inference time and use them as anchors to guide generation, effectively transferring pretraining's semantic richness into post-trained models. We find that models trained with annotation-anchored training can attain $6 \times$ less diversity collapse than models trained with SFT, and improve with scale.

</details>


### [323] [Medical Incident Causal Factors and Preventive Measures Generation Using Tag-based Example Selection in Few-shot Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10025)
*Yuna Haseyama,Tomoki Ito,Hiroki Sakaji,Itsuki Noda*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本研究提出了一种基于标签的少样本示例选择方法，用于从医疗事件报告中生成背景/因果因素和预防措施。通过在日语医疗事件数据集上使用GPT-4o和LLaMA 3.3进行实验，发现基于标签的方法在生成精度和稳定性上优于随机采样和余弦相似度选择方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高风险医疗领域，LLM的可靠性至关重要。现有少样本提示方法在医疗事件分析中的示例选择策略缺乏系统性研究，尤其是如何利用结构化数据的标签信息提升生成质量。

Method: 提出一种基于标签的少样本示例选择方法，从JMID数据集的3,884份真实医疗事故和未遂事件报告中选取示例。与随机采样和基于余弦相似度的选择策略进行对比，使用GPT-4o和LLaMA 3.3两种大模型进行评估。

Result: 基于标签的方法在生成精度上最高，生成行为最稳定；而基于余弦相似度的选择方法常导致非预期输出并触发安全过滤器。

Conclusion: 选择基于人类可解释的数据集标签作为少样本示例，能够提升临床LLM应用中的生成精度和稳定性。

Abstract: In high-stakes domains such as healthcare, the reliability of Large Language Models (LLMs) is critical, particularly when generating clinical insights from incident reports. This study proposes a tag-based few-shot example selection method for prompting LLMs to generate background/causal factors and preventive measures from details of the medical incidents. For our experiments, we use the Japanese Medical Incident Dataset (JMID), a structured dataset of 3,884 real-world medical accident and near-miss reports. These reports are variably annotated with a wide range of tags--some include descriptive information (e.g., "medications," "blood transfusion therapy"). We compare three few-shot example selection strategies--random sampling, cosine similarity-based selection, and our proposed tag-based method--using GPT-4o and LLaMA 3.3. Results show that the tag-based approach achieves the highest precision and most stable generation behavior, while similarity-based selection often leads to unintended outputs and safety filter activation. These findings suggest that selecting examples based on human-interpretable dataset tags can improve generation precision and stability in clinical LLM applications.

</details>


### [324] [Speech-based Psychological Crisis Assessment using LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10027)
*Terumi Chiba,Yang Luo,Ziyun Cui,Yongsheng Tong,Chao Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文提出基于大语言模型的自动化危机等级分类框架，通过副语言注入方法将非言语情感线索融入语音转录文本，并采用推理增强训练策略，在心理支持热线三分类任务上达到0.802的宏F1与0.805的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有心理支持热线的评估依赖人工操作员，其判断受限于专业经验差异和人力不足，亟需自动化危机等级分类方法来提升热线服务质量和下游任务性能。

Method: 提出LLM框架进行自动化危机等级分类；引入副语言注入方法，将识别出的非言语情感线索插入语音转录文本，使LLM能利用声学细微差别；采用推理增强训练策略，生成诊断推理链作为辅助任务，作为正则化器提升分类性能。

Result: 在五折交叉验证下，三分类任务上达到宏F1为0.802，准确率为0.805。

Conclusion: 结合数据增强，所提出的副语言注入和推理增强训练策略能有效提升LLM在心理支持热线危机分类任务上的表现。

Abstract: Psychological support hotlines provide critical support for individuals experiencing mental health emergencies, yet current assessments largely rely on human operators whose judgments may vary with professional experience and are constrained by limited staffing resources. This paper proposes a large language model (LLM)-based framework for automated crisis level classification, a key indicator that supports many downstream tasks and improves the overall quality of hotline services. To better capture emotional signals in spoken conversations, we introduce a paralinguistic injection method that inserts identified non-verbal emotional cues into speech transcripts, enabling LLM-based reasoning to incorporate critical acoustic nuances. In addition, we propose a reasoning-enhanced training strategy that trains the model to generate diagnostic reasoning chains as an auxiliary task, which serves as a regulariser to improve classification performance. Combined with data augmentation, our final system achieves a macro F1-score of 0.802 and an accuracy of 0.805 on the three-class classification task under 5-fold cross-validation.

</details>


### [325] [PlantMarkerBench: A Multi-Species Benchmark for Evidence-Grounded Plant Marker Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2605.10032)
*Sajib Acharjee Dip,Song Li,Liqing Zhang*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: PlantMarkerBench是一个跨物种的植物标记基因证据解释基准，包含4个物种、5550条句子级证据实例，定义了标记证据有效性和证据类型分类两项任务，评估发现前沿模型在直接表达证据上表现较好，但在功能性、间接和弱支持证据上性能显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有植物细胞类型特异性标记基因资源主要依赖 curated 数据库或高通量研究，缺乏对科学文献中支撑证据的显式建模，亟需一个能够评估从文献中提取和解释标记基因证据的系统化基准。

Method: 采用模块化策展流程，整合大规模文献检索、混合搜索、物种感知生物学基础构建、结构化证据提取和人工审核，构建覆盖拟南芥、玉米、水稻和番茄4个物种、包含5550条句子级证据实例的基准，定义标记证据有效性判断和证据类型（表达、定位、功能、间接、阴性）分类两项任务。

Result: 前沿模型在直接表达证据上表现较好，但在功能性、间接和弱支持证据上性能显著下降；证据类型混淆是主要失败模式；开源模型在模糊生物学背景下表现出更高的假阳性率。

Conclusion: PlantMarkerBench为文献基础的生物学证据归因提供了具有挑战性且可重复的评估框架，支持未来可信赖科学信息提取和AI辅助植物生物学研究。

Abstract: Cell-type-specific marker genes are fundamental to plant biology, yet existing resources primarily rely on curated databases or high-throughput studies without explicitly modeling the supporting evidence found in scientific literature. We introduce PlantMarkerBench, a multi-species benchmark for evaluating literature-grounded plant marker evidence interpretation from full-text biological papers. PlantMarkerBench is constructed using a modular curation pipeline integrating large-scale literature retrieval, hybrid search, species-aware biological grounding, structured evidence extraction, and targeted human review. The benchmark spans four plant species -- Arabidopsis, maize, rice, and tomato -- and contains 5,550 sentence-level evidence instances annotated for marker-evidence validity, evidence type, and support strength. We define two benchmark tasks: determining whether a candidate sentence provides valid marker evidence for a gene-cell-type pair, and classifying the evidence into expression, localization, function, indirect, or negative categories. We benchmark diverse open-weight and closed-source language models across species and prompting strategies. Although frontier models achieve relatively strong performance on direct expression evidence, performance drops substantially on functional, indirect, and weak-support evidence, with evidence-type confusion emerging as a dominant failure mode.
